mēliyāriyac coṟkaḷ
pakku(pi) - ON. baggi, ME. bagge, E. bag.
pakkam - L. pagina, OF., F., E. page.
itaṟku L. pangere, to fasten eṉṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭuvatu poruntātu. E. Paginate = pakkamiṭu.
pāṅku (iṭam), pākkam (ūr), pākki (ūr) - L. pagus, a place, village, district, province.
gh§f‹ - OF., ME., F. page, a boy or man, usu. in livery, employed as personal attendant of person of rank and to go on errands. pidos (pital) eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟu tirinta paggio eṉṉum ittāliyac colli itaṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
paksh, to take a part or side, (W).
pakkam - paksha, a wing, RV; the shoulder, the flank or side or half of anything, RV; the half of a lunar month (sukla or krishna paksha); a side, party, faction; place; one of two cases or one side of an argument, a thesis, the proposition to be proved in a syllogism; an army, favour, proximity, neighbourhood etc.
panti - pankti, a row or set or collection of five.
pāku - bahu, the arm.
paku - bhaj, to divide, distribute, allot or apportion to or share with.
 o.nō : puku (uṇ) - bhuj.
bhakta, distributed, assigned, allotted, RV.
bhakti, distribution, partition, separation, RV; a division, portion, share; the being a part of; that which belongs to anything else, attachment, fondness for, devotion, homage, piety, faith.
bhaktri, devotedly attached, an adorer, worshipper.
bhaga, dispenser, gracious lord, patron.
bhagavat, possessing fortune, prosperous, happy, RV; adorable, venerable, AV.
bhagaval, or bhagavan, in comp. e.g. bhagavad - gita.
bhagavati, name of Lakshmi or Durga.
bhagin, prosperous, happy.
bhagini, a sister (‘the happy or fortunate one’ as having a brother).
bhajaka, distributor, apportioner.
bhaga, a part, portion, share, allotment, inheritance, lot, good fortune,
	luck, destiny.
	bhagavata, relating to or coming from Bhagvat, i.e. Vishnu or Krishna, a
follower or worshipper of Bhagavat.
1. bhagya, to be shared or divided, divisible.
2. bhagya, entitled to a share, lucky, fortunate; good fortune, fate, destiny.
bhaj, sharing or participating in.
bhajaka, a divisor.
bhajana, sharing or participating in.
bhajita, shared, divided, distributed, portioned.
bhajya, to be shared or distributed., to be divided.
mēṟkāṭṭiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷellām, paku eṉṉum orē mūlac colliṉiṉṟu toṭarcciyākat tōṉṟiyavi yeṉpatu. kūrntu nōkkuvārkkut teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil, avi pakṣ, pañccaṉ (pancan), paj (bhaj), pah (bah) eṉṉum toṭarpaṟṟa nālvēṟu mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyaviyākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. am mūlaṅkaḷuḷ  bah eṉpataṟku banh eṉpatē aṭimūlam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟku vaḷartal allatu mikutal eṉpatē aṭipporuḷ. ki eṉṉum ciṉippeyarukkup pakkattiliruppatu eṉpatē yaṉṟi vaḷarvatu eṉṉum poruḷ poruntātu. panti eṉṉuñ colliṟku (aintu) pañcaṉ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum mūlamākātu.
pāl eṉṉuñ colliṟkup pakuti, ūḻ eṉṉum iru poruḷ iruppatupōṉṟē, pākku eṉṉum colliṟkum iruttal vēṇṭum. palaraṟiyār pākkiyattāl eṉṉum kuṟaḷaṭikku (1141), an nilipēṟṟit teyvattāl yāṉē aṟivatallatu kūṟukiṉṟa palarum aṟiyār eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar urittiruppati nōkkuka. avar teyvam eṉṟatu pāl vari teyvatti.
kiḷivayi ṉīkkiyik keṇṭiyaṅ kaṇṇiyik koṇṭutanta
viḷiviyal lālviya  vēṉaya vēṉteyva mikkaṉavē.  (6).
poṉṉiv vuyarpoḻilvāyc cūḻuṭi yāyatti nīkkum
vitituṇiyā.  (7).
coṟpā lamutivaḷ ..... naṟpāl viṉitteyvan tantiṉṟu  (8).
eṉṟu tirukkōvi kūṟutaliyuṅ kāṇka.
patti (pakti) eṉṉuñ colliṟku oruvaṉ pāṟpaṭṭa taṉmi eṉpatē mūlapporuḷ.
pakuttaṟiviṉ payaṉ
ulakil vēṟu ennilappakutiyilum illātatum, intiyāvilum vaṭanāṭṭiṉun teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kaṭumiyākavum kaṇṭippākavuṅ kik koḷḷappaṭuvatum, teṉmoḻiyiṉattiṉ oṟṟumiyik kulittu ataṉ valimiyik keṭuppataṟkē āriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭatum, ceyyun toḻiloṭu ciṟitun toṭarpaṟṟatum, pakuttaṟiviṟku muṟṟum muraṇāṉatum, āṉa piṟavikkulap piriviṉiyi uṇarttum kulappaṭṭaṅkaḷi iṉṟun tamiḻar tāṅki varuvatu ikkālattiṟ kēṟkātatākum.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil im mūṭavaḻakkam iruntatē yilli. ellā vakuppārum, piṟanāṭṭār pōl tam peyari iṇippu vāliṉṟi veṟumiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. aṟivum nākarikamum tuppuravum oḻukkamum uṇmiyiṉmi kaḷē, uyarvu tāḻvik kuṟittu vantaṉa. āriyap piṟavikkula vuyarvutāḻvit tiruvaḷḷuvar teḷivākak kaṇṭittār.
pakuttaṟiviyakkap pakalavaṉ eṉṉum periyār tam kaṟpippiṟ kēṟpa, piṟavikkula voḻuṅki mīṟiyē oru peṇṇi maṇantār. avarip piṉpaṟṟuvatākap pitaṟṟum palar, mataviyal oṉṟu tavira maṟṟavaṟṟil avar peyar keṭumāṟē oḻukivarukiṉṟaṉar. piṟavikkula vuṇarcciyic collaḷavil nīkkiṉum uḷḷaḷavil nīkkavilli.
pakuttaṟiviyakkam tamiḻarellārkkum potuvāyiṉum, periyārip piṉpaṟṟiyar allatu pakuttaṟivāḷar eṉṟu tammic collik koḷvōr, tāmē muṉpu pakuttaṟivo ḻukkam pūṇṭu piṟarkku vaḻikāṭṭal vēṇṭum.
pakuttaṟivu viḷakkam
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ, pakuttaṟi vaṭippaṭiyilēyē, poruḷkaḷiyellām uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇiyeṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, ulakam pōṟṟum oppuyarvaṟṟa ilakkaṇam vakuttirukkiṉṟāṉ.
kaṭavuḷ illiyeṉpataṟkup pōṉṟē, uṇṭu eṉpataṟkum pakuttaṟivē tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. ulakil ettuṇi nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu nāṭāyiṉum, iravil nakaraviḷakku arimaṇi nēram eriyātu pōyiṉum, aintu nimiyam aracaṉ atikāram illātu pōyiṉum, kāṭṭuvilaṅkiṉuṅ kēṭāka nāṭṭumakkaḷ naṭantu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉamirukka, uyiraṟṟa paṉṉiru kōḷkaḷum paṟpala nāṇmīṉkaḷum iṭiviṭātu peruveḷiyil oḻuṅkāy iyaṅki varuvatu iyakkuvāṉ oruvaṉiṉṟi iyalumō? kaṭavuḷuṇmiyik kāṭṭa itupōl vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ ettaṇiyō iruppiṉum, iḵtoṉṟē pōtumāṉatām.
āyiṉum, naṭuniliyāka nōkki, irucārārum oruvari yoruvar ikaḻātuṅ kuṟikūṟātum iruppatē pakuttaṟiviṟ kaḻakām.
paccik kutiri viḷiyāṭṭu
ciṟuvar, kutiripōṟ kuṉintu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu paccik kutiri. oru ciṟuvaṉ kuṉintu niṟpatu oru ciṟu kutiripōl tōṉṟuvatāl, itu ippeyar peṟṟirukka lām. itil iruvakiyuṇṭu.
I. oru vaki
palar variciyāka iṭiyiṭṭuk kuṉintukoṇṭu niṟpar. avaruḷ oru kōṭiyil iruppavaṉ, piṟariyellān tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟpōyk kuṉintu niṟpāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē piṟarum varicippaṭi ovvoruvaṉāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟ pōy niṟpar. ivvāṟu āṭṭut toṭarat toṭara iṭam peyarntu koṇṭēyiruppar. inta āṭṭu, pala cuvarkaḷit toṭarntu tāṇṭutaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyākum.
II. maṟṟoru vaki
ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppil tavaṟiya ciṟuvaṉ oru kāli nīṭṭi uṭkārntiruppāṉ. avaṉatu nīṭṭiya kālip piṟa ciṟuvar ovvoruvarāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭic celvar. aṭutta muṟi avaṉ kālmēṟ kālvittu nīṭṭiyiruppāṉ. atiyum avvāṟē tāṇṭuvar, ataṟkaṭutta muṟi oru ki ataṉmēl vittiruppaṉ. piṉpu, muṟiyē, uṭkārnta niliyil tāḻak kuṉintum, anniliyil caṟṟu nimirntum, eḻuntu niṉṟu tāḻak kuṉintum, anniliyil caṟṟu nimirntum, ivvāṟu paṭip paṭiyāka uyarntukoṇṭiruppāṉ. piṟarum uṭaṉuṭaṉ tāṇṭik koṇṭē varuvar. enniliyilēṉum oruvaṉ tāṇṭamuṭi yātu niṉṟāl, piṉpu avaṉ kīḻē uṭkārntu muṉcoṉṉa vāṟu paṭippaṭiyāy uyarntukoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻēyiruntavaṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu viḷiyāṭuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam āṭṭut toṭarum. ciṟuvarkku iḵtoru paṭimuṟi yuyarat tāṇṭaṟ payiṟciyām.
pañcāyp paṟattal 
vaṟumiyāl varuntum māntar noyya uṭampuṭaṉ vaḷḷalāri nāṭi aṅkum iṅkum alintu tirital āliliyum ilavam pañcum āṭikkāṟṟil aṅkum iṅkum paṟantu tirivati okkum. itaṉāl ālāyp paṟattal, pañcāyp paṟattal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
eḷimiyik kuṟikkum pañci eṉṉum collum, vaṟkaṭattik kuṟikkum pañcam eṉṉum collum pañcāyp paṟattal eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉiṉṟu eḻuntaviyākum. pāvēntar kāṟṟil ilavam pañcākap paṟakkiyilē eṉṟa paṭikkācup pulavar pāṭalaṭiyi nōkkuka.  col : 8, 9.
pañci
pañci - va. pañci
pimmi = pacumi, iḷami, meṉmi, noymmi.
pi - (pintu) - (piñcu) - pañcu - pañci
o. nō : ai - aintu - (aiñcu) - añcu.
ni - nintu - niñcu - nañcu = nintu.  va.va : 195.
pañcu veṭṭuṅ kampaṭō viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō eṉṟu colli āṭappeṟum āṭṭu. ac colliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa iruvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : maṇaṟ kuviyalil maṭṭum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉi maṇaliṟ paṭukka vittut tuṇiyāṟ pōrttu avaṉatu uṭaliṉ kīḻp pakutiyi maṇalāl mūṭi pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē, paruttiveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkic colli mella mella avaṉ mutukil taṭṭik koṇṭēyiruppaṉ. ciṟitu nēram poṟuttup paṭuttavaṉ eḻuntuviṭuvāṉ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : pōril tōṟṟōṭiṉavaruḷ oru cārār. tammit toṭarntu varum veṟṟi maṟavariṉiṉṟu tappikkoḷ ḷum poruṭṭu naṭitta naṭippukkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ pōliyāka, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyatōveṉa aiyuṟa iṭamuṇṭu.  (ta.nā.vi.)

ma. paṭṭam.
mēṉmēlum taṭṭuvatāl oru poruḷ taṭṭiyākum. paṭṭuvatāṟ paṭṭiyākum eṉpatum atuvē.

paṭṭa viḷiyāṭṭu
paṭṭam oruvaṉ āṭiṉāl poḻutupōkkām (Pastime); iruvar āṭiṉāl viḷiyāṭṭām.
oruvaṉ paṭṭattiṟkumēl oruvaṉ paṭṭattip paṟakkac ceyvatum, oṉṟiyoṉṟu tākki vīḻttac ceyvatumē iv viḷiyāṭṭām.
paṭṭi
paṭṭi - va. paṭṭi ma. paṭṭi, te. paṭṭi,
paṭṭiki - va. paṭṭikā
paṭṭutal = taṭṭutal. paṭṭu - paṭṭi = paṭṭi, mūṅkiṟ piḷāccu, ataṉālaminta toḻu, ēṭṭileḻutiya peyar varici, nāyk kaḻuttuvār.

pinnāp paṭavaravu  (tiruvāca. 34 : 1)  (va.va : 196 - 197.)
paṭarkki i vikuti
maturik kallūri yitaḻil ikara vikutiyip paṟṟi variyappaṭṭuḷḷa pakuti varumāṟu :
tamiḻil peṇpāl vikuti
i eṉpatu tamiḻil peṇpāl vikuti eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. uṇmiyil peṇpāl vikuti i alla. atu peṇpāl vikutiyāyiṉ villi, nāṟkāli mutaliyavaṟṟil ap poruḷil varātiruppatēṉ? uṇmi eṉṉaveṉṟāl, i eṉpatu tiri eṉpataṉ citivu. perumpālum, tti, cci, ṭṭi eṉpaviyē peṇpāl vikutikaḷāka varukiṉṟaṉa. ivi tiri eṉpataṉ māṟupaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivu. mutalil tiri eṉpatē peṇpāl vikutiyāka iruntu piṟaku atu citintu tti, cci, ṭṭi eṉṟu māṟiyirukka vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu :
kuṟa + tiri = kuṟatti	puli + tiri = pulicci
veḷḷāṉ + tiri = veḷḷāṭṭi.	vali + tiri = valicci.
vaṇṇāṉ + tiri = vaṇṇātti.	āy + tiri = āycci.
pārppāṉ + tiri = pārppātti	pirāmaṇa + tiri = pirāmaṇatti
rājā + tiri = rājātti	paṟi + tiri = paṟicci
peṇṭu + ām + tiri = peṇṭāṭṭi.
kaṇ + ām + tiri = kaṇṇāṭṭi.
pommaṉāṭṭi. kammaṉāṭṭi eṉpaviyum ivvāṟē vantirukkavēṇṭum.
iṉi itaṉ maṟuppu viḷakkam varumāṟu:
tamiḻil, āṇpāl peṇpāl vikutiyēṟpaṭāta mutukālattil. atāvatu uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṉum tiṇi vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟāta paḻaṅkālattil, orumippāl paṉmippāl eṉṉum irupālkaḷē iru tiṇikkum potuvāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa. mutaṟkālattilēyē pakuttaṟivuḷḷatum illatum eṉap poruḷkaḷit tamiḻar iru pakuppākap pakuttirukka muṭiyātu. nākarikamum aṟivu vaḷarcciyumākiya paṇpāṭaṭinta piṉṉarē, avar avvāṟu ceytiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟum, irutiṇikkum potuvākap pala coṟkaḷum vikutikaḷum vaḻaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. avi muntukāla moḻiniliyik kāṭṭum eñcu kuṟikaḷākum.
e-ṭu :
coṟkaḷ
āṇ, peṇ, tāy, tanti, piḷḷi, kuṭṭi, tāṉ, tām.
vikutikaḷ
kattarippāṉ = kattarikkiṉṟavaṉ (uyartiṇi āṇpāl)
kattarippāṉ = kattarikkōl (aḵṟiṇi oṉṟaṉpāl)
kaṇṇi = kaṇṇiyuṭiyavaḷ (uyartiṇip peṇpāl)
kaṇṇi = kaṇṇiyuṭiyatu (aḵṟiṇi oṉṟaṉpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟavaṉ (uyartiṇi āṇpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟavaḷ (uyartiṇip peṇpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟatu (aḵṟiṇi oṉṟaṉpāl)
irutiṇic coṟkum ōraṉṉa urimiyiṉ
tiripu vēṟupaṭūum ellāp peyarum
niṉiyuṅ kālit tattam marapiṉ
viṉiyō ṭallatu pālteri pilavē  (657)
iyaṟpeyar ciṉippeyar ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
muṟippeyark kiḷavi tāmē tāṉē
ellām nīyir nīeṉak kiḷantu
colliya alla piṟavum āaṅku
aṉṉavi tōṉṟiṉ avaṟṟoṭuṅ koḷalē  (659)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
pālkāṭṭum vikutikaḷ, iṉṟu eṅṅaṉam perumpālum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkamuṅ kuṟiyumāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō, aṅṅaṉamē muṟkālattum iruntaṉa.
e-ṭu :	atu - valatu, taṉatu, uḷatu, nallatu.
	itu - veḷitu, valitu, karitu, putitu.
	utu - viḻutu, paḻutu, erutu (ērutu), marutu.
	avaṉ - āṉ - aṉ: nallavaṉ - nallāṉ - nallaṉ.
	avar - ār - ar: vallavar - vallār - vallar.
	ivar - īr - ir: (makaḷivar) - (makaḷīr) - makaḷir.
ikara vukaravaṭi vikutikaḷ ikkālattup perumpālum vaḻakkiṟantaṉa.
tamiḻiṉ acinilik kālattil (Monosyllabic or Isolating Stage), cuṭṭuccoṟkaḷ vikuti peṟāmal cuṭṭaṭiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantaṉa. iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum palaviṉpāl akaravikuti muntuniliyi yuṇarttum aṭiyāḷamām.
e-ṭu	:	nalla = nallavi (peyar)
		vanta = vantaṉa (viṉimuṟṟu)
oppa nāṭi attaka voṟutti  (puṟam. 10)
eṉṉum aṭiyil, attaka eṉpatu ataṟkuttaka eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalāl, acinilikkālat tuvakkattil, cuṭṭacic coṟkaḷ eṇṇuṇarttātu cuṭṭup poruḷoṉṟē uṇartti vantaṉa eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu.
cēymi, aṇmi, muṉmi ākiya mūvakiyiṭaṅkaḷiyum muṟiyē cuṭṭum a, i, u (allatu ā, ī, ū) eṉpaṉa, aci nilik kālattil taṉiccollēyākavum, ataṟkaṭutta puṇar nilikkālattil (Compounding Stage) taṉiccollum kūṭṭuc collumākavum, ataṟkaṭutta vikutinilikkālattil (Inflexional Stage) taṉic collum kūṭṭuc collum vikutiyākavum vaḻaṅki vantu, iṉṟu nālāṅkālamākiya koḷuvunilik kālattiṉ (Agglutinative Stage) piṟpakutiyil, taṉiccollāy vaḻaṅkuva toḻintu ēṉi yiruvaki yākavē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
eccollum vēṟoru colliṉ vikutiyāka amintavuṭaṉ, taṉ vitappup poruḷi iḻantuviṭum. ākavē, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷum vikutiyāyamintapiṉ tam cuṭṭupporuḷiyiḻantu pālmaṭṭum uṇartti niṟkum.
orumikkum paṉmikkum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭa kālattil, ikaraccuṭṭu orumi vikutikaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷḷap peṟṟatu. aṉṟu āṇ peṇ pālvēṟupāṭu kuṟikkappeṟāta kālamātalāl, āṇpāl peṇpāl oṉṟaṉpāl ākiya mup pālukkum potuvākavē ikaraccuṭṭu vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭu, aṉṟiruntu ammuṟiyē toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu. (peṇmakaḷ eṉṉum māṟōkkattu vaḻakkum peṭṭip piyaṉ eṉṉum vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkum, āṇ pāl peṇpāl vikutiyēṟ paṭāta muntukāla moḻiniliyi ūkittuṇara vutavum).
e-ṭu :	viṟakuveṭṭi (āṇpāl)
	maṇveṭṭi (oṉṟaṉpāl)
	kayaṟkaṇṇi (peṇpāl)
	kāṭikkaṇṇi (oṉṟaṉpāl)
ākavē, ikara vikuti orumi vikutiyēyaṉṟip peṇpāl vikutiyaṉṟu. atu oru kuṟitta collil mēṟkūṟiya muppāluḷ oṉṟi uṇarttumē yaṉṟit taṉitta niliyil uṇarttātu. kaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟku nērāṉa peṇpāṟ col. kaṇṇaḷ eṉpatē yaṉṟik kaṇṇi eṉpataṉṟu. kaṇṇāṉ eṉpatu kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu kuṟukuvatu pōlak kaṇṇāḷ eṉpatum kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu kuṟukum. vantāṉ eṉpataṟku vantāḷ eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam peṇpālō, aṅṅaṉamē kaṇṇaṉ eṉpa taṟkum kaṇṇaḷ eṉpatē nērāṉa peṇpālām. kaṇṇāḷ allatu kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu colvataṟkup patilākak kaṇṇi eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl. ikara vikutiyip peṇpāṟ ciṟappu vikutiyeṉṟu palar mayaṅka nērntuviṭṭatu. av vikuti peṇpāl vikutiyāyirukkak kūṭiyatē yaṉṟip peṇpālukkē yuriya vikutiyaṉṟu.
iv vikuti aḷ vikutiyiviṭap palukkuvataṟku (uccarippa taṟku) eḷitāyum iṉṉōciyuḷḷatāyu miruttalē, atu peru vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamākum. kaṇṇaḷ kaṇṇi eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷiyum palukkik kāṇka. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē, tāyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuṭ cila aḷ vikutiyi viṭṭu i v vikutiyi ēṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu :	āṇpāl (tanti)	peṇpāl (tāy)
	attaṉ	atti
	accaṉ	acci
atti eṉpatoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa atti eṉṉum peyar. attaṉiṉ (tantiyiṉ) uṭaṉpiṟantāḷik kuṟikkum. attaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripē accaṉ eṉpatu. accaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar cēranāṭāṉa maliyāḷa nāṭṭilum, acci eṉṉum peṇpāṟ peyar pāṇṭināṭṭilum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
acci = akki (pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakku)
akki tāy pōṉṟavaḷātalāl acci eṉappaṭṭāḷ.
o.nō : tam + avvi (tāy) = tavvi = tamakki.
accu (accaṉ) acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, toṭarmoḻi yuṟuppāka, muṟiyē tāy tantip poruḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
appaccu = appaṉiṉ tanti = pāṭṭaṉ (pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakku)
ammācci = ammiyiṉ tāy = pāṭṭi (cōḻanāṭṭu vaḻakku)
appaccu eṉpatu appacci eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
arumiyum perumiyumpaṟṟi, paruvamaṭintuḷḷa āṭavari yum peṇṭiri yum tāytantiyarik kuṟikkum muṟip peyarāl aḻikkum vaḻakkam, nam nāṭṭil toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varuvatāl. ap peyarkaḷ āṇpāṟ peṇpāṟ peyarīṟṟut taṉmiyaṭintuḷḷaṉa. ōr āṭavari aiyā eṉṟum, oru peṇṭi ammā eṉṟum aḻittal kāṇka. ammi allatu ammā eṉṉum peyar, iyaṟpeyarum toḻilpaṟṟiya potuppeyarumāṉa peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷiṉ īṟākap peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu :	poṉṉammi, poṉṉammā
	tāyammi, tāyammā		iyaṟpeyar
	vāttiyārammā, ṭākṭar ammā -	toḻil paṟṟiya peṇpāṟ potup peyar.
iṅṅaṉamē, atti acci eṉṉum tāymuṟip peyarkaḷum, kulamum toḻilum kuṇamum niṟamum nilimiyum piṟavum paṟṟiya peṇpāṟ potuppeyarkaḷiṉ īṟāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu :	kuṟa + atti = kuṟatti
	taṭṭā(ṉ) + atti = taṭṭātti		- 	kulam.
	paṟi + acci = paṟicci
	vali + acci = valicci
	upātti + acci = upātticci
	maruttuva + acci = maruttuvacci	-	toḻil
	maṭi + acci = maṭicci		-	taṉmi
	veḷḷi + acci = veḷḷicci	- 	niṟam
	muṇṭi + acci = muṇṭicci
	āṇṭi + acci = āṇṭicci		-	nilimi
	pēy + acci = pēycci
	neṭṭi + acci = neṭṭicci
	kuḷḷa + acci = kuḷḷacci		-	aḷavu
	kuppa + acci = kuppacci		-	iṭam
	caṭi + acci = caṭicci		-	uṟuppu
	ciṟṟiṭi + acci = ciṟṟiṭicci
veḷḷicci, pēycci, kuppacci, caṭicci mutaliya peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil iyaṟpeyarāka iṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
atti acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ īṟāka niṉṟu puṇarumpōtu. avaṟṟiṉ akaramutal keṭṭuviṭukiṉṟatu. keṭātu niṟpatum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kaḷḷavatti
veḷḷicci kuppacci eṉṉum peyarkaḷi veḷḷiyammā kuppammā eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppunōkkuka.
atti eṉṉum colliṉ kuṟiyāṉa ti īṟu, nilimoḻi yīṟṟu ḷakara meyyōṭu puṇarumpōtu, av viraṇṭum ṭṭi eṉat tiriyum. atu eṉṉum colliṉ kuṟiyāṉa tu vvīṟum, iṅṅaṉamē ṭuvvīṟākat tirital kāṇka. (tāḷ + tu = tāṭṭu.)
āḷaṉ (āḷ + aṉ) eṉṉum āṇpālīṟṟiṟku etirāka āṭṭi (āḷ + ti) eṉpatu peṇpālīṟāy varum.
e-ṭu :	veḷḷāḷaṉ - veḷḷāṭṭi
			kaṇṇāḷaṉ - kaṇṇāṭṭi
			maṇavāḷaṉ - maṇavāṭṭi
			tiruvāḷaṉ - tiruvāṭṭi
			kuṇavāḷaṉ - kuṇavāṭṭi
			kuṇāḷaṉ - kuṇāṭṭi
veḷḷāḷaṉ eṉpati veḷḷāṉ eṉpatu tavaṟu.
makaṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu māṉ eṉṟu maruvuvatu pōl, makaḷ eṉṉum peṇpālīṟu māḷ eṉṟu maruvum.
e-ṭu :	perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, kuṟumakaṉ - kuṟumāṉ, marumakaṉ - marumāṉ, tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ, veḷimakaṉ - veḷimāṉ.
	perumakaḷ - perumāḷ, vēṇmakaḷ - vēṇmāḷ, tirumakaḷ - tirumāḷ.
māḷ eṉṉum peṇpālīṟṟoṭu mēṟkāṭṭiya ti eṉṉum īṟum cērumpōtu, av viraṇṭumē māṭṭi eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu :	āṇpāl	peṇpāl
	perumāṉ	perumāṭṭi
	tirumāṉ	tirumāṭṭi
	cīmāṉ	cīmāṭṭi
perumāṉ eṉpatu pirāṉ eṉṟu maruvumpōtu, perumāṭṭi eṉpatu pirāṭṭi eṉa maruvum. perumāḷ tirumāḷ eṉṉum oṟṟiyīṟṟup peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻakkiṟantaṉa. vikutimēl vikuti vantu iraṭṭippaṉmip peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, īṟṟiṉmēl īṟu vantu iraṭṭip peṇmip peyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu : veḷḷāṭṭi + acci = veḷḷāṭṭicci.
peṇṭāṭṭi (peṇṭu + āṭṭi) eṉpatiluḷḷa āṭṭi īṟu maṉivip poruḷ kuṟikka vantatākum. peṇ, peṇṭu eṉpaṉa iṭamnōkki maṉivip poruḷuṇarttum peṇpāṟ potuppeyarām.
ki + peṇ = kimpeṇ.
ki + peṇṭu = kimpeṇṭu
ki + peṇṭāṭṭi = kimpeṇṭāṭṭi
peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi eṉpatu, āṇpiḷḷip piḷḷi kārāṉ pacu ariñāṇ kayiṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mikipaṭu coṟṟoṭar.
oru peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi āṇuṭi yuṭuttu  (īṭu. 6 : 2.).
kimpeṇṭāṭṭi peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē, kammaṉāṭṭi pommaṉāṭṭi eṉak kocci vaḻakkil maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
atti acci eṉṉum īṟukaḷ peṇpāl maṭṭum uṇartti uyarvu kuṟikkāmiyāl, taṭṭāttiyammā, upātticciyammā mutaliya peyarkaḷ uyarvu kuṟikka vanta iraṭṭip peṇpāṟ peyarākum.
appaṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṉum tanti muṟip peyarkaḷ, aruḷappaṉ, kaṇṇappaṉ, cellappaṉ, nākappaṉ, aruḷiyaṉ, kaṇṇiyaṉ, celliyaṉ, nākiyaṉ eṉa evvāṟu āṇpāṟ peyarīṟukaḷāy āḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉavō; avvāṟē, ammi, atti, acci eṉṉum tāymuṟip peyarkaḷum peṇpāṟ peyarīṟukaḷāy āḷappeṟum eṉka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻil peṇpāl kuṟittu varum tti, cci, ṭi (ṭṭi) eṉṉum īṟukaḷellām. atti acci eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ kuṟiyum tiripumēyaṉṟi, tiri eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ citivalla eṉṟu teṟṟeṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.  ( centamiḻc celvi ākattu 1951.)
paṭalam 
paṭalam - va. paṭala (t´)
paṭartal = paravutal. paṭam - paṭal = ōli taṭṭi māṟu mutaliyavaṟṟālākiya katavupōṉṟa aṭippu, uḻuta nilattiṟ parampaṭikkum paranta māṟu, kaṇṇiṟ paṭarum puri.  (va.va : 197.).
paṭām 
paṭām : paṭṭutal taṭṭutal, paṭṭu - paṭṭi = taṭṭiyākat taṭṭappaṭṭatu. paṭṭu - paṭṭam = paṭṭiyāṉa takaṭu, paṭṭiyāṉa tuṇi, paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi. tuṇiyileḻutiya ōviyam, cittirac cīli, yāṉi mukapaṭām. paṭam - paṭām. 
o.nō : kaṭam - kaṭām. paṭam - paṭa (va.). paṭam (paṭām) - paṭṭa (va.).  ti.ma : 747.
paṭi 
paṭi - va. paṭ (t´h)
paṭutal = olittal. paṭu - paṭi = oliyeḻa vāyittal (vācittal).
o. nō : ōti - ōci. ōtutal = paṭittal. paṭi - pāṭam.
paṭi - va. prati
paḷ - paṭu. paṭutal = viḻutal. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = oṉṟiṉmēl viḻutal, viḻuntu patital, patintu uruvam amital.
nilattiṟ patinta poruḷiṉ vaṭivam nilattilum, tāḷiṟ patinta acciṉ vaṭivam tāḷilum, amital kāṇka.
paṭi = 1. uruvam mayilaṉārkkup paṭivittu (cīvaka. 1156).
2. uṭampu.niṉiyāravaṉmippaṭiyē(tiv.iyaṟ.  tiruvirut. 93).
paṭinta uruvam paṭinta poruḷi ottiruttalāl, paṭi eṉṉum col oppumik karutti uṇarttiṟṟu.
appaṭi = atupōl. paṭiyoruva rillāp paṭiyār pōlum (tēvā. 44 : 7).
paṭi = otta vaṭivam (True copy). kiḻitta vōli paṭiyōli (periyapu. taṭuttāṭ. 56).
mūlamum paṭiyum eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam. paṭi - paṭimi - paṭimam.
paṭi - vaṭi (kocci). paṭivu - vaṭivu. paṭivam - vaṭivam.  (va.va : 197).
paṭivam 
paṭimi - va. pratimā
paṭi - paṭimi = 1. vaṭivam kaṭṭaḷip paṭimiyiṟ paṭiyātu (cīvaka. 2752). 2. pāvi. 3. vaḻipaṭu teyva vuruvam. 4. nōṉpu, tavam. palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimi yōṉē  (tol. ciṟappup.)
pratimā - patumi - pommi.  (va.va : 197-198.)
paṭivam : paṭutal = viḻutal. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = oṉṟiṉmēl iṉṉoṉṟu viḻutal, viḻuntu patital, patintu uruva vaṭivam amital.
paṭi = uruvam, uṭampu, oppu. appaṭi = atupōl.
paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam. paṭi - paṭimi - paṭimam.
paṭi - vaṭi, paṭivu - vaṭivu. paṭivam - vaṭivam.
paṭi - prati (va.). paṭimi - pratimā (va.).  (ti.ma. 747.)
paṭu 
paṭu eṉṉum col perumi allatu mikutip poruḷ taruvatip paṭukuḻi, paṭutaṇṭam, paṭupoy, paṭumōcam eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkki yuṇarka.  (kuṟaḷ. 676).
paṭiñaṉ iyalpu 
paṭiñar pōril orukāl tam uyiriḻakkumāṟu pakivarik kollun toḻili allatu vellun toḻili mēṟkoṇṭiruppataṉālum avarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭut toṇṭum niliyāmic ciṟappum nōkki avarkaṭkuk koṭukkap paṭṭirukkum urimi allatu oḻukkavitit taḷarcciyiṉālum, avarkaḷ piṟarporuḷi virumpiyaviṭattu avarik kēḷāmalē eṭuttuk koḷvatu vaḻakkam. itaṟkut tārāḷam eṉṟu peyar. tār eṉpatu cēṉi. āḷam eṉpatu āḷiṉ taṉmi. tārāḷ, paṭiñaṉ. tārāḷam oruvarik kēḷāmalē avar poruḷi eṭuttuk koḷḷum paṭiñaṉ iyalpu. oruvarkkuriya iṭattil avarik kēḷāmaṟ pukuvatum tārāḷam eṉappaṭum. poruḷi vuvuvatum urimiyi vuvuvatum oṉṟē.  (col : 112.)
paṭiyum pātukāppum
1. paṭi
oru nāṭṭu araciyaṟkup paṭiyē aṭippaṭi. paṭiyiruppiṉ, nāṭillātavaṉum nāṭṭip peṟalām; paṭiyillāviṭiṉ, nāṭuṭiyavaṉum ati iḻappāṉ. ātaliṉ, paṭiyāṉatu aracaṉukku iṉṟiyamiyāta vuṟuppākum. itaṉālēyē aracarkkuriyavaṟṟik kūṟum tolkāppiyac cūttirattilum (1571). aracuṟuppukkaḷik kūṟum kuṟaḷilum (381). ciṟappuṭip poruḷi muṟpaṭak kiḷattal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟip paṭi muṟkūṟap peṟṟatu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cēṉiyi aracaṉ tirumēṉi eṉṟaṉar (cilap 25 : 191). aracar toḻilākiya kāval pōr eṉṉumiraṇṭum paṭiyiṉṟi yamiyāmiyiṉ,
uṟuppamin tūṟañcā velpaṭi vēntaṉ
veṟukkiyu ḷellān tali  (kuṟaḷ. 761).
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
paṭi vakikaḷ : paṭiyāṉatu. porum iṭampaṟṟi,
(1)	nilappaṭi (Military)
(2)	kaṭaṟpaṭi (Navy),
eṉa iruvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ nilappaṭi, ūrtipaṟṟi.
(1)	karippaṭi.
(2)	parippaṭi.
(3)	tērppaṭi.
(4)	kāṟpaṭi allatu kālāṭpaṭi,
eṉa nālvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉ, kālāṭpaṭi mīṇṭum karuvipaṟṟi,
(1)	viṟpaṭi.
(2)	vāṭpaṭi.
(3)	vēṟpaṭi.
eṉa  mūvakippaṭum. eppaṭiyarāyiṉum. pōrmaṟavarkkup porunar eṉṟum paṭiyāṭkaḷ eṉṟum paṭiñar eṉṟum peyar.
mūvēntariṭattum toṉṟutoṭṭuk kalappaṭi (kaṭaṟpaṭi) yiruntu vantatu.

pēe nili iya irumpavvattuk
koṭum puṇari vilaṅku pōḻak

neṭuṅkoṭimici yitiyeṭut

nāṭāra naṉ kiḻitarum
āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
maḻimuṟṟiya malipuriyat
tuṟimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkit
teṉkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelliṉ
ūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava
eṉṟu, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭimpalampaniṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ, kalappaṭi koṇṭu cāli eṉṉum cāvakat tīvi (java) veṉṟa ceytiyi, avaṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉāṉa taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ mēlēṟṟi maturik kāñci (75-88) kūṟuvatiyum;
ciṉamiku tāṉi vāṉavaṉ kuṭakaṭaṟ
polantaru nāvā yōṭṭiya ñāṉṟip
piṟarkalañ celkalā taṉiyēm
eṉṟu, ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēlkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ ceyta kaṭaṟpōrip paṟṟip puṟac ceyyuḷ (126) kūṟuvatiyum; ki.pi.10ām 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷi liruntē mutalām irācarācac cōḻaṉ, īḻattiyum munnīrp paḻantīvu paṉṉīrāyirattiyum  (Maldive islands) kalappaṭi koṇṭu veṉṟu, cēranāṭṭuk kāntaḷūrccāli kalamaṟut taruḷiyatiyum, avaṉ makaṉ irācēntiraṉ nakkavāram (Nicobar). maliyā (Malaya), cumaturā (Sumatra) mutaliyavaṟṟi veṉṟa tiyum nōkkuka.
muttamiḻaracariṭamum yāṉippaṭi mikutiyāyiruntatu. cōḻa vēntaṉiṭam aṟupatiṉāyiram yāṉikaḷ iruntatāka oru cīṉa vaḻippōkkaṉ kūṟiyiruppatāl, yāṉi iyalpākak kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāy vāḻum kuṭamalit toṭariyuṭiya cēraṉiṭattum pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattum, avi ettuṇip peruntoki yiṉavāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpati ūkittuṇarntu koḷḷalām.
yāṉi maṟavarkku, āṉiyāṭkaḷ āṉippākar kuñcaramallar attimallar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. aracaṉ aṭikkaṭi yāṉippaṭiyi aṇivakuppittuk kaṇṭu kaḷippaṉ. yāṉippaṭi akkālattuc ciṟanta paṭiyākak karutappaṭṭatu.
yāṉiyuṭiya paṭikāṇṭal mikaviṉitē
eṉṟār pūtañcēntaṉārum.
kutiri tamiḻakattiṟku allatu intu tēyattiṟku uriya vilaṅkaṉṟātaliṉ, atu ataṉ iyaṟki vāḻakamāṉa arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ērāḷamāka māperuñ celavil varuvikkap paṭṭatu.
parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟpurāṇam kutirikaḷip puravi, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṉṉi, kutiri, pari, kantukam eṉa eṭṭuvakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟum. (naripariyākkiya paṭalam, 87-94)
kutirimaṟavar, ivuḷimaṟavar eṉṟum kutiric cēvakar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭuvar.
paṭikku eṉṟum cēṅkutirikaḷum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷumē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum. viṉiyiṟciṟanta kutirikaṭkum yāṉikaṭkum ciṟappup peyariṭap paṭuvatuṇṭu.
tērppaṭi caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉ payaṉpaṭuttap peṟavilli.
iṉi, paṭiyi, mēṟkūṟiya muṟiyilaṉṟi, ūḻiyak kāla aḷavu paṟṟi, mūlappaṭi kūlippaṭi yeṉṟum; tokukkum iṭam paṟṟi. nāṭṭuppaṭi kāṭṭuppaṭi eṉṟum; cērntuḷḷa pakkam paṟṟi, tuṇippaṭi pakippaṭi eṉṟum; etirnili vakiyil ivviraṇṭāka vakuppatumuṇṭu.
mūlappaṭiyi vaḷḷuvar tolpaṭi eṉpar. vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka iruntu varuvatum, evvaki ūṟṟiyum poruṭpaṭuttātatum, iṟappiṟku añcātatum, pōriyē virumpuvatum, aracaṉik kākka eṉṟum uyiruvantīvatum, ekkāraṇattiyiṭṭum aṟipōkātatum, veṉṟē mīḷvatum tolpaṭit tiṟaṅkaḷām.
uṭpaki orutiṟam paṭṭeṉap puṭpakik
kēvā ṉākaliṟ cāvēm yāmeṉa
nīṅkā maṟavar vīṅkutōḷ puṭippa  (puṟam - 68)
eṉṟu kōvūr kiḻārum.
uṟiṉuyi rañcā maṟavar iṟivaṉ
ceṟiṉuñcīr kuṉṟa lilar
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉārum, kūṟiyiruttalāl;  nīṇṭa nāḷākap pōr peṟāta tolpaṭi taṉ maṟamikiyāl tāṉē pōrukku muṉivatum, aracaṉ taṭuppiṉum mīṟuvatum uṇṭeṉpataṟiyap paṭum.
kūlippaṭiyāvatu, pōrkkālattil putuvatākak kūlik
kamarttap paṭuvatu. nāṭṭuppaṭi marutanilamakkaḷ cērntatu. kāṭṭuppaṭi, pālinilavācikaḷum kuṟiñci nilamāntarum cērntatu. aracapattiyālum nāṭṭup paṟṟālum, kūlikkaṉṟit toṇṭu kāraṇamākat tāmāka vantutavum ūrmakkaḷ paṭi kuṭippaṭi eṉappaṭum.
pōrkkālattup perumpaṭi amitikkālattu vēṇṭā vātaliṉ. pōr muṭinta piṉ mūlappaṭiyoḻinta piṟa vellām kalikkappaṭuvatu marapu. aṉṟu mūlappaṭip pakutikaḷ talinakarilum, piṟa kōnakarkaḷilum ellip puṟaṅkaḷilum, putitāy vellappaṭṭa pakivar nāṭṭilum niṟuttap peṟum, aṅṅaṉam niṟuttap peṟṟavi nilippaṭi eṉap peyar peṟum, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kōṭṭāṟṟil oru nilippaṭiyi niṟuttiyiruntāṉ, kalikkappaṭṭa paṭiyiṉar tam maraput toḻilic ceytuvaruvar.
nilippaṭi niṟuttappaṭṭa nakar paṭivīṭu eṉṟum atu niṟuttappaṭṭa nilappakuti paṭippaṟṟu eṉṟum, atu kuṭiyirukkum vīṭṭut tokuti paṭinili eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. nilippaṭiyiṉar maṉivi makkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi vāḻvar.
paṭippayiṟci: aracarum paṭimaṟavarum maṟpōr paṭikkalap pōr ākiya iruvakip pōrum payiṉṟu vantaṉar.
maṟpōr payilvataṟku āṅkāṅku pōravi allatu muraṇkaḷari eṉappaṭum payiṟcik kūṭaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. kōp perunaṟkaḷḷi eṉṉuñ cōḻaṉ oru pōravi naṭatti vantatāl pōravik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
