pacittuppaṇi muyalum yāṉi pōla
irutali yociya eṟṟik
kaḷampuku mallaṟ kaṭantaṭu niliyē  (puṟam.80).
eṉṉum ceyyuṭkaḷ, muṟiyē, taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pala mallariyum, pōravik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi āmūr mallaṉiyum, maṟpōriṟ koṉṟatit terivikkum.
murukkamarattiṉ parutta aṭiyi naṭṭu ataṉmēṟpaṭik kalaṅkaḷi yeṟintu, porunar eṟipaṭippōr payiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu,
iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi kaṉmār
ikaliṉa reṟinta akalili murukkiṉ
perumarak kampam  (puṟam. 169).
eṉpatāl aṟiyalākum.
araṇmaṉiyi yaṭutta ceṇṭuveḷi allatu viyāḷi vīti eṉappaṭum veḷinilattil, vācivāriyar eṉpōr kutirikaṭku irucārikaḷum aiṅkatikaḷum patiṉeṇ maṇṭilaṅkaḷum payiṟṟiṉar. avaruḷ talimiyāṉavaṉ aracavāriyaṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ.
kāḻōr vātuvar eṉpōr yāṉikaḷip pōrt tuṟiyiṟ payiṟṟiṉar.
paṭippirivukaḷ : paṭikaḷ, karuvivakiyālum, ūrti vakiyālum, kula nila moḻi vakiyālum, ūḻiyavakiyālum, mutumi vakiyālum, vevvēṟu pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu vevvēṟu peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. appeyarkaḷ, perumpālum, aracaṉuṭiya koṟṟap peyarkaḷi muṉṉuṟuppākak koṇṭirukkum. cila pirivukaḷ ciṟutaṉam peruntaṉam eṉṟum, iṭaṅki valaṅki* eṉṟum, ivviru uṭpirivukaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉa.
villikaḷ vāḷpeṟṟa kikkōḷar, parāntakak koṅka vāḷar, paṇṭitac cōḻatterinta villikaḷ, uttamac cōḻat terinta antaḷakattār, ciṟutaṉattu vaṭukakkāvalar, valaṅkip paḻampaṭikaḷilār, pala vakip paḻampaṭikaḷilār, ciṅkaḷāntakatterinta kutiriccēvakar, mummuṭiccōḻat terinta āṉippākar, peruntaṉattu āṉiyāṭkaḷ, eṉpaṉa cila paṭippirivukaḷiṉ peyarākum.
paṭittalivar: paṭittalivarkku, paṭimutali, taṇṭa nāyakaṉ, cēṉimutali, cēṉāpati, cāmantaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; perumpaṭittalivaṉukkup perum paṭimutali, cēṉāvariyaṉ, cēṉātirāyaṉ, makātaṇṭa nāyakaṉ, makācāmantaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. nāyakaṉ eṉṉum peyar cēranāṭṭil nāyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. mutaliyār nāyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutali nāyaṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ uyarvup paṉmi vaṭivākum.
potuvāka, paṭittalimikku avvap paṭimaṟavaruḷ tiṟami mikkavarum, perum paṭittalimikku araca kuṭumpattiṉarum kuṟunilamaṉṉarum uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāṇ kuṭittalivarāṉa vēḷirumē, amarttap peṟṟaṉar.
paṭikaḷi mēṟpārttaṟkup paṭikāṇpār eṉṟu cila atikārikaḷ iruntaṉar.
paṭikkalaṅkaḷ:  amitikkālattil, meykāvaṟpaṭi parivārappaṭi nilippaṭi ākiya paṭikaḷ kiyāḷum paṭikkalaṅkaḷit tavira, maṟṟap pōrkkaruvikaḷellām paṭikkalakkoṭṭil eṉṉum āyutacāliyil vittup pōṟṟappeṟum. aracaṉuṭiya paṭikkalaṅkaḷi vittaṟkut taṉiyāka oru kaṭṭil uṇṭu. atu paṭikkalakkaṭṭil eṉap peṟum.
āyutaṅkaṭkellām neypūcip pīliyaṇintu māli cūṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
2. pātukāppu
taṟkāppu : aracaṉukkuk kaṭṭiṭavakiyālum kāvalāḷ vakiyālum, ciṟanta taṟkāppu vacatikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa.
kaḷvarum pakivarum ēṟaṟkariya matilum;  pakivarit tolivilēyē kāṇṭaṟkum, uḷ maṉik kalakattilum pōrilum ēṟip porutaṟkum, uytaṟkumuriya kōpuramum; pakivaṉ talinakari muṟṟukiyiṭumpōtu, noccippōr iyalātāyiṉ karantu veḷippaṭaṟkuriya curuṅkiyum;  kaṭṭiṭa vakiyāṉa taṟkāppākum.
meykāvalarum vēḷikkārarumeṉak kāvalāḷar irupālār.
meykāvalar, aṇukkar eṉavum, vācal meykāppōr eṉavum, parivāra meykāppōr eṉavum, mūvakiyar. avar, muṟiyē, aracaṉukku neruṅki niṟpavarum, araṇmaṉi vāyilkaḷil niṟpavarum, aracaṉ araṇmaṉikku veḷiyē celluṅkāl cūḻntu celpavarum, āvar. pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya meykāvaṟpaṭiyil rōma kirēkkap porunarum iruntaṉar.
vācal meykāppōriṉiṉṟum vēṟāka. vācal kāppōr eṉṟum cilar iruntaṉar. vāyilkaḷ, aṇukka vāyil iṭivāyil talivāyil puṟavāyil eṉap pala vātaliṉ, vācalkāppōrum, aṇukkavācal kāppōr  kēraḷāntaka vācal kāppōr eṉap pala tiṟattār. naṭip peruvāyil eṉappaṭum araṇmaṉit talivāyilil eppōtum oru paṭittalivaṉ niṟpāṉ.
parivāra meykkāppōriṉiṉṟum vēṟākap parivārattār eṉṟu cila paṭippirivukaḷiruntaṉa. kēraḷāntakat terinta parivārattār nikarilic cōḻat terinta uṭaṉilik kutiric cēvakar eṉṉum paṭip pirivup peyarkaḷ, parivārattāruḷ kālāḷ maṟavarum kutirimaṟavarumāka irupiriviṉar irunta miyit terivikkum, mūlap paṭiyic cērnta parivārattār mūlapparivāram eṉṉum peyarāṟ pirittuk kūṟap peṟṟaṉar. mūlapparivāra viṭṭēṟu eṉṉum peyar, mūlapparivārattuḷ karuvipaṟṟiya pala pirivukaḷiruntamiyik kāṭṭum.
aracaṉpāl aḷaviṟanta aṉpum pattiyum uṭiya ciṟṟaracarum paṭittalivarum, avaṉukku iṭukkaṇ nērntaviṭattu uṭukki iḻantavaṉ kipōl utavavēṇṭumeṉṉum pūṭki mēṟkoṇṭu, avvāṟu cūḷuṟavuñ ceytu koḷvar. uṟṟiṭattutavuvatōṭu uṭaṉiṟappatum avar vaḻakkam. vēḷiyil vantu utavuvatāl avarkku vēḷikkārar eṉṟu peyar. avarip pōṉṟē cūḷuṟṟa paṭikaḷ - vēḷikkārap paṭikaḷ eṉappaṭum. vēlikkārarkkuḷḷum vēlikkārap paṭikaṭkuḷḷum, valaṅki iṭaṅkip pirivukaḷum ciṟutaṉap peruntaṉap pirivukaḷum iruntaṉa. pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya vēḷik kārarkkut teṉṉavaṉ āpattutavikaḷ eṉṟu peyar.
aracaṉ ciṟanta paṭikkalap payiṟci peṟṟum iṭi vāḷēntiyu miruppaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ meyvaliyuṅ ki vāḷuṅkūṭa ōraḷavu taṟkāppāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aracamakaḷir uṟiyum uvaḷakattil, cōṉaka (milēcca)p pēṭiyar vāḷēnti niṉṟu kāval purintu vantaṉar.
atikāra vēṇavā tantiyiyum uṭaṉpiṟappiyumkūṭak kollumātaliṉ, aiyuṟaviṟkiṭamāṉa putalvariyum uṭaṉpiṟan tāriyum, paruvam vantavuṭaṉ talinakarkkup puṟampē vevvēṟu patavikaḷil amartti vippatum aracar vaḻakkam.
talinakark kāppu:  talinakarāṉatu, matilaraṇ, nilavaraṇ, nīraraṇ, kāṭṭaraṇ, maliyaraṇ eṉṉum aivakiyaraṇkaḷuḷ palavaṟṟik koṇṭiruntatu.
1. matilaraṇ :  matilaraṇ, matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa nālvakippaṭum. purici eṉpatu avaṟṟiṉ potuppeyar, purintiruppatu purici. purital vaḷital allatu cūḻtal. matil nakaric cūḻntiruppatāl purici eṉappaṭṭatu.
nālvaki matilaraṇkaḷuḷ, uyaramoṉṟēyuṭiyatu matil;  uyarattoṭu akalamumuṭiyatu eyil;  avaṟṟoṭu tiṇmiyumuṭiyatu iñci; ammūṉṟoṭu arumiyu muṭiyatu cō. ākavē, cōvaraṇē taliciṟantatām.
uyarvakalan tiṇmi arumiyin nāṉkiṉ
amivaraṇ eṉṟurikku nūl  (743),
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
noccippōril villiyar mēlniṉṟu maṟintu ampeytaṟ kuriya puḻikaḷiyuṭiyatu eyil. ampeyyum puḻi ēppuḻiyeṉṟum ēvaṟiyeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. eyyum illiyuṭiyatu eyil eṉappaṭṭatu.
cempiyurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiṉa matil iñciyeṉpar. iñcutal iṟukutal. iñciyatu iñci.
cempiṭṭuc ceyta viñcit tirunakar  (kumpakaruṇaṉ vatippaṭalam,159)
eṉṟu kamparum,
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici
 uvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu  (puṟam. 201)
eṉṟu kapilarum pāṭiyiruppatāl, ilaṅkiyilum tuvāra camuttiram eṉṉum tuvari nakarilum* iñciyaraṇ iruntami aṟiyappaṭum. ceppukkōṭṭi eṉpatu irāvaṇaṉ kōṭṭip peyarāka iṉṟum īḻattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
matilaraṇi arumippaṭuttuvatu poṟiyātalāl, ēvaṟi kaḷum poṟikaḷumuṭiya iñciyē cōvaraṇāyirun tiruttal vēṇṭum.
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅki kaṭṭaḻitta
eṉṟu āycciyar kuraviyum,
cuḻalaḻaluḷ vikiṉṟu cō
eṉṟu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyum (228), kūṟuvatāl, irāvaṇaṉ nakarilum vāṇācuraṉ nakarilum cōvaraṇ irunta tākat terikiṉṟatu. maturi matilaraṇ pala poṟikaḷik koṇṭiruntatāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟuvatāl, ataṉi oruvakic cōvaraṇ eṉṉalām.
maturip puṟamatililirunta poṟikaḷuṅ karuvikaḷum uṟuppuk kaḷumāvaṉa:-
1.	vaḷiviṟpoṟi - vaḷintu tāṉē eyyum entira vil.
2.	karuviralūkam - kariya viraliyuṭiya kuraṅku pōliruntu cērntārik kaṭikkum poṟi.
3.	kallumiḻ kavaṇ - kalliyeṟiyum iruppuk kavaṇ (iṭaṅkaṇi).
4.	parivuṟu venney - kāyntiṟittalāṟ cērntāri varuttum ney.
5.	pākaṭu kuḻici - cempurukkum miṭā.
6.	kāypoṉṉuli - urukak kāycciyeṟiyum eḵku uli.
7.	kalliṭu kūṭi - iṭaṅkaṇip poṟikkuk kalliṭṭu vikkuṅ kūṭi.
8.	tūṇṭil - akaḻiyik kaṭantu matilip paṟṟuvāri māṭṭiyiḻukkun tūṇṭil vaṭivāṉa poṟi.
9.	toṭakku - kaḻuttiṟ pūṭṭi muṟukkuñ caṅkili.
10.	āṇṭaliyaṭuppu - paṟantu ucciyik kotti mūḷiyik kaṭikkum āṇṭalippuḷ vaṭivamāṉa poṟivarici.
11.	kavi - akaḻiyiṉiṉṟēṟiṉ ataṟkuḷ viḻat taḷḷum iruppukkavi.
12.	kaḻu - kaḻukkōl.
13.	puti - ampukkaṭṭu
14.	puḻi - ēvaṟikaḷ.
15.	aiyavit tulām - amputtiraḷ toṅkaviṭṭa viṭṭam.
16.	kipeyarūci - matiṟṟaliyip paṟṟuvāriṉ kiyip potukkum ūcippoṟikaḷ.
17.	ceṉṟeṟi ciral - pakivarmēṟ ceṉṟu avar kaṇṇik kottum ciccilikkuruvi vaṭivāṉa poṟi.
18.	paṉṟi - matiṟṟaliyilēṟiṉā ruṭalik kompāṟ kiḻikkum iruppup paṉṟi.
19.	paṇi - aṭikkum mūṅkiltaṭi vaṭivamāṉa poṟi.
20.	eḻuvuñcīppu - katavukku valiyāka uḷvāyiṟpaṭiyil nilattil kīḻēviṭum maraṅkaḷ.
21.	kaṇiyam - kōṭṭi matiṟ katavukkut taṭiyākak kuṟukkēyiṭum uttaram.
22.	kōl - viṭṭēṟu.
23.	kuntam - ciṟucavaḷam.
24.	vēl.
25.	ñāyil - kuruvittali eṉṉum matiluṟuppukkaḷ.
26.	nūṟṟuvarik kolli.
27.	taḷḷiveṭṭi.
28.	kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi.
29.	viḻuṅkum pāmpu.
30.	kaḻukuppoṟi.
31.	pulippoṟi.
32.	kuṭappāmpu.
33.	cakaṭappoṟi.
34.	takarppoṟi.
35.	ari nūṟpoṟi.
mutaliyaṉa.
ivaṟṟuḷ, iṟutiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa pattum, iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷāl piṟa eṉṉum collāl taḻuvap peṟṟu uriyāciriya rāṉa aṭiyārkku nallārāl peyar kuṟikkap peṟṟavi.
talinakarkaḷil, matil oṉṟaṉṟi oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu cūḻntu palavā yiruppatumuṇṭu. akamatilkaḷiviṭap puṟamatilkaḷ paṭimuṟiyil araṇ ciṟantirukkum. kōṭuṟaḻnteṭutta  eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattuc ceyyuḷil akamatil matil eṉṟum, puṟamatil iñciyeṉṟum, kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. puṟa matilil tāṉ mēṟkūṟiya palavakip poṟikaḷ amikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. matiliyoṭṭi uṭpuṟattiluḷḷa mēṭikku akappā eṉṟu peyar.
pakivar varavit tolivilēyē kāṇumpaṭi, puṟamatiliṉ mēl pala ticiyilum aṭṭāli eṉṉuṅ kāvaṟ kōpuraṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭirukkum. avaṟṟiliruntu kāval ceyvārkku aṭṭālic cēvakar eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru matilukkum kāvalarum, matil nāyaṉ eṉṉum kāval talivaṉum iruppar.
matiluḷḷa nakarkaḷ perumpālum, kōṭṭi, puri, purici, eyil, kaṭakam eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟip peyarākavō peyarīṟākavō koṇṭiruntaṉa.
2. nilavaraṇ : nilavaraṇ eṉpatu, pakivar akaḻi kaṭantavuṭaṉ puṟamatilip paṟṟāmipporuṭṭu ataṉ puṟattu viṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷa veḷḷiṭi nilamum; pakivar muṟṟuki nīṭittirukkum pōtu, akattārkku vēṇṭum uṇavup poruḷkaḷi viḷittuk kōṭaṟporuṭṭup puṟamatiliṉ uṭpuṟamāka viṭappaṭum taṇṇaṭi nilamum eṉa iruvaki.
maṇinīru maṇṇu maliyu maṇiniḻaṟ
kāṭu muṭiya taraṇ
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil (742). maṇ eṉṟatu nilavaraṇi. parimēlaḻakar ataṟku veḷḷiṭi nilameṉṟu colluri kūṟi, matiṟpuṟattu marutanilam pakivar paṟṟāmip poruṭṭu eṉṟu viḷakka vuri kūṟiyiruppatiyum; nāṭukaṇṭaṉṉa kaṇituñcu vilaṅkal  eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattut toṭarukku (16:2)  neṭunāṭpaṭa aṭimatiṟpaṭṭa kālattē viḷittuk kōṭaṟku, vayalum kuḷamum uḷavākac camittu vittamiyāṟ kaṇṭārkku nāṭu kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa.... iṭimatil, eṉṟu an nūliṉ paḻiya vuriyāciriyar viḷakka murittiruppatiyum kāṇka.
3. nīraraṇ :  nīraraṇ eṉpatu, puṟamatiṟ puṟattu āṟuṅ kaṭalumākiya iyaṟki nīrniliyākavum, akaḻi allatu kiṭaṅku eṉṉum ceyaṟki nīrniliyākavum, irukkum araṇ.
4. kāṭṭaraṇ :  kāṭṭaraṇ eṉpatu, paṭimaṟavar pakivarkkut teriyāmal maṟintirukkak kūṭiyatum, tokutiyākap puka muṭiyātatumāṉa maramaṭar cōli. itu potuvāka maliyaṭuttiruppatu.
5. maliyaraṇ :  maliyaraṇ eṉpatu, makkaḷ tokutiyāka ēṟa muṭiyātatum. mēliruntu peruṅkaṟkaḷik kīḻē eḷitāka uruṭṭak kūṭiyatum, ucciyil pōtiya uṇavum uṟiyuḷum uṭiyatumāṉa, paṟampupōlum taṉik kuṉṟu. itu kuṟunila maṉṉarkku - avaruḷḷum kuṟumparacarkku - iṉṟiyamiyātatu. caṅkakālattuk kuṟunila maṉṉar, perumpālum, ovvoru malikkiḻavarāyiruntaṉar.
talinakarkaḷil, irākkālattil, ūrkāvalar yāmantōṟum paṟiyaṟintu viḷakkuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, kaḷvar kaḷavu ceyyātapaṭi kāval ceytu  vantaṉar.
perunāṭṭukkāppu :  nilippaṭikaḷ, talinakarilum piṟa kōnakarkaḷilum kalakam nērum iṭaṅkaḷilum, pakiyaccamuḷḷa ellippuṟaṅkaḷilum, niṟuttappeṟṟirukkumeṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
nāṭṭiṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum, pakivar varavip pārttaṟiyak kūṭiya uyarnta pārvalirukkikaḷ iruntaṉa.
aracaṉuṭiya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (allatu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa) oṟṟar, piṟavaracar talinakarkaḷil iravum pakalum karanta kōlat tuṭaṉ tirintu, aṅku naṭappavaṟṟi uṭaṉuṭaṉ aracaṉukku maṟivākat terivittu vantaṉar. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉ, taṉ vaṭanāṭṭuc celavi muṉṉatāka vaṭanāṭṭaracarkkut tūtar vāyilāyt terivikkak karutiyapōtu, aḻumpilvēḷ eṉṉum amiccaṉ, in nāvalantīvil namakkup pakivarāyuḷḷa araca ruṭiya oṟṟarellām, ivvañcimā nakaril nīṅkātuṟivar. avvoṟṟarē nam celavi avvaracariṭam terivittu viṭuva rātaliṉ, avarkkut tūtar vāyilāyt tirumukam viṭukka vēṇṭā. innakariṟ paṟiyaṟintu viḷamparañ ceytalē pōtu māṉatu, eṉṟu kūṟiyatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
aracaṉuṭiya vēlikkārarum paṭittalivarāṉa ciṟṟaracarum perunāṭu muḻutumiruntamiyiṉ, pakivar varaviyaṟintavuṭaṉ paṭitiraṇṭu evviṭattilum pōr ceytu nāṭṭik kāttuk koḷvataṟku vacatiyāyiruntatu.
paṇṭamāṟṟuṅ kācum
caṅkakāla nāṇayattiṭṭam :  ulakameṅkaṇum mutaṟ kālattil, poruṭpari māṟṟellām paṇṭamāṟṟu (Barter) muṟiyi lēyē naṭantu vantatu. ataṉpiṉ paṭippaṭiyākak kācu (nāṇayam) eṉṉum parimāṟṟuk karuvi ēṟpaṭṭatu.
piṟanāṭukaḷil, nāṇayam mutalāvatu kalluṅ kavaṭiyum koṭṭiyum tōltuṇukkum pōṉṟa ulaka matippaṟṟa kuṟip poruḷāyiruntatu. piṉpu tātu (ulōka)p poruḷāka māṟiyatu. tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāyp poṉ viḷintatiṉālum, paḻaṅkālattilēyē tamiḻar nākarikamaṭintiruntatiṉālum, tamiḻar toṭakkattilēyē tātukkaḷiṟ ciṟanta poṉṉik kācākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
tamiḻar mutaṉmutaṟ payaṉpaṭuttiya tātu poṉṉāyiruntatiṉā lēyē poṉ eṉṟa colliṟku ulōkam eṉṉum poruḷum, piṟkālattiṟ kiṭitta veḷḷi cempirumpiṟku muṟiyē veṇpoṉ cempoṉ karumpoṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷum, tōṉṟiṉa.
ulaka matippuḷḷatum, uṟutiyāṉatum, eḷitāy iṭam peyarkkak kūṭiyatum, ciṟu matippiyum perumatippiyuṅ kuṟikkac ciṟitākavum peritākavum veṭṭappaṭak kūṭiyatum, aṅṅaṉam veṭṭappaṭiṉum taṉ aḷaviṟkuriya matippiḻakkāta tum, urukki vēṇṭiyāṅku vaṭivuṟuttakkūṭiyatum, poṉṉē yeṉṟu tamiḻar kaṇṭu koṇṭatiṉāl ataṉiyē parimāṟṟuk karuviyākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.
āyiṉum, mutalaṭiyilēyē, poṉ ippōtuḷḷa kācāka muttiriyiṭṭa takaṭṭu vaṭivam peṟavilli. atu ciṟitum peritumāṉa kaṭṭiyākavē (Bullion) mutalāvatu vaḻaṅkivantatu. ak kaṭṭikaḷ, koṭpayaṟu (kāṇam, kuṉṟimaṇi, mañcāṭi viti, kaḻaṟcikkāy (kaḻaṅku), mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷiṉ niṟi kaḷuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟik kaṭṭi nāṇayam eṉṉalām.
piṟa tātukkaḷ kiṭikkap peṟṟup poṉ aruki vantapōtu, nāṇayap poṟkaṭṭikaḷ pala, nāḷaṭivil, arāviyeṭukkap paṭṭu eṭi kuṟintum cempu kalantu māṟṟut tāḻntum pōyiṉa. ataṉāl, araciyalatikārikaḷum nakara vaṇikar kuḻāṅkaḷum, nāṇayak kaṭṭikaḷiyellām urittum niṟuttum nōṭṭañceytu, avaṟṟuḷ māṟṟum niṟiyum cariyāyuḷḷavaṟṟiṟku muttiriyiṭa vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. aṅṅaṉam muttiriyiṭuvataṟkuk kaṭṭi vaṭiviṉum takaṭṭu  vaṭivam ēṟṟatātaliṉ, muttiri nāṇayaṅkaḷellām nāḷaṭi vil takaṭṭu vaṭivu peṟṟu viṭṭaṉa. muttiriyum takaṭṭu vaṭivumē nāṇattiṟku mutaṉmiyāṉa iyal kaḷātaliṉ, muttiri peṟṟa niliyik kācu nāṇayat toṭakkam eṉak kūṟalām.
aṉṟē yaminta pacumpoṉ aṭar āṟukōṭi
kuṉṟāmal viṟṟāṉ kuḷircākara tattaṉ eṉpāṉ
eṉṉum cintāmaṇic ceyyuṭ pakutiyil (1973) vantuḷḷa poṉ aṭar eṉpatit takaṭṭuppoṉ eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. aṭar eṉpatu takaṭu.
kaṭic caṅkak kālattil, paṇṭamāṟṟu, kaṭṭi nāṇayam, kācu nāṇayam ākiya mūvaki muṟikaḷum iruntu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaḻañcu māḻi eṉpaṉa kaṭṭi nāṇayamākavum, aḵkam kāṇam kācu poṉ mutaliyavi kācu nāṇayamākavum, iruntaṉapōlum. taṉiyuṭimi nāṭṭilellām, nāṭṭup puṟac ciṟṟūrc cillaṟi viṟpaṉiyil paṇṭamāṟṟu eṉṟuṅ kiyāḷap peṟum.
caṅka kālattil, veḷḷi cempirumpiruntum tamiḻakattu nāṇayaṅkaḷellām taṅkattiṉālēyē iyaṉṟu, et tokikkum celāvaṇiyuḷḷaṉavā yiruntatiṉāl, akkāla nāṇayat tiṭṭatti (Currency Standard) oṟṟiyulōkat tiṭṭam (Monometallism) eṉṟum;  taṅka nāṇayat tiṭṭam (Gold Standard) eṉṟum, niṟi celāvaṇic caṭṭa nāṇayat tiṭṭam (Unlimited legal tender) eṉṟum, kūṟalām.
pulavarum pulattiyarum ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa kāṇamum kācum, pāṇarum āṭiyaṉmakaḷirum āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉṉum, aracariṭam peṟṟu vantaṉar eṉpatiṉāl, kaṭic caṅkak kālattup poṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷ niṟi celāvaṇi yuṭiyaṉa vāyiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
araṇmaṉikku aṇikalam ceyyum taṭṭārē poṟkācukaḷi yum aṭittu vantaṉar. kācaṭikkum iṭattiṟku aḵkacāli eṉṟum, atil viṉiceyyum taṭṭārkku aḵkacāliyar eṉṟum, accāliyum avarum irunta teruviṟku aḵkacālit teru eṉṟum, peyar vaḻaṅkiṉa. aḵkam eṉpatu kācu eṉṉum nāṇayattil paṉṉiraṇṭi loru pākam eṉṟum, kācu eṉpatu patiṉāṉku kuṉṟimaṇi eṭiyuḷḷateṉṟum, collappaṭum. aḵkamuṅ kācum cikkeṉat tēṭu  eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiṉāl, ivviru kācukaḷum peru vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅ kiṉami aṟiyappaṭum.
mūvēntarum tattam muttiriyiṭṭa kācukaḷiyē tattam nāṭṭil araciyal nāṇayamāka vaḻaṅkuvittaṉar. avaṟṟil muttiri tavira vēṟoṉṟum poṟikkappaṭavilli. avi cēraṉ kācu cōḻaṉ kācu pāṇṭiyaṉ kācu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉa. avi aḷavil ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ kācukaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟil oru mīṉum, cilavaṟṟil iru mīṉum, cilavaṟṟil mum mīṉum poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
akkālattu nāṇayaṅkaḷ, poṉṉā liyaṉṟaṉavākavum, ulōka matippākiya (Bullion value) akamatippum (Intrinsic value) araciyal matippākiya puṟamatippum (Face Value) ottaṉavākavum, iruntatiṉāl, tamiḻakattār maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa nāṭṭārum viḻuc celvamāka virumpiyēṟkum niliyiliruntaṉa.
caṅka kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nāṇayattiṭṭam
tamiḻaracar kācukaḷ:  aḷaviṟanta poṟ kācukaḷ koṭivaki yiṟ koṭukkap paṭṭu viṭṭataṉālum, aṇikalam teyvaccili ampalamukaṭu taṭṭumuṭṭu mutaliya pala vakiyil iṭṭu vikkap paṭṭa poṉmutal muḻukiya mutalāy (Sunk capital)  iruntatiṉā lum, paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil peruntoki yāṉa poṟkācukaḷ makkaṭkut teriyātapaṭi maṇṇiṟ putiyuṇṭu kiṭantatiṉālum, piṟa nāṭṭaracar aṭikkaṭi koḷḷi koṇṭum tiṟikoṇ ṭum ceṉṟatiṉālum, varavarap poṉviḷivu ciṟuttum makkaṭ ṭoki peruttum vantatiṉālum, caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉpu nāṇayac celāvaṇikkup pōtiya poṉ kiṭṭāmiyāl, kīḻttara nāṇayaṅkaṭku mutalāvatu veḷḷi payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. aṉṟu iraṭṭi nāṇayattiṭṭam (Bimetallism) ēṟpaṭṭatu. ciṟitu kālattiṉ piṉ veḷḷiyum arukivaravē, kaṭik kīḻttara nāṇayaṅkaṭkuc cempu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. aṉṟu mukki nāṇayat tiṭṭam (Trimetallism) ēṟpaṭṭatu. atil, talittara nāṇayaṅkaḷ poṉṉākavum, iṭittara nāṇa yaṅkaḷ veḷḷiyākavum, kaṭittara nāṇayaṅkaḷ cempākavum iruntaṉa. iṅṅaṉam, taṅka nāṇayam talimi nāṇayamum (Principal coin) veḷḷi ceppu nāṇayam cillaṟi nāṇayamum (Subsidiary coin) āyiṉa.
mūvaki yulōka nāṇayaṅkaḷum mutalāvatu orē niṟi yuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntatāka ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.
kācu poṉ kaḻañcu māṭi (māḻi) eṉap pala taramāy vaḻaṅkiya poṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷuḷ, uyarntaviyellām ovvoṉṟāy nīṅki, iṟutiyil kācu oṉṟē eñciyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. poṉ nāṇaya aḷavilēyē veḷḷi ceppu nāṇaṅkaḷum aṭikkappaṭṭu. aviyum avaṟṟōṭortta niṟiyuḷḷa  poṉnāṇayam pōṉṟē kācu kaḻañcu māṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa.*
mūvaki yulōkak kācukaḷum 14 kuṉṟimaṇiyiliruntu 72 kuṉṟi maṇivari palvēṟu eṭiyuḷḷaṉavāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatāl, ovvōr ulōkattilum, peruṅkācum ataṉ pakutikaḷāṉa ciṟu kācukaḷum vaḻaṅkiyiruntami ūkikkappaṭum. iṉi, orētarak kācu vevvēṟaracar kālattil vevvēṟu niṟiyuḷḷa tāyirun tiruppatāl, avvavvaracar kālattu ulōka iruppiṟkut takkapaṭi, kācukaḷiṉ eṭi ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
pōrk kālattil aracaṉāl aṭikkappaṭṭa poṟkācu kaḷilum, potuvākap poṟkollarāl tiruṭṭuttaṉamāy aṭikkappaṭṭa poṟkācukaḷilum, cempu kalantiruntatiṉāl;  avaṟṟi makkaḷ vāci peṟṟē vāṅkiṉar. avi vācipeṟṟa kācukaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vāciyāvatu, kalavip poṟkāciṟ kalantuḷḷa cempiṟkāka vāṅkappaṭṭa vaṭṭam. tūya poṟ kācu naṟkācu eṉappaṭṭatu.
tirunāvukkaracarum tiruñāṉacampantamūrttiyārum tiruvīḻi miḻalit tirukkōyiliṟ peṟṟa kācukaḷuḷ, muṉṉavar peṟṟatu naṟkācum piṉṉavar peṟṟatu kalappuk kācumāyiruntaṉa. kalappuk kāciṟku vāci vāṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, campantar vāci tīravē - kācunalkiṭīr - māciṉmiḻaliyīr - ēcalilliyē,  eṉṉun tiruppatikam pāṭi naṟkācu peṟṟār.
naṟkācukaḷiyum kalappuk kācukaḷiyum urittuk kāṇpataṟku āṅkāṅku kaṭṭaḷikkal eṉṉum uri kallum, taṇṭavāṇi eṉṉum uriyāṇiyum iruntaṉa. 
kāycci urukkiṉum māṟṟum niṟiyum kuṉṟāteṉpataṟkaṟi kuṟiyāka, atikārikaḷāl tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭa tuḷippoṉ eṉpatum, akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. tūya veḷḷikkācum tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.*
mutaṟkālattil akamatippum puṟamatippum ottirunta nāṇayaṅ kaḷ cila, piṟkālattil niṟi kuṟintum tāḻnta tātu kalantum kuṟināṇayam (Token coins) āyiṉa.
caṅka kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kācukaḷil, aracar kula muttiri yōṭu avvavvaracar ciṟappup peyarum vaḻipaṭu teyvakkuṟiyum piṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi avaṟṟi aṭip pitta aracar peyarāl maturāntakaṉ māṭi  vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ kācu  eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉa. muttirimaṭṭumuḷḷa paḻaṅkācukaḷ paḻam pāṇṭiyaṉ kācu paḻañcōḻaṉ kācu eṉap paṭṭaṉa. pēraracar kācukaḷil, avarāl vellappaṭṭa aracar muttirikaḷum uṭaṉ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. cila kācukaḷ irupuṟamum orē vaki yākavum, cila vēṟu vakiyākavum, kuṟikaḷ koṇṭiruntaṉa.
kācaṭippataṟkut taṉivari vāṅkappaṭṭatu. atu aṭikācu poṉvari māṭikkūli mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
mūvēntar kācukaḷum, mutalāvatu caturamākavum nīḷ caturamākavu miruntu piṉṉar vaṭṭa vaṭivu peṟṟaṉa veṉṟu, nāṇaya vārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.
piṟavaracar kācukaḷ:  ki.pi.4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu, vaṇikat toṭarpākavum araciyal toṭarpākavum. piṟavaracar kācukaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkivantaṉa.
palakāla rōmakkācukaḷ - ciṟappāka 4ām 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuriyavi - tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum, avaṟṟuḷḷum ciṟappāka maturiyic cuṟṟiyum, mūvulōkattilum peruvāri yāyk kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutaṟpāṇṭiyap pēraracuk kālattil (590-925) tirupputtūriluḷḷa oru pārppaṉiyār avvūrk kōyilil oru viḷakkēṟṟutaṟku 10 tiṉāram (rōmap poṟkācu)* (denarius) aḷittaṉar. caṭāvarmaṉ kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ (1190- 1217), taṉ peyar tāṅkiya kuḷam oṉṟi āḻamākkutaṟku 100 tirammam (kirēkkap poṟkācu) (drachma) koṭuttāṉ. itaṉāl, yavaṉak kācukaḷ aracarālum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭami aṟiyalākum.
pallavar mēmpaṭṭirunta iṭikkālattil, avaruṭiya kāḷi muttiriyum viḷakku cakkaram mutaliya piṟa kuṟikaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭa kācukaḷ, cōḻanāṭu muḻumiyum paravi yiruntaṉa.
12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vīrapāṇṭiyaṉukkum kulacēkara pāṇṭi yaṉukkum naṭanta uḷnāṭṭup pōril, muṉṉavaṉukkut tuṇiyāyirunta mutalām parākkiramapāku eṉṉum ilaṅki yaracaṉukkup pāṇṭi nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa ātikkattiṉāl, avaṉatu kākap paṇam eṉṉum īḻakkācu ciṟitukālam teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. atu caturamāṉatum, 72 allatu 73 kuṉṟimaṇi eṭiyuḷḷatum, oru muraṭṭumāntaṉ oru puṟattil niṟpatum oru puṟattil iruppatum pōlap poṟittatum, aracu (pōti) yāṉi cakkaram kumpam mutaliya vuruvaṅkaḷik koṇṭatu māṉa ceppuk kācākum.
cāḷukkiyarkkut tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattil celvākkēṟ paṭṭa piṟkālattil, avarkaḷatu varāka muttiri koṇṭatum 3½ rūpā matippuḷḷatumāṉa varākaṉ eṉṉum poṟkācu, tamiḻ nāṭu muḻumiyum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ṭilliyiliruntu āṇṭa ṭōkḷāk 
(1325-50) eṉṉum mukamatiyap pēraracaṉiṉ taṅkar eṉṉum ceppukkācu, teṉṉāṭuvari vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam, iṟutikkālattil, mūvēntar kācukaḷiṭiyē vēṟu pallaracar kācukaḷum viravi vaḻaṅkiyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa.  (pa.ta.pa.nā.)
paṇṭāram 
paṇṭāram - va. pāṇṭāra (bh) 
paṇ - paṇṭu - paṇṭam = paṇṇappaṭṭa poruḷ.
paṇṭam - paṇṭāram = carakkaṟi, pokkacam (Treasure) pokkacaccāli (Treasury) kaḷañciyam, pokkacaccāliyuḷḷa kōyil allatu araṇmaṉi, palporuṭ carakkaṟipōṉṟa perum paṇṭitaṉ, perumpulamiyāl ulakappaṟṟu viṭṭa tuṟavi, pōlit tuṟaviyāṉa irappōṉ.
paṇṭāra vāriyam = kōyil mēṟpārvik kuḻuvār. 
(T.A.-S.I.293) 
paṇṭāram - va. piṇṭāra
paṇṭāram = pōlit tuṟaviyāṉa āṇṭi, irappōṉ.
vaṭavar piṇṭam (cōṟṟuruṇṭi) vāṅkip piḻippavaṉ eṉṟu mūlapporuḷ kūṟuvar. itaṟkup paṇṭāram eṉṉum collip piṇṭāra eṉṟu tirittuk koṇṭatē kāraṇam. (va.va. 198).
paṇṭitaṉ
paṇṭitaṉ - va. paṇṭita
paṇṭu- paṇṭam - paṇṭitaṉ = pala poruḷkaḷi aṟinta pulavaṉ. paṇṭitaṉ - paṇṭitam = pulami.
pērā. parō vēṟu vakiyil iccollit teṉ collākak kāṭṭuvar. avar kūṟumāṟu:-
“pandÀita - ‘wise, learned’; properly ‘ripened, mature’ cf. Te. panÀdÀu ‘to ripen, mature; ripe’, panÀdÀa ‘ wisdom, intelligence’, Pj. panÀdÀ - ‘to mature’, Go. Kol. panÀdÀ - ‘to ripen’- The Sanskrit Language, p. 384. 
vaṭavar, paṇṭā (ōti, aṟivu, kalvi) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu cilarum, paruntita (tuṭippu) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu cilarum, āka iru vēṟu vakiyil paṇṭitaṉ eṉṉum collit tirippar. paṇṭā eṉṉum collum paṇṭam eṉpatiṉ tiripē. tuṭippi maṉattuṭippeṉpar.  (va.va. 198).
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇam
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇam eṉṟu iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavi, tamiḻakattil, toṉṟutoṭṭup pirāmaṇar peruntokiyāy vantu palavūrkaḷilum taṅkumaḷavum, tamiḻap pārppārālum kulat talivarālum tamiḻil naṭattap peṟṟu vanta maṇaṅ kaḷākum.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu pukat toṭaṅkiyatu ēṟattāḻak ki.mu.2000 eṉiṉum, avar mutalaṭiyilēyē peruntokiyiṉarāy iṅku vantavarallar. mutaṉ mutal iṅkuk kāl vitta pirāmaṇar viral viṭṭu eṇṇattakkavarē yāvar. kiṟittava vūḻi toṭaṅkumvari iṭiyiṭṭiṭiyiṭṭuc ciṟu ciṟu kūṭṭattārākavē avar vantukoṇṭiruntaṉar. ki.pi.3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ, pallava aracar kālattiltāṉ, avar peruvāriyāka vaṭa nāṭṭiliruntu kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭaṉar. itip piṟkālac cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarum piṉpaṟṟiṉar.
māmutu pārppāṉ maṟivaḻi kāṭṭiṭa  eṉṟu cilappatikāram pirāmaṇa allatu āriyap pārppaṉap purōkitattik kuṟit tiruppatu, kaṭiccaṅkak kālattil nakaraṅkaḷilum mānakaraṅ kaḷilum perumpālum aracariṭiyum vaṇikariṭiyum irunta nilimiyittāṉ kuṟikkum. iṉṟum pirāmaṇarillāta pala nāṭṭuppuṟattūrkaḷ iruppatālum, cila tamiḻakkulattār pirāmaṇap purōkitamiṉṟit tam maṇaṅkaḷi naṭattik koḷvatālum, pirāmaṇar vantapiṉpum, kaṭiccaṅkak kālam vari perumpāl tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷ tamiḻmarapilēyē naṭantu vantaṉa eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
tamiḻap pārppār, paṇṭāram, pulavaṉ, kurukkaḷ, tiru(kkaḷ), pūcāri, uvaccaṉ, ōtuvāṉ, pōṟṟi, nampi, arumikkāraṉ (puṭavikkāraṉ), vaḷḷuvaṉ mutaliya palvēṟu vakuppār āvar. pārppāṉ kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷip pārppavaṉ.
antaṇaṉ aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻat tuṟaviyariyē kuṟittatu pōṉṟu, pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyiyē kuṟittatu. turi eṉṉum tamiḻ allatu teluṅkac col nam nāṭṭil taṅkum mēṉāṭṭārkku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu pōṉṟē, antaṇar, pārppār eṉṉum peyarkaḷum pirāmaṇarkku variyaṟukkap paṭṭaṉa eṉka. tamiḻaruḷ, pārppār eṉpār illaṟattārum, antaṇar eṉpār tuṟavaṟattārum āvar.
kulattalivarāvār, nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periya taṉakkāraṉ, ampalakkāraṉ, ūrkkavuṇṭaṉ, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ūrāḷi, mūppaṉ eṉa āṅkāṅku vevvēṟu peyarāl aḻikkappeṟum kulaviyal ūrāṭciyāḷar.
paṇṭit tamiḻmaṇa muṟiyilēyē iṉṉum pala maṇaṅkaḷ naṭantu varuvatāl, avaṟṟiyun taḻuvumāṟu paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇam eṉṉum talippiṟkup paṇṭik kālattil ellāt tamiḻarum kiyāṇṭa maṇamuṟi eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka.
1. maṇavaki
1. ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷiṉ ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu, piṉ varumāṟu palvēṟu muṟi paṟṟiyatākum.
1. koḷmuṟi paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭittamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, peṇkōṭal muṟi paṟṟi, (1) koṭimaṇam (2) kātal maṇam, (3) kavarvu (vaṉkōḷ) maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
(1) koṭi maṇam
koṭimaṇamāvatu, maṇamakaṉēṉum avaṉ peṟṟōrēṉum maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōri aṭuttukkēṭka, avar koṭuppatu. atu, (i) tāṉakkoṭi, (ii) vilikkoṭi, (iii) nilippāṭṭuk koṭi eṉa mūvakittu.
tāṉakkoṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷit takka ēḻi maṇāḷaṉukkut tāmē ellāc celavum ēṟṟu maṇañceytu vippatu;  vilikkoṭiyāvatu, oruvaṉ taṉ makaḷukku īṭāka oru tokiyi oru celvaṉiṭam peṟṟuk koṇṭu; avaḷi avaṉukku maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu;  vili viṟpaṉi;  itu kīḻōriṭam aruki nikaḻvatu:  nilippāṭṭuk koṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷi oruvaṉukku ētēṉum nilippāṭu allatu akkuttup (Condition) paṟṟi maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu. an nilippāṭu, ittuṇip parican taral vēṇṭu meṉṟum, ittuṇik kālam peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōrukku uḻittal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, iṉṉa maṟac ceyalip purital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇamakaḷi iṉṉa kaliyil vellutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇattiṉpiṉ peṇ vīṭṭilēyē vatital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, palvēṟu tiṟappaṭṭatāyirukkum.
maṟacceyalkaḷ, kollēṟu kōṭal (ēṟu taḻuval), tiripaṉṟi yeytal, pulippāl kaṟattal, koṭuvilaṅku kōṟal, pakivari yaḻittal, vilnāṇēṟṟal, peruṅkal tūkkal mutaliyaṉa. ēṟu- kāḷi, kōṭal- aṭakkal, kōṟal - kollutal. tiripaṉṟi yeytalāvatu, mika vuyarattil virivākac cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum ciṟiya paṉṟiyuruvi orē taṭaviyil eytu vīḻttutal.
paṇṭik kālattil mulli nilattilirunta āyariṭi ēṟu taḻuvi maṇappatē kulamarapāka iruntu vantatu. ōr āyarpāṭiyil allatu cēriyil, oru peṇ kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ ataṉ peyarukku oru cēṅkaṉṟip peṟṟōr otukki vippar. ak kaṉṟik kāyaṭiyāmalum, vēliyil vayakkāmalum, koḻutta ūṭṭaṅ koṭuttuk kompu cīvik kūrākkuvar. āṇṭu tōṟum, kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, maṇapparuvamaṭinta maṅkiyark kuriya kāḷikaḷiyellām oru toḻuvattil aṭittu vittu, ovvoṉṟākat tiṟantu viṭuvar. makkaḷ āravāratti yum ēṟukōṭpaṟi muḻakkattiyum, kaṇṭum kēṭṭum, maruṇṭōṭum ovvoru kollēṟṟiyum, māṇiyarāṉa āya iḷiñar piṭittu niṟutta muyalvar. palar kollēṟukaḷāṟ kuttik kollappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. oru kollēṟṟi evaṉ piṭittaṭakki niṟuttukiṉṟāṉō avaṉ av ēṟṟiṟkuriya āya makaḷi maṇappāṉ. im maṇamuṟi, kalit toki eṉṉum caṅka nūlil, mullik kaliyil, virivākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṇṇaṉ ēḻ ēṟutaḻuvi nappiṉṉiyi maṇantatu it tamiḻ marapē.
iṅṅaṉam oru maṟacceyali maṇamakaṉatu takutiyākak koḷḷum vaḻakkattiṉāl, oru kulattāriṉ maṟat tiṟam mēṉmēlum vaḷarntu varuvatuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷum iyalpāka maṟavuṇarcci viñciya vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu, maṟañciṟanta āṭavariyē peṇṭir maṇakkum ēṟpāṭṭi, pāliyal terippu (Sexual Selection) eṉpar ṭārviṉ pēraṟiñar. oru kulattār tamakkuḷḷēyē neṭukalum maṇantu varuvatāl ēṟpaṭum eccaviyaṟ kuṟipāṭṭiṟku īṭu ceyvatu, ēṟutaḻuval pōṉṟa maṇamakaṉ maṟaviyal takuti ēṟpāṭē.
ēṟu taḻuval eṉṉum paṇṭi vaḻakkamē, iṉṟu kaḷḷar maṟavariṭic callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum, mañcuviraṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyar iv vaḻakkatti nīṇṭa kālamāka aṭiyōṭu viṭṭuviṭṭatiṉāl, tam muṉṉōriṉ maṟatti muṟṟum iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. callikkaṭṭu māṭṭut toḻuvi iṉṟum pāṭi allatu pāṭi vācal eṉṟaḻippatu, iv vaḻakkam paṇṭik kālattil āyarpāṭiyil nikaḻntatu eṉpati uṇarttum.
tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ ūrvalam varuvatum, cila ciṟṟūr kaḷil ayalūr maṇamakaṉ vantu oru peṇṇi maṇantu mīḷumpōtu, avvūr iḷiñar iḷavaṭṭak kācu eṉṉum kinnīṭṭam kēṭpatum, paṇṭikkālattil oru peṇṇi nōkkip pala iḷiñar pōṭṭiyiṭṭup porutatik kuṟippāy uṇarttum.
āṇmakap pēṟillāta peṟṟōr, tam marumakaṉit tam illattilēyē iruttik koḷvar. iv ēṟpāṭṭi illattam eṉpar kaṉṉaṭar.
(2) kātal maṇam
kātal maṇamāvatu, ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum peṟṟōrik kēḷātum piṟarukkut teriyātum oruvariyoruvar kātalittu, tāmē kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumākak kūṭik koḷvatu. itu, kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatum kaṟpil toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iru vakittu. kaḷaveṉpatu maṟivu; kaṟpeṉpatu veḷippaṭi nī iṉṉavāṟoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉa maṇamakaḷ tirumaṇa āciriyaṉāl kaṟpikkappaṭum nilimi kaṟpu eṉpar.
kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatu, irumāta ellikkuḷ eṉṟēṉum veḷippaṭṭuk kaṟpāka māṟiviṭum, ik kaḷavu veḷippāṭu, (1) uṭaṉ pōkku, (2) aṟattoṭu nili eṉṉum iruvaki kaḷuḷ oṉṟāl ēṟpaṭum. uṭaṉpōkkāvatu, kaḷavoḻukkam taṭippaṭṭaviṭattu allatu kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr avaḷit tara iciyāviṭattu, kātalaṉ avaḷi vēṟṟūrkkēṉum taṉ vīṭṭiṟkēṉum aḻittuk koṇṭu pōyviṭal. aṟattoṭu niliyāvatu, kātaliyiṉ melivu kaṇṭu atip pōkkuvataṟku, avaḷ peṟṟōr kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟikāri) vēlaṉ (mantirakkāraṉ) mutaliyōriṉ tuṇi vēṇṭumpōtō, ataṟku muṉṉa tākavō, kātali tāṉāka vēṉum taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavēṉum taṉ  katalaṉip paṟṟit terivittal.
kātalar oru nāḷuṅ kaḷavoḻukkamiṉṟik kaṟpākavē tam kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅkuvatumuṇṭu. iv iruvakit toṭakkamum, karaṇattōṭu kūṭiyatākavum irukkalām; kūṭāta tākavumirukkalām, ak karaṇamum, maṇamakaṉ tāṉē ceyvippatākavumirukkalām;  maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōrik koṇṭu ceyvippatākavumirukkalām.
iṅkuk kaṟpeṉpatu uṇmiyil kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ veḷip pāṭēyāyiṉum, atu potuvākak karaṇattoṭu kūṭiyatākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē.  (kaṟpiyal,1)
koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa.  (kaṟpiyal 2)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa kātalaṉ, taṉṉūr vēṟṟūrāyiṉ pōṉaviṭattum kātali yūrēyāyiṉum, perumpālum taṉ maṉiyilēyē ati vittuk koḷvaṉ, uṟṟārkkuriyar poṟṟoṭi makaḷir eṉpatālum.
uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum
ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpuc ciṟantaṉṟu
eṉpatālum (tol. kaḷa. 22), kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr kātalaṉi oppukkoṇṭu, avaṉ vatuvi maṇattit tam maṉiyil naṭattavirumpiṉ, av viruppam niṟivēṟuvatumuṇṭu, yār maṉiyil vatuvi nikaḻiṉum, vatuvikku muṉ maṇamakaḷ kālil avaḷ peṟṟōrāl aṇiyappaṭṭirunta cilampi nīkkutaṟku oru caṭaṅku ceyyappaṭum. atu cilampu kaḻi nōṉpu  eṉappaṭum.
iti,
nummaṉic cilampu kaḻīi ayariṉum
emmaṉi vatuvi naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻikeṉac
colliṉ evaṉō maṟṟē veṉvēl
miyaṟa viḷaṅkiya kaḻalaṭip
poyval kāḷiyi yīṉṟa tāykkē.
eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl (399) aṟiyalām.
itu, uṭaṉkoṇṭupōṉa kātalaṉ mīṇṭu vantu, taṉ kātaliyit taṉ illattiṟkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟa viṭattu, avaṉ tāy avaḷukkuc cilampu kaḻi nōṉpu ceykiṉṟāḷeṉak kēṭṭa naṟṟāy (peṟṟa tāy), aṅku niṉṟum vantārkkuc colliyatu.
(num-um. cilampu - taṇṭi. kaḻīi- kaḻittu. ayariṉum- koṇṭāṭiṉālum. kaḻikeṉa -  naṭakkaveṉṟu. evaṉō- eṉṉa. veṉ-veṟṟi. miyaṟa- kuṟṟam nīṅka, kaḻal- vīrakkālaṇi, kāḷi - vīraṉākiya kātalaṉ).
karaṇamiṉṟiyum kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka iru kātalar icintu vāḻakkūṭu māyiṉum, karaṇattoṭu toṭaṅkum illaṟamē ellārālum pōṟṟappaṭuvatām;  aḵtillākkāl, atu vippu eṉa iḻintōrālum tūṟṟappaṭuvatē.
kātalar vāḻvu kaḷavoḻukkattōṭu toṭaṅkiṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcciyum, allākkāl uḷḷappuṇarccimāttiriyum, kaṟpiṟkumuṉ peṟupavarāvār.
peṟṟōrum, piṟarum muṭittu vikkum tirumaṇattilum maṇamakkaḷ iruvarkkum kātaluṇṭākalāmeṉiṉum, kātal maṇameṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappeṟuvatu ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum tāmāka vāḻkki oppantañ ceytukoḷvatē.
maṭalēṟṟam
mutupaḻaṅkālattil, oru kaṭuṅkātalaṉ allatu kātaṟpittaṉ avaṉ kātaliyi maṇattaṟku avaḷ peṟṟōr iciyāviṭiṉ, avaḷip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum uyirccētattiṟ kiṭamāṉa oru vaṉmuṟiyik kiyāḷ vatuṇṭu. atu ikkālattuc cattiyākkirakameṉṉum pāṭukiṭappup pōṉṟatu.
maṭalēṟat tuṇinta kātalaṉ, nīrccīli oṉṟēyuṭuttu uṭampelāñ cāmpaṟ pūci erukkamāliyaṇintu, taṉ kātaliyiṉ ūr naṭuvē tavaniliyilamarntu, avaḷ uruvi varinta oru tuṇiyik kiyilēnti, ati uṟṟu nōkkiya vaṇṇamāy vāḷāviruppaṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭa avvūrār ‘nī āyvu (cōtaṉi) tarukiṉṟāyā?  eṉak kēṭpar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭiyāṟ ceyta oru poyk kutiriyiṉ mēl avaṉiyēṟṟit teru neṭuka iḻuttuc celvar. aṅṅaṉam iḻukkumpōtu, avaṉ uṭampiṟ karukkaṟuttuk kāyam paṭṭaviṭamellām veḷuttut tōṉṟiṉ, avaṉukku avaṉ kātaliyi maṇamuṭittu vippar;  allākkāl viyār. itu kātalar tāmākak kūṭuvataṉṟā yiṉum, orupuṭi yoppumi paṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kātaṟ pāṭṭukkaḷ
ivvulakiṟ ciṟantatu illaṟa iṉpam. atilum, kātalar nukarvatu kariyaṟṟatu. avar ōrūrarāyum oruvariyoruvar muṉṉaṟinta varāyum irukkavēṇṭumeṉṉum yāppuṟavilli karuṅ kaṭaluppiṟkum, karumali nārattikkum tontam ēṟpaṭuvatu pōl, neṭṭiṭippaṭṭa iruvar ōriṭattu oruvari 
yoruvar kaṇṭu kātalikkavum nērum. attaki nilimi vāynta oru kātalaṉ kūṟṟākavuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu:-
yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō
entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷir.
yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi aṟitum
cempulap peynnīr pōla
aṉpuṭi neñcam tāṅkalan taṉavē.  (kuṟun, 40),
(yāy-eṉ tāy, ñāy- uṉ tāy, enti- eṉ tanti. nunti- uṉ tanti, kēḷir- uṟaviṉar. cempulap peynnīr pōla- civanta nilattiṟ cērnta nīrum civappatupōla.)
iṉi, oru kātali kūṟṟāka vuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu:-
irāmaḻi peyta īra īrattuḷ
paṉinukaṅ koṇṭu yāṉiyēr pūṭṭi
veḷḷi vitittup poṉṉē viḷiyiṉum
vēṇṭēṉ piṟantakat tīṇṭiya vāḻvē
ceṅkēḻ varakup pacuṅkatir koytu
kaṉṟu kāttuk kuṉṟil uṇakki
ūṭupatar pōkkimuṉ utaviṉōrk kutavik
kāṭukaḻi intaṉam pāṭupārt teṭuttuk
kuppik kīri uppiṉṟi ventati
iraval tālam parivuṭaṉ vāṅkic
cōṟatu koṇṭu pīṟal aṭittē
oṉṟuviṭ ṭorunāḷ tiṉṟu kiṭappiṉum
naṉṟē tōḻinam kaṇavaṉ vāḻvē.
(nukam-nukattaṭi-īṇṭiya- celvamikunta. uṇakki- kāyavittu. intaṉam- viṟaku. pāṭu- viḻutal. tālam- uṇkalam. parivu- varuttam. pīṟal - cēlikkiḻivu.
3. kavarvu maṇam
kavarvu maṇamāvatu, oru peṇṇip peṟṟōricivum avaḷ icivumiṉṟi, valintu paṟṟutal.
atu, ūrukku veḷiyē taṉittu niṟkum ōr iḷam peṇṇi, vēṟṟūrāṉ oruvaṉ valintu paṟṟik koṇṭu pōy, avaḷi maṉiviyākak koḷvatu pōṉṟatu.
oru maṟakkuṭip peṇṇi maṇakka virumpiya vēntaṉ, ak kuṭiyār ataṟkiciyāviṭattu, perum paṭiyoṭu ceṉṟu avarōṭu pōr purivatuṇṭu. am maṟavar tam kuṭiyiṉ māṉattik kāttaṟku añcātu etirppar. itu makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum.
nikarttumēl vanta vēntaṉoṭu mutukuṭi
makaṭpā ṭañciya makaṭpā lāṉum.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu ituvē (puṟattiṇiyiyal, 24). itaṉiyē, maṟam eṉṉuṅ kalampaka vuṟuppu, aracaṉ peṇ kēṭṭu viṭutta tirumukattik koṇarnta tūtaṉi nōkki, maṟavar eccarittum accuṟuttiyum viṭuppatākak kūṟum.
oru maṇappeṇṇiṉ aḻakip paḻikkumpōtu, avaḷ eṉṉa oru periya ciṟiyā? eṉṟu peṇṭir kūṟum vaḻakkam, oru kālattiṟ pēraḻakuṭiya peṇkaḷ valiyōrālum aracarālum ciṟi piṭikkappaṭṭamiyi uṇarttum.
ōr aracaṉ, taṉ pakiyaracaṉi veṉṟu allatu koṉṟu, avaṉuṭiya tēviyaric ciṟi piṭittu vantu vēḷam eṉṉum ciṟiccāliyil iṭṭu avamāṉappaṭuttuvatu, kavarvu maṇattiṉ pāṟpaṭātu.
2. kulamuṟi paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭimuṟit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, kulamuṟi paṟṟi akamaṇam (Endogamy), puṟamaṇam (Exogamy) eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
akamaṇamāvatu, oru kulattār tam kulattiṟkuḷḷēyē maṇattal;  puṟamaṇamāvatu oru kulap pirivār tam piriviṟkuḷ maṇavātu vēṟoru pirivil maṇattal. 
iṉṟuḷḷa kulaṅkaḷuḷ, kalappuk kulaṅkaḷ tavira ēṉiya vellām akamaṇattaṉavē. tiṇimayakkam ēṟpaṭumuṉ kuṟavar, āyar, vēṭṭuvar, uḻavar, nuḷiyar (cempaṭavar) eṉat tamiḻar aintiṇi makkaḷāy vevvēṟu nilattil vāḻntapōtu, avarkkuḷ perumpāl vaḻakkamāy nikaḻntatu akamaṇamē.
kulap pirivukaḷ, nāṭu, ūr, kulam, kūṭṭam, kiḷi, vakuppu, illam, kari mutaliyaṉavākap palvēṟu tiṟappaṭum.
nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum
piṟappum ciṟappum
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1060).
cēlam māvaṭṭattuc cērvarāyaṉmali paccimali kolli mali  mutaliya malikaḷil vāḻum tamiḻ maliyāḷik kulattil, (1) periya maliyāḷi, (2) pacci maliyāḷi, (3) kolli maliyāḷi eṉṉum mūṉṟum uṭkulaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, kolli maliyāḷi eṉṉum uṭkulattil (1) munnāṭṭu maliyāḷi, 
(2) nālnāṭṭu maliyāḷi, (3) añcūr maliyāḷi eṉṉum nāṭṭut tokutiyil, (1) mayilam (2) tiruppuli, (3) iṭappuli, (4) piṟakari, (5) ciṟṟūr eṉṉum aintum nāṭukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, ciṟṟūr eṉṉum nāṭṭil, (1) pīlaṉ, (2) mūkkāṇṭi, (3) pūcaṉ,(4) māṇikkaṉ, (5) tiruvicci, (6) kaṇṇaṉ (7) tillāṉ eṉṉum ēḻum vakuppukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉ aintum oru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ; piṉ iraṇṭum maṟṟoru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ, iv iru tokutikaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum aṭutta tokutiyuḷaṉṟit taṉ tokutiyuḷ maṇappatilli. itu puṟamaṇamām.
koṅkavēḷāḷar kulattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷāṉa, tūraṅkulam cempōttaṅkulam āntikkulam mutaliya irupattiraṇṭu kulaṅkaḷum, puṟamaṇattaṉavām. ik kulaṅkaḷ kūṭṭameṉavum paṭum.
orē kulattuḷ toṭarntu maṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟuvatāl viḷiyum kēṭṭi vilakkutaṟkē, kulappirivukaṭkup puṟamaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
aracar piṟakulattil tām peṇkoṇṭālum, tam makaḷirip piṟakulattārk kuk koṭuppatilli, itu uyarmaṇattiṉ (Hypergamy) pāṟpaṭum.
3. maṇamakkaḷ toki paṟṟiyatu
maṇamakkaḷ tokipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ (1) oru maṉiyam (Monogamy) (2) palmaṉiyam (Polygamy), (3) palkaṇavam (Polyandry) eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
oru kālattil orē maṉiviyuṭimi orumaṉiyam;  orē kālattil palamaṉiviyaruṭimi palmaṉiyam;  orē kālattil pala kaṇavaruṭimi palkaṇavam.
paṇṭik kālattil, pala aracarum talivarum, palmaṉiyat tit taḻuviyatuṭaṉ potu makaḷirākiya parattiyaruṭaṉum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar. parattiyaruḷ, vēṟāka ōr illattil iruppavaḷ iṟparattiyeṉṟum, parattiyar cēriyil iruppavaḷ cēripparattiyeṉṟum, cēripparatti yaruḷ ciṟantu kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kātaṟparattiyeṉṟum collappaṭuvar.
parattiyar pōṉṟē potu makaḷirāyuḷḷa iṉṉoru vakuppār kaṇikiyar (kūttiyar) eṉpār. kālaṅ kaṇittāṭupavar kaṇikiyar. avar nāṭakak kaṇikiyar kōyiṟ kaṇikiyar eṉa irucārār.
iṉi, potu makaḷillātu, karaṇamiṉṟi maṉiviyar pōl vittuk koḷḷappaṭum vippu eṉṉum makaḷirumuṇṭu. avar kaṉṉiyarākavō kaṭṭuppaṭṭavarākavō kimpeṇṭi rākavō irukkalām.
paṇṭit tamiḻar potuvākap paṇpāṭṭilum paltuṟip paṭṭa nākarikattilum ciṟantiruntārēṉum, peṇṇiṉpat tuṟiyil perumpālum nekiḻnta neṟiyuṭiyarākavēyirun taṉar. aracar kaṭṭumaṭṭiṉṟic ciṟṟiṉpatti nukarntu vanta tiṉālēyē, ulā, maṭal, kātal mutaliya akapporuṭ paṉuval kaḷum (pirapantaṅkaḷ) parattiyiṟ pirivu eṉṉum kōvik kiḷavik kottum iṭikkālat teḻuntaṉa. mēṉāṭṭār in nāvalan tēyattiṟku vanta piṉpē, in neṟitiṟampiya nili tiruntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
palkaṇavam peṇ pañcattālum peṇkoliyālum ēṟpaṭuvatu. atu iṉṟu nīlamalit totuvariṭiyē uḷḷatu.
4. maṇamakaḷ nili paṟṟiyatu
maṇamakaḷ nilipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, (1) kaṉṉi maṇam, (2) kaṭṭup paṭṭavaḷ maṇam, (3) kimpeṇ maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kaṉṉi maṇam, oru kaṉṉip peṇṇip putitāka maṇattal;  kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇam, orumuṟi maṇakkappaṭṭut tīrappaṭ ṭavaḷi maṇattal;  kimpeṇ maṇam, kaṇavaṉi yiḻanta vaḷi maṇattal. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉavi yiraṇṭum iḻintōr maṇamākavum iṭittarattōr maṇamākavum iruntu vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
orumuṟi maṇakkappaṭṭavaḷi maṟumaṇañ ceytal ciṟap pillātatākak karutappaṭuvatāl, kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇattil maṇamuḻā (kaliyāṇa mēḷam) iṉṟiyē tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. atu kaṭṭuttāli yeṉṟum, maṇamuḻavu uḷḷatu koṭṭut tirumaṇam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. kimpeṇ maṇam perumpālum kaṭṭuttāliyākavē yirukkum.
(2) ilakkaṇap pākupāṭu
iṉi, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, maṇamakkaḷiṉ kātal nili paṟṟit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷi. (1) kikkiḷi, (2) aṉpiṉ aintiṇi, (3) peruntiṇi eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟum.
kikkiḷiyāvatu, āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvaruḷḷum oruvarukkē kātal iruppatu; aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal iruppatu;  peruntiṇiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal illātiruppatu allatu oruvari yoruvar valintu koḷvatu. im mūṉṟum, muṟiyē orutalik kāmam, irutalik kāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉṟum collappeṟum. ki eṉpatu pakkam. kiḷi eṉpatu nēyam. ākavē kikkiḷi eṉpatu orutalik kātal, aintiṉi eṉpaṉa, kuṟiñci, mulli, pāli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum ainnilaṅkaḷ. kuṟiñci maliyum malicārnta iṭamum; mulli maliyaṟṟa ceṭiṅkāṭum pulveḷiyum; pāli nīrum niḻalumiṉṟi vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭa iṭam;  marutam nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum oruṅkēyuḷḷa vayalnāṭu; neytal kaṭalum kaṭaṟ kariyum. iv ainnilaṅkaṭkum potuvākak koḷḷappaṭṭa tiṉāl, irutalik kātal aṉpiṉ aintiṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
peruntiṇi perumpakuti makkaḷ maṇamuṟikaḷuṭ perum pālaṉavaṟṟit taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟpatāl, poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu. kavarvu maṇaṅkaḷum, iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa ellāp puṇarccivakikaḷum, peruntiṇiyē, kātaloṭu poruntāmiyum neṟiyoṭu poruntāmiyum paṟṟi, peruntiṇi poruntāk kāmam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kikkiḷikkum peruntiṇikkum nilamillātapaṭi, aivaki nilaṅkaḷiyum naṭunāyakamāṉa aṉpiṉ aintiṇikkē urimi yākkiviṭṭataṉāl, makkaṭku nalam payavāta ēṉi yiru maṇa muṟikaḷum in nilavulakattillātu nīṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, muṉṉit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāriṉ karuttākum.
maṇamakkaḷiṉ icivik kēḷātu peṟṟōrē muṭittu vikkum tirumaṇaṅkaḷil, maṇamakaṉukku allatu maṇa makaḷukku maṭṭum kātal iruppiṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamām;  iruvarukkum iruppiṉ, atu aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyām;  oruvarukkum illāviṭiṉ, peruntiṇiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatām.
maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷ oruvarukku maṭṭum kātal irukkum pōtu, ēṉiyavarum etirppiṉṟi icintiruntāltāṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamākum. allākkāl atu peruntiṇiyē. orutalik kāmam eṉpatu kātaliṉṟiyum icintuvarum peṇṇi āṭavaṉum āṭavaṉip peṇṇum maṇappatē yaṉṟi, iciyāp peṇṇiyum āṭavaṉiyum valitil maṇappataṉṟu.
orutalik kāmam eṉṉum muṟiyil kikkiḷiyum, peruntiṇiyum okkumēṉum, muṉṉatu neṟippaṭṭateṉṟum, piṉṉatu neṟi tiṟampiyateṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. 
kāmavuṇarcciyillāc ciṟumiyiṭattum, kāmavuṇarcci yiruntum taṉmēṟ kātal koḷḷāp paruvap peṇṇiṭattum, oruvaṉ kātal moḻikaḷik kūṟi iṉpuṟuvatu kikkiḷik kuṟip pēyaṉṟik kikkiḷi maṇamākātu.
irutaliyuṅ kātaluḷḷaviṭattum, mutiyāḷoṭu kūṭutalum, kūṭṭa iṭiyīṭu pāṭṭāl ēṟpaṭum kāmap pittamum kaḻi neñcaḻivum, peruntiṇiyeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl;  viṇṇiṉpatti yottatoru peṇṇiṉpatti maṇṇula kōrkku vakuttaṉar muṉṉit tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar eṉka.
ittakiya iṉpam mika mika aritāy oru cilarkkē kiṭṭuvatāyiṉum, ati ōr aḷaviyākak koṇṭu kūṟiyatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum.
2. maṇattoki
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaṭku ittuṇi eṉṉun toki varampilli. maṇapparuvam maṇamakaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇ ṭeṉṟum, maṇamakaḷukkup paṉṉīrāṇ ṭeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum. paṇṭik kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ ettuṇi valimi mikkavarāy iruntiruppiṉum, nūlkaḷil maṇamakkaṭkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa paruvam muntiyatē. akkālattil tirumaṇac caṭṭa varampiṉmiyālum, iṉpac ciṟappoṉṟiyē makkaḷ karutiyatālum, iḷami mutal mutumi vari ettuṇi maṇañ ceyya muṭiyumō attuṇi maṇañ ceytaṟku aṉṟu iṭamiruntatu. āyiṉum cila aracarum kīḻōrumē inniliyi mikutiyāyp payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. peṇṭirkkuk kāmanukarccip paruvam paṉṉīrāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭuvari eṉpatu nūṉ marapu.
peṇṇiṉ aintāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭu varippaṭṭa paruvattip piṉvarumāṟu eḻuniliyāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ vakuttuk kūṟum.
(1) pēti 5-7 āṇṭu.
(2) petumpi 8-11 āṇṭu.
(3) maṅki 12-13 āṇṭu.
(4) maṭanti 14-19 āṇṭu.
(5) arivi 20-25 āṇṭu.
(6) terivi 26-31 āṇṭu.
(7) pēriḷampeṇ 32-40 āṇṭu.
aracarkkuc celvacciṟappum, kīḻōrukkut tīrvi muṟiyum maṉiviyar uḻippum, palmaṇañ ceytaṟkut tuṇiyā yiruntaṉa.
akkālattaracar kaṇkaṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷātaliṉ, avar paṉ maṇañ ceytalit taṭuppār evarumilar. ātalāl, avar vaḻimuṟi maṇaṅkaṭkuk kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭa vēṇṭuvatilli. kīḻōr, nōyum mātappūppum caṇṭiyumpōlac ciṟu kūṭṭattaṭi ēṟpaṭiṉum, atik kāraṇamākak kāṭṭi maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷvar. mutal maṉivikku ōrāṇṭu piḷḷiyillāviṭiṉ, uṭaṉē maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷvatu cilar iyalpu. cilar, piḷḷiyiruppiṉum, irupālum illi yeṉṟum palarilliyeṉṟum collik koṇṭu, maṟumaṇañ ceyvatuṇṭu. itu iṉṟum toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu.
3. maṇa naṭimuṟi
paṇṭittamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu naṭantu vantaṉa.
(1) maṇappēccu
potuvāka, ōr iḷiñaṉukkup patiṉeṭṭāṇṭu nirampiya piṉ, avaṉ peṟṟōr avaṉ kāmak kuṟippaṟintō tāmākak karutiyō, perumpālum marapuppaṭi otta kulattil oru peṇṇip pēci maṇañ ceytu vippar.
ammāṉ makaḷum atti makaḷumpōla muṟikārap peṇṇāyiṉ urimippeṇ eṉṟum, vēṟu celvar vīṭṭup peṇṇāyiṉ perumip peṇ eṉṟum aḻippatu vaḻakkam. urimip peṇ illāviṭattum avaḷi maṇavāviṭattum, ayalilēyē maṇam pēcap peṟum.
maṇappēccu muṉkālattil oru tolliyāṉa kaliyāka iruntu vantatu. maṇamakaṉ peṟṟōr, maṇakkattakka oru peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōriṭam, vaḷḷuvaṉ allatu kaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, periyōri viṭuppar. peṟṟōrum uṭaṉ celvatuṇṭu. peṇ pēcac cellumpōtu, kuṟi (cakuṉam), naṟcol (vāyppuḷ), puḷ (paṟavi nimittam) mutaliyaṉa pārttal vaḻakkam.
peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr tam makaḷit tara icintapiṉ maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaṉukkum peṇṇukkumuḷḷa poruttaṅ kaḷik kaṇiya  (cōtiṭa) muṟippaṭi palvēṟu vakiyiṟ pārppar. apporuttaṅkaḷ, potuvāka.
(1) nāḷ (naṭcattiram)
(2) kaṇam
(3) eṇṇikki (mākēntiram)
(4) peṇ nīṭci (tirī tīrkkam)
(5) piṟavi (yōṉi)
(6) ōri (irāci)
(7) kōḷ (irāciyatipati)
(8) vaciyam
(9) iṇakkam (vēti)
(10) caraṭu (iraccu)
eṉap pattu vakippaṭum. ip pattu vakiyum oruṅkē perumpālum poruntu vatilli. ataṉāl, palvēṟu peṇṇi nāṭip palvēṟu ūrkkuc celvatu vaḻakkam, itu mikunta aliccaliyum kālak kaṭappiyum uṇṭu paṇṇum, itaṉālēyē, oru maṇattiṟku ēḻu cerupput tēya vēṇṭum  eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. iṉi, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉ poruttattuṭaṉ, vāḻnāḷ, kuṟippu (pāvakam), maram, puḷ, kulam eṉṉum aimporuttam pārppatuṇṭu.
(2) maṇavuṟuti (niccayārttam)
yārēṉum oru peṇṇiṭam patiṉ poruttamum amintirup piṉ, allatu amintiruppatākac collappaṭiṉ, uṟuppuk kuṟiyum nōyk kuṟṟamum illāviṭattu, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum peṇ vīṭṭārum oru naṉṉāḷil peṇ vīṭṭil palaraṟiya maṇavuṟuti ceytu koḷvar. avvuṟutic caṭaṅki uṟuti veṟṟili (niccaya tāmpūlam) eṉṟum aḻikkalām.
maṇavuṟutic caṭaṅkil, veṟṟili pākku, kuṅkumam, mañcaḷ mutaliyaṉa vikkappaṭṭa oru taṭṭil, peṇ vīṭṭār kēṭṭa paricap paṇamum, peṇṇiṟku oru kūṟiyum vikkappeṟum iru vīṭṭārum maṇappeṇṇiṟku aṇiyavēṇṭiya aṇikaḷum, tirumaṇac celavil ēṟṟuk koḷḷavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷum, aṉṟē pēci muṭivu ceyyappaṭum.
cila camayaṅkaḷil, maṇamakaṉ kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum, maṇamakaḷ kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭārum, maṟittuvippatumuṇṭu. tirumaṇam iruvarkku vāḻvi yuṇṭupaṇṇum maṅkala nikaḻcciyātalāl, aṅṅaṉam maṟittuvippōr āyiram poy colli oru tirumaṇam ceytuvi (oru viḷakkēṟṟivi) eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyic collit tammit tēṟṟik koḷvar.
3. maṇaviḻā i.
(muṉṉikaḻccikaḷ)
maṇavuṟutiyāṉa piṉ, maṇattiṟkuriya cittiri, āṉi pōṉṟa nalla mātattil, tiṅkaḷ, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) pōṉṟa nalla kiḻamiyil, 2-āvatu 7-āvatu pōṉṟa nalla pakkattil (titiyil), puravi (acupati) cakaṭu (urōkaṇi) pōṉṟa nalla nāḷil (naṭcattirattil), nalla ōriyil (ilakkiṉattil) amiyum maṅkala muḻuttam (mukūrttam) kuṟikkap peṟum. ataṉpiṉ, maṇacceyti kālamum iṭamuṅkuṟittu, uṟṟār uṟaviṉar aṉivarkkum pākku vittuc collappeṟum.
maṇamaṉi, kulamarapuppaṭi, maṇamakaṉ vīṭākavō maṇamakaḷ vīṭākavō irukkum.  potuvāy, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭipeṟum.
maṇamaṉiyil, maṇanāḷukku muṉ 3, ,5, 7, 9 ākiya oṟṟitta nāṭkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, nalvēḷiyil muḻuttakkāl nāṭṭip pantal allatu koṭṭaki pōṭappeṟum. pantal alaṅ karippu avaravar celva niliyip poṟuttatu. pantalil vāḻi kamuku kūntaṟpaṉi maṭal mutaliyaṉa kaṭṭappeṟu vatuṇṭu. ettuṇi yeḷiyarāyiṉum vāḻi maram tappātu kaṭṭappeṟum. atu maṇamakkaḷ marapu kuñcuṅ kuḻuvāṉumāy vaḻivaḻi vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ kuṟippiyuṭiyatākak karutap peṟuvatu.
pantal naṭuvil allatu ataṉ oru kōṭiyil maṇavaṟi amikkappaṭum. ataṉ kāloṉṟil aracaṅkompu kaṭṭap peṟum. ati aracāṇikkāl eṉpar. paṇṭikkālattil aracaṉ teyvamākak karutappaṭṭatiṉālum, maṉṉaṉ uyirttē malartali yulakam eṉpatiṉālum, aracaṉik kuṟittaṟku aracaṅ kompi maṇavaṟiyil naṭṭi, ati araca āṇikkāl eṉṟu aḻittatākat terikiṉṟatu. maṇavaṟiyi aracāṇi mēṭi eṉpatumuṇṭu.
maṇavaṟiyil, pū, mañcaḷ, kuṅkumam, cantaṉam, arici, aṟuku, veṟṟili pākku, tēṅkāy, muḷippāliki, viḷakku, niṟikumpam, kōlappāṉi mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷ vikkappeṟum, kōlap pāṉiyi āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉi yeṉṟum, aracāṇip pāṉiyeṉṟum āyirattāḻiyeṉṟuñ colvatuṇṭu. maṇamakkaḷ kuṟiviṉṟi maṅkalamāy nīṭūḻi vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉpatē, maṇavaṟiyil maṅkalap poruḷkaḷi vippatiṉ karuttu. viḷakku teyvac ciṉṉam
maṇaviṉikaḷ toṭaṅkumuṉpē, maṇamakaṉukkum maṇa makaḷukkum, muṟiyē, valakkiyilum iṭakkiyilum kāppu nāṇ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum, maṇaviḻā muṭiyum vari maṇamakkaṭkup pēyālum piṟavaṟṟālum evvakit tīṅkum nērak kūṭāteṉpatē, ataṉ nōkkam. kāppu tīṅku varāmaṟ kāttal. mutaṟkālattil kuḷicampōṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa kāppu nāṇē, piṟkālattil appeyaruḷḷa aṇiyāka māṟiṟṟu.
maṇa nāḷil maṇa maṉiyil maṅkala maṇamuḻā muḻaṅkum, uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum, maṇappantaṟkīḻ vantamarvar. tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ muḻutta vēḷiyil maṇavaṟiyil vantamarvāṉ. avaṉ tamiḻap pārppāṉākavō. kulat talivaṉākavō iruppāṉ. maṇamakkaḷum nīrāṭi mañcaḷ tōytta allatu tōyāta puttāṭiyaṇintu, maṇamakaṉ muṉṉum maṇamakaḷ piṉṉumāka, taṉittaṉiyāka maṇavaṟikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭuvar. maṇamaṉi maṇamakaṉ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaḷum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaṉum, vēṟōr maṉiyiliruntu mēḷa tāḷattuṭaṉ aḻittu varappeṟuvar.
ii. karaṇam
tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ teyva vaḻipāṭāṟṟi, maṇamakkaḷ peyarum maṇamum viḷampi, viḷakkuc cāṉṟāka avarkaḷic cūḷiṭuvittu, kuṅkumam mañcaḷuṭaṉ taṭṭiliṭṭuc cūṭaṅ koḷuttappaṭṭup periyōrāl vāḻttap peṟṟa tāliyi eṭuttu, maṇamakaṉ kiyil koṭukka, avaṉ ati vāṅkip peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvaṉ, aṉṟu muḻavu iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅkum. maṇamakkaḷ mēl malar māri poḻiyum. ataṉpiṉ maṇamakkaḷ māli māṟṟik koḷvar. periyōr aṟukum ariciyum maṇamakkaḷ mēl iṭṭu attipōl tuḷirttu, ālpōṟ paṭarntu, (taḻittu) aracupōl ōṅki, aṟuku pōl vēruṉṟi, mūṅkil pōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr. eṉṟum, pakavaṉaruḷāl pāṅkāyiruntu patiṉāṟum peṟṟup pallāṇṭu vāḻka. eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum vāḻttuvar. itu aṟukiṭal eṉappaṭum.
iii. piṉṉikaḻccikaḷ
tirumaṇattiṟku vantōrkkellām tēṅkāy paḻamum pākku veṟṟiliyum cantaṉamum pakirappeṟum. maṅkala makaḷirum ciṟumiyarum avaṟṟoṭu malarum peṟuvar. ataṉpiṉ tirumaṇa avi kaliyum. āyiṉ, uṟṟār uṟaviṉarellām aṅkēyē yiruntu maṇavīṭṭārkku moyyum maṇamakkaṭku naṉkoṭi yum vaḻaṅkuvar. moyyellām ōliyil eḻutappeṟum. maṇamakkaḷ periyōr kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkuvar.
aṉṟu māli allatu iravu, ūrvalam nikaḻum. maṇamakkaḷ avvakkula marapuppaṭi tērilō, pallakkilō, yāṉi kutiri mītō valam varuvar, ūrvalam muṭintu vīṭu cērntavuṭaṉ, tēṅkāyuṭittum, mañcaḷ nīrāl maṇamakkaḷ pātaṅkaḷik kaḻuviyum, ālatti yeṭuttum, kaṇṇeccil (kaṇ tiruṣṭi) kaḻikkappaṭum.
tirumaṇa viruntu akkālattil vēḷikkaṇakkā yirātu, nāṭkaṇakkā yirukkum. eḷiyar oru nāḷum, celvar ēḻunāḷ variyum, viruntaḷippar.
maṇamakkaḷ paruvam vantōrāyiṉ aṉṟē kūṭṭappeṟuvar.
maṇaviḻā muṭintavuṭaṉ, maṇamakkaṭkuk kāppuk kaḻaṟṟappaṭum.
maṇamāṉapiṉ, 3ām, 5ām, 7ām, 9ām, nāṭkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, maṇamakkaḷ, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭiṟkum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiṟkumāka vīṭu māṟuvar. itu maṟuvīṭu (allatu maruvīṭu) pōtal eṉappaṭum. maṟuvīṭu maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ, aṅku maṇamakaṉukkuc ceyyappaṭum viruntu, maru allatu maruvu eṉap peyar peṟum.
maṉiyaṟam
maṇaviṉikaḷellām muṭintapiṉ, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki pōlum celvakkuṭi maṇamakkaḷi illaṟattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷellalām iṭṭu nirappappeṟṟa oru taṉimaṉiyil iruttuvatu marapu. atu maṉiyaṟam paṭuttal eṉappaṭum.
ciṉṉāṭ ceṉṟapiṉ, maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōr maṇamakaṉ vīṭu ceṉṟu, tam makaḷ maṉiyaṟam naṭattum tiṟattip pārvi yiṭuvatuṇṭu. atu vīṭu pārttal eṉappaṭum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉa ellārkkum potuvāṉa paruviṉic caṭaṅkukaḷ. iṉi, avvak kula marapiṟkēṟpac ciṟappāka naṭi peṟum nuṇviṉic caṭaṅkukaḷ ettaṉiyō pala. virivañci avi viṭukkappaṭṭuḷa.
paṇṭikkāla vatuvimaṇam paṟṟiya iru pāṭṭukkaḷ akam. 86, 136.
itaṉ poruḷ -
uḻuttam paruppoṭu cērttuc camitta koḻumiyāṉa kuḻinta poṅkalōṭu periya cōṟṟut tiraḷiyi uṇṭal iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻa, variciyāṉa kālkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaṟkīḻk koṇṭu vantu koṭṭiya maṇalip parappi vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi, mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu,
tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkiya vaḷinta veṇṇilā vik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiyuṭiya cakaṭam (urōkaṇi) eṉṉum nāḷ aṭiya, mikunta iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟkāliyil, uccantaliyiṟ kuṭattiyum kiyil putiya akaṉṟa montiyiyum uṭiya, maṇañceytu vikkum āravāramuḷḷa mutiya maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟi yum piṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum muṟippaṭi eṭutteṭuttuk koṭukka,
makaṉip peṟṟa tēmaluḷḷa aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉiyum tūya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭi niṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum tavaṟātu pala naṟpēṟukaḷippeṟṟu, uṉ kaṇavaṉ virumpip pēṇum viruppattiṟ kiṭamākuka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīrōṭu cērttup peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ, aṭarnta kariya kūntalil nellōṭu viḷaṅka.
17. nalla vatuvi maṇam muṭinta piṉpu.
cuṟṟattār āravāra ōciyuṭaṉ virintu vantu, periya maṉikkiḻattiyāvāy eṉṟu collic cērttu vikka, ōr aṟiyil uṭaṉ kūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil,
mutuki vaḷittuk kōṭip puṭavikkuḷ oṭuṅkik kiṭanta pakkattic cārntu, kaṭṭiyaṇikkum viruppattuṭaṉ mukatti mūṭiyirunta āṭiyi vilakka, avaḷ accattōṭu mūccuviṭṭapōtu, uṉ uḷḷam niṉittati oḷiyātu col eṉṟu piṉpu yāṉ kēṭṭatiṉāl, iṉiya makiḻcciyōṭu irukkiyil,
māṉiṉ maṭattōṭu cerukkiyuṅkoṇṭa pārviyi yum oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntaliyum uṭiya, am māniṟatti yuṭiyavaḷ, civanta maṇikaḷ patitta viḷakkamāṉa tiraṇṭa kātaṇikaḷ kātil aciya, uḷḷam niṟinta makiḻcciyaḷāki mukattit tāḻtti eṉṉi virintu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
neñcē kuṟṟamaṟap paruppuṭaṉ kalantu ākkiya neymikunta veṇ cōṟṟi, nīṅkāta īkit taṉmiyuṭaṉ uyarnta cuṟṟattār mutaliyōri uṇpittu, puṭkuṟi iṉitākak kūṭa, teḷḷiya oḷiyiyuṭiya aḻakiya iṭamakaṉṟa periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamaṟa viḷaṅka, tiṅkaḷic cakaṭam kūṭiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉṉāḷil, maṇamaṉiyi aḻakupaṭuttik kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭu, maṇamēḷattuṭaṉ periya muracam muḻaṅka, talivikku maṇa nīrāṭṭiya makaḷir, tam kūriya kaṇkaḷāl imiyātu nōkki virintu maṟiya,
melliya pūviyuṭiya vākiyiṉ aḻakaṟṟa piṉ puṟattiyuṭiya kavarnta iliyi, mutiya kaṉṟu kaṟitta paḷḷattiṟ paṭarnta aṟukiṉ, iṭi muḻaṅkiya vāṉattu mutaṉ maḻikku arumpiya kaḻuviya nīlamaṇi pōlum kariya itaḻiyuṭiya pāviyotta kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṇamuḷḷa arumpuṭaṉ, cērttuk kaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūlic cūṭṭi, tūya puttāṭiyāṟ poliyac ceytu, viruppattuṭaṉ kūṭi, maḻiyōci pōṉṟa maṇa vōci mikunta pantalil aṇikaḷi mikutiyāy aṇintirun tatiṉāl uṇṭāṉa viyarviyi viciṟiyāl āṟṟi, avaḷ cuṟṟattār avaḷi namakkut tanta mutal nāḷ iravil,
veṟuttalillāta kaṟpōṭu kūṭi eṉ uyirukku uṭampāka aṭuttavaḷ, kacaṅkāta putuppōrviyit taṉ uṭal muḻutum pōrttik koṇṭatāl, mikunta puḻukkattiyaṭinta uṉ piṟi pōṉṟa neṟṟiyil arumpiya viyarviyi, mikunta kāṟṟup pōkkumāṟu, ciṟitu tiṟa eṉṟu aṉpumikka neñcat tōṭu pōrviyik kavaravē, uṟiyiṉiṉṟu uruviya vāḷip pōl avaḷ uruvam veḷippaṭṭu viḷaṅka, ati maṟikkum vaki aṟiyātavaḷāki, virintu nāṇi viruppattōṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
paṇṭit tamiḻmaṇam : iṭikkāla māṟutalkaḷ
pirāmaṇar peruntokiyiṉarāyt tamiḻakam vantu cērnta piṉ, tamiḻar tirumaṇaṅkaḷiṟ pala māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
1. pirāmaṇap purōkitamum vaṭamoḻik karaṇamum
pirāmaṇar tēvarvaḻi vanta nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum; avar muṉṉōr moḻiyākiya vētamoḻiyum ataṉoṭu vētakāla intiya vaṭṭāramoḻikaḷākiya pirākirutaṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutamum, tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum; iru perun tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ kaṭiccaṅkak kālattilēyē aracar uḷḷattil āḻa vērūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, piṟkālattil pirāmaṇap purōkitam tamiḻakattil virintu paravuvataṟku mikunta vacatiyāyiruntatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutiyum, cōḻaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum, cēraṉ palyāṉic cel keḻukuṭṭuvaṉum pōṉṟa tamiḻ vēntarum piṟa ciṟṟaracarum, pirāmaṇari nilattēvariṉum mēlākat tēvareṉṟē koṇṭu, avar coṉṉatiyellām nampi avar ēviyatiyellām iyaṟṟi, vaṭamoḻi mantiram variyiṟanta valimiyuḷḷa teṉṟum, iṟivaṉ cevikku elliyaṟṟa iṉpan taruva teṉṟum, āriya vēḷvikaḷellām tappātu oruvaṉi uyarkatikkuc celuttum eṉṟum, teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa vaṭamoḻi mantirattit teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa pirāmaṇaṉē ōtavallāṉeṉṟum, piṟar ati ōtiṉāl ataṉ āṟṟal keṭumeṉṟum, tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōrmaṭṭum atik kātāṟ kēṭkavum ataṉāṟ payaṉpeṟavun takkavar eṉṟum, tamiḻ makkaḷ nampumāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. maṉṉaṉ eppaṭi maṉṉuyir appaṭi aṉṟō!
pirāmaṇar mutalāvatu ciṟu tokiyiṉarā yiruntatiṉāl, aracariṭattumaṭṭum āriyak karaṇamum caṭaṅkum āṟṟivantaṉar. piṉpu caṟṟut toki mikkapiṉ, peruñcelvarāṉa peru vaṇikarkkum āṟṟivantaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, toki mikamikac ciṟuvaṇikarkkum uyar vēḷāḷarkkum āṟṟat talippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, perumpālum ovvoru pērūrilum oru pirāmaṇar kuṭiyamara allatu amarttappaṭa nērntatu. ōr ūrkkup potuvāyiruntu avvūriluḷḷa uyarntōr caṭaṅkukaḷi yellām āṟṟi vanta pirāmaṇaṉ, ūrppārppāṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ. pirāmaṇaṉ kuṭiyirāta ūrc caṭaṅkukaḷi, aṭuttavūrp pārppāṉ vantu ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
āriyakkaraṇattāl viḷinta tīmikaḷ
(1) tamiḻukkum tamiḻaṉukkum tāḻvu
vaṭamoḻi vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyum caṭaṅku moḻiyumāy vaḻakkūṉṟiyapiṉ, tamiḻ avaṟṟiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭut taṉ paḻan talimiyi iḻantatuṭaṉ, tāḻvum aṭintatu. vaṭa coṟkaḷic coṉṉāl uyarvum teṉ coṟkaḷic coṉṉāl tāḻvum uṇṭākumeṉṟa tavaṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻmakkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ pukuntatiṉāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟāy vaḻakkiṟ pukuntu, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷi vaḻakku vīḻttiviṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻukku nērnta tāḻvu tamiḻaṉukkum nērntatu. ataṉāl, pirāmaṇaṉ toṭṭati ellāt tamiḻarum uṇṇalā meṉṟum, ettuṇi uyarnta tamiḻaṉāyiṉum. avaṉ toṭṭa tip pirāmaṇaṉ uṇṇak kūṭāteṉṟum kūṭṭaravu (camutāya) ēṟpāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa. itu tamiḻaṉ poruḷiyal vāḻvi mikamikat tākkiṟṟu.
cila tūya tamiḻkkulattār, tamiḻaṉ uyarviyum varalāṟṟiyum aṟiyātu, tammic cattiriyareṉṟum viciyareṉṟum 
collit tamakku āriyat toṭarpu kōravum, cila uyar vēḷāḷar tammic caṟcūttirar eṉṟaḻittu, tammi uyarttuvatu pōṟ karutikkoṇṭu uṇmiyil tāḻttavum, nilimi ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu.
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntavarākak karutappeṟum nelli maravūṇ (civa) vēḷāḷarum pirāmaṇarukkuk kīḻppaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻarellārum tāḻttappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu tāḻttap paṭṭavar eṉappaṭuvār uṇmiyil oṭukkap paṭṭavarē.
(2) tamiḻappārppārukkup piḻippiṉmi
pirāmaṇap purōkitar, uyarntōriṉ allatu uyarttap paṭṭōriṉ, ellā mataviyaṟ caṭaṅkukaḷiyum āṟṟum takutiyi muṟṟūṭṭākap peṟṟuviṭṭatiṉāl, tamiḻap pārppār palarkkup piḻippillātu pōyiṟṟu.
(3) ciṟuteyva vaḻipāṭu
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōr, ciṟuteyva vaṇakka niliyum perun teyva vaṇakka niliyum kaṭantu, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷ vaṇakka niliyi aṭintavar. aṅṅaṉamiruppavum, iṉṟu āriyak karaṇattāl, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, varuṇaṉ, tī mutaliya iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷiyum kōḷkaḷiyum pūtaṅkaḷiyum, vaṇaṅkum, muntiyal nilimikkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.
(4) kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟu
pirāmaṇap purōkitaṉ, maṇamakaṉi nōkki, nī mutal nāḷ tiṅkaḷukkum (cōmaṉukkum), iraṇṭām nāḷ yāḻōrukkum (kantaruvarukkum), mūṉṟām nāḷ tīkkum (akkiṉikkum), uṉ maṉiviyi aḷittuviṭṭu nālām nāḷ avaḷi nukarvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟum karaṇavurip pakuti.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyum maḻi.
eṉṟu koḷḷum tamiḻaṉ kaṟpuṇarccikku mikamika iḻukkut taruva toṉṟām. atilum tiṅkaḷ nukarntuviṭṭu yāḻōrkkut tara, yāḻōr nukarntuviṭṭut tīkkut tara, tī nukarntuviṭṭu uṉakkut tara, nī avaḷip peṟṟu nukarvāyāka eṉṟu maṇamakaḷmēṟ piṟarkku muṉṉurimiyum maṇamakaṉukkup piṉṉurimiyun tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu, evvakiyiṉum ēṟṟuk koḷḷat takkataṉṟu.
vittuva caṉa kōlākalaṉ eṉṉum ākkiyāḻvāṉukkum āḷavantārkkum kulōttuṅkaccōḻaṉ (?) avikkaḷattil nikaḻnta tarukkattil, āḷavantār mēṟkūṟiya kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟic cāṉṟu kāṭṭi, cōḻaṉ tēvi kaṟpiḻantavaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭiyapōtu, aracaṉum araciyum uṭpaṭa aviyōr aṉivarum pārāṭṭiyatu, tamiḻaṉukku aḻiyāp paḻiyit taruvatām.
(5) vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷ
tī valañceytal, ammi mitittal, aruntati kāṭṭal mutaliya pala caṭaṅkukaḷ vīṇāṉaviyākum.
ammi mitittal, akaliki cāvak katiyi niṉippittu maṇamakaḷi eccarikkum caṭaṅkākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ammiyit tirumakaḷ taṅkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷvatiṉāl, ati mitikkum vaḻakkam iyalpākat tamiḻarkkilli. atu kaṭṭukkaḻuttiyiṉ aṭiyāḷameṉṟu tamiḻppeṇṭir kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ammiyum kuḻaviyum tāyiyuñ cēyiyuṅ kuṟittu maṇamakaḷ makappēṟṟi muṉṉōkkuvaṉa veṉṟum, cilar kūṟuvatuṇṭu. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, camiyaṟ kiṉṟiyamiyāta ammi uṇavut taṭṭaṟṟa nal vāḻvik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatu, kuṟṟamākātu. ētōvoru naṟkuṟippiṉatāka maṇavaṟiyiṟ koṇṭuvantu vikkappeṟum ammikkum, aḻakiya kōlamiṭappeṟum āyiraṅ kaṇṇup pāṉikkum, poruttamāka ōr āriyattoṭarpuk kati kaṭṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. akaliki cāvattik kuṟippataṟkup potuvakiyāṉa kal pōtumē! avaḷ kallākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟallātu, ammiyākac cāvikkap paṭṭāḷ eṉṟu kati kūṟavilliyē! mēlum, koṅku vēḷāḷar tirumaṇattil maṇamaṉikku veḷiyē ammi niṟuttappaṭṭu, nāṭṭukkal eṉap peyarpeṟṟup pūciyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, pirāmaṇar tirumaṇattil, maṇamakaḷ oru kalli mitikkumpōtu, maṇamakaṉ ō peṇṇē! ik kalli miti. itippōl uṟutiyāyiru. uṉakkut tīṅku ceyya nāṭuvāri aḻittuviṭu. uṉ pakivari vel. eṉṟu maṇamakaḷi nōkkik kūṟuvatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
aruntati kāṭṭal eṉpatum āriyat toṭarpu karutiyatē. tamiḻa marapuppaṭi aruntati taliyāya kaṟparaci yallaḷ. avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya vaciṭṭamuṉivaṉ oḻukkattippaṟṟi aiyuṟṟu, avaṉāl viṇmīṉākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu kati kūṟukiṉṟatu. tamiḻap pattiṉip peṇṭirō, kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu coṟṟiṟam pāmi, eṉṉuṅ koḷkiyiṉar. ātalāl, ātimantiyār, pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, kaṇṇakiyār, āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tēviyār, tilakavatiyār mutaliya ettuṇiyō taliyāya tamiḻakak kaṟput teyvaṅ kaḷirukkavum, avari viṭṭuviṭṭu aruntatiyi niṉippittal, kaṉiyiruppak kāy kavarntaṟṟē.
(6) karaṇam viḷaṅkāmi
makkaḷ āṟaṟivuṭiyār. ataṉāl eticceyyiṉum aṟivōṭu ceytaṟkuriyar. oruvar vāḻkkiyil taliciṟanta nikaḻcciyāyum, iruvar iṉpa vāḻviṟku aṭikōluvatāyum, palar makiḻcciyuṟutaṟkuriya kāṭciyāyum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ vantu kūṭuvatāyum, uḷḷa tirumaṇac caṭaṅkip purōkitaṉukkaṉṟi poruḷoṭu ōtukiṉṟāṉā poru ḷillātu uḷaṟukiṉṟāṉā eṉpatiyum aṟiya viyalāta niliyil, ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamāka naṭatti varuvatu, nākarikattiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum muṟṟum muraṇāṉatām.
2. kulak kaṭṭuppāṭṭu miki
toḻilpaṟṟiya kulap pākupāṭu toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻakattil iruntuvantatēṉum, tamiḻa iṉam oṟṟumi kulintu ciṉṉā piṉṉamāyc cititaṟkum, ayalār eḷitāyp paṭiyeṭuttu vantu kippaṟṟutaṟkum, vaḻivakuttatu, piṟkālattil piṟappoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭa varuṇācirama tarumam eṉṉum āriya muṟik kulappiriviṉiyāl viḷinta kulaveṟiyē. itaṉā lēyē, kuraṅkāṉālum kulattilē koḷ. paḻaṅkālit tūrkkātē, putukkāli veṭṭātē, eṉap pala tīya koḷki kaḷ piṟantaṉa. tiri kaṭalōṭiyun tirumikat tēṭu eṉṉum tāḷāṇmi mikka nāṭṭil, kaṭal tāṇṭakkūṭā teṉṟum, āṟu tāṇṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
orē kulattiṟkuḷ tirumpattirumpa maṇañ ceytu vantatiṉāl, aṟivāṟṟal mikka piḷḷikaḷ arukip piṟantaṉa.
aṇṭāmi, tīṇṭāmi, kāṇāmi mutaliya kūṭṭaravuk koṭumi kaḷellām, iv iṭikkālattiltāṉ tali virittut tāṇṭavamāṭiṉa.
3. poruntā maṇamum vīṇcaṭaṅkum
kulaveṟi mella mellac cuṟṟaveṟiyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. oru muṟikkārap peṇṇukku allatu āṇukku, eṅkē vēṟiṭat til maṇam muṭintuviṭukiṉṟatō eṉṉum accattiṉāl, piḷḷip paruvattilēyē avarkku maṇam ceytu vippatu vaḻakkamāyviṭṭatu. cilar toṭṭiṟ kuḻantikkum maṇañ ceytu vittatuṇṭu. atu toṭṭil maṇam eṉṉappaṭṭatu. orutti kaṉṉiyāy iṟantāl tīkkati yaṭivāḷ eṉṉum āriyakkoḷkiyum, iḷami maṇattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
āṟiluñ cāvu nūṟiluñ cāvu ātalāl, maṇañceytu vik kappaṭṭa piḷḷi maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ pūppaṭiyumuṉ iṟantu viṭuvatumuṇṭu. avaṉāl maṇakkappaṭṭa ciṟumi uṇmiyil avaṉi maṇantirāviṭṭālum, maṇantatu pōṉṟē karutap paṭṭuk kimpeṇṇāy viṭuvāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇañ ceyyuṅ kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇavāḻkki yuṇṭu. atilum putuppeṇmiyi iḻantēyiruppāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇañ ceyyāk kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇa vāḻkki illavē yilli. kālamellām amaṅkaliyāyk karutappaṭṭu, pūp paṭintapiṉ kāmavuṇarcciyaṭakkum kariyaṟṟa tuṉpattiṟkō, oḻukkakkēṭṭiṟkō uriyavaḷāvāḷ. piṟa peṇṭirpōl oru kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻavum, pūccūṭavum, maṅkala viṉikaḷiṟ kalantukoḷḷavum, veḷip paṭiyāyp piḷḷi peṟavum, iṉpanukaravum, urimiyaṟṟavaḷāy, taṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum ulaka vāḻkkiyi oru ciṟivāḻkkiyum aḷaṟṟu (naraka) vāḻkkiyumākavē kaḻittu, colloṇāt tuṉpuṟuvāḷ.
piḷḷi maṇamakaṉ taṉ ciṟu maṉivi pūppaṭintu taṉṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻumvari iṟavātiruppiṉum, nīṭittuvāḻum nilimiyillākkāl, mēṟkūṟiya tuṉpaṅkaḷē nērum. pūppaṭinta piṉṉarē makaḷirkku maṇañceytuvippiṉ, ittakiya tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu perumpālum tappi yuyvar.
piḷḷip paruvattil maṇañ ceytu vippatāl, maṉivi pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭivāḻa nērum. uṭal muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiyumuṉ karukkoḷḷum makaḷir, talimakap pēṟṟil iṟantuviṭuvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam iṟavātiruppiṉum, piṟakkum piḷḷi uraṉuḷḷatāyum nīṭu vāḻiyāyum iruppa tilli; tāyuṭalum taḷarntuviṭuvatuṇṭu.
pūppaṭiyumuṉ maṇañceytu vittālum pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ maṇañceytu vittālum, makaḷiriṉ iḷamiyip poṟutta variyil, iraṇṭum oṉṟē. pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ ellā makaḷirkkum kāmavuṇarcci ēṟpaṭṭuviṭuvatilli. itaṉāl, maṇam nikaḻnta piṉpum kūṭṭanāḷ taḷḷivikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu cērvu maṇam (cānti kaliyāṇam) allatu cērvu muḻuttam (cānti mukūrttam) eṉṉum vīṇ caṭaṅkiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
kāmak kuṟippum cērkkit tuṇivum illāta iḷamparuva āṇiṟkum peṇṇiṟkum maṇañceytu vippatiṉālēyē, avaṟṟi avarkku ūṭṭumporuṭṭu, nalaṅkiṭal, uṇa vūṭṭal, veṟṟili maṭittuk koṭuttal, appaḷan taṭṭal, oṟṟiyā iraṭṭiyā piṭittal, pūcceṇṭeṟital, eḻuttāṇiyeṭuttal, oḷintu viḷiyāṭal, mōtiram eṭuttal, piḷḷiyār allatu pommi koṭuttal, ūñca lāṭal, kōpittukkoṇṭu pōṉa maṇamakaṉi aḻittu varal, mañcaḷ nīrāṭal mutaliya pala vīṇāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. maṇavāḻkkikkēṟṟa vaḷarcciyaṭinta paruvattil makkaṭku maṇañceytu vittāl, avar piṟar tuṇiyiṉṟit tāmākavē kūṭi vāḻvar.
cila tamiḻakkulattār maṇavaṟiyil tirikaṭṭit tāli kaṭṭuvatu, makamatiyar innāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa payaṉaṟṟa ceyalākum.
iṉi, vaḷarcciyaṭinta makkaṭku maṇañceytu vitta viṭattum, avar viruppattik kēḷātu peṟṟōr tam viruppappaṭiyē iciyāta iḷiñaṉiyum iḷiñiyiyum iṇittu vittu, piṉpu avar tuṉpuṟumpōtu tāramuṅ kuruvun taliviti eṉṟum, kaḻuttil viḻunta māli kaḻaṟṟa muṭiyātu eṉṟum, aṭuppil vitta koḷḷi erintu tāṉ tīravēṇṭum eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu avar maṭamiyik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatēyākum.
iṉi maṇamakkaḷiṉ otta vaḷarcciyi oru ciṟitum kavaṉiyātu, oru ciṟuvaṉukku oru perum peṇṇi maṇañceytu vittu māmaṉē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉāvatum, oru muḻu vaḷarcci yaṭinta muraṭaṉik kaṇṭu añcic cākumāṟu, avaṉukku oru maṅkip paruvattu melliyali maṇam puṇarppatum, iṭikkālattu nikaḻntuvanta koṭuñ ceyalkaḷām.
paḻañcēra nāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil, pūppiṉ muṉ tāli kaṭṭu eṉṉum poy maṇamum, pūppiṉ piṉ campantam eṉṉum meym maṇamum, āka irumaṇam ovvoru peṇ ṇiṟkum cila kulattil nērntataṟkuk kāraṇam āriyamē.  (ta.ti.)



paṇṭit tamiḻa nākarikam
1. moḻi
potumakkaḷ amippu : oru nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ nākarika niliyik kāṭṭuvatu avar tāy moḻiyē. ennāṭṭilum, moḻi potumakkaḷ amippeṉṟum ilakkiyam pulamakkaḷ amippeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṭukuṟi yetuvumiṉṟi oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷum nuṇporuṭ pākupāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum niṟintu, pakuttaṟiviṟ kotta ciṟanta ilakkaṇam amintu, ellāk karuttuk kaḷiyum taṉ coṟkaḷālēyē teḷḷat teḷivākat terivippatu taliciṟanta moḻiyām.
muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭat teṉ kōṭiyaṭuttu, nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum uṇavuvaḷamum poṉ vaḷamum maṇi vaḷamum niṟintirunta paḻantamiḻnāṭṭil, vāḻntuvanta mutaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ ikkālap pulavariṉum ciṟanta nuṇmāṇ nuḻi pulattiṉarātaliṉ, kāṭci, karuttu eṉṉum iruvakip poruḷkaḷiyum kūrntu nōkkiyum nuṇukiyārāyntum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpiṟkēṟpa aḻakiya poruṭ (karaṇiyak) kuṟikaḷākavē ellāc coṟkaḷiyum vēṟupaṭuttal, oṉṟupaṭuttal iṉappaṭuttal eṉṉum mummuṟiyil amittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vēṟupaṭuttal
nālvakiyili :
cila pūṇṭukaḷilum, ceṭi koṭikaḷilum, vāḻi mā puḷi mutaliya maraṅkaḷilum, kiḷi nuṉiyiṟ kāmpuṭaṉ taṉittum aṭarntum perumpālum muṭṭi vaṭivil mellitā yiruppatu ili.
pullilum nel varaku pōṉṟa payirkaḷilum, nuṉiyilum taṇṭiyoṭṭiyum īṭṭipōl oṭuṅki nīṇṭu curacurappā yiruppatu tāḷ.
karumpilum, cōḷam nāṇal pōṉṟa perun taṭṭikaḷilum, nuṉiyilum kaṇuvoṭṭiyum peruntāḷāka ōṅki maṭintu toṅkuvatu tōki.
teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ pōl, nuṉiyiyum aṭiyiyum oruṅkē oṭṭiyē taṉittaṉi maṭṭiyōṭu tōṉṟi, viciṟi vaṭivil allatu tūvu vaṭivil muraṭāyiruppatu ōli.
aivaki malar nili :
tōṉṟum nili arumpu; malarat toṭaṅkum nili pōtu; malarnta nili malar; kīḻē viḻunta nili vī; vāṭic civanta nili cemmal.
arumpu, moṭṭu, muki, mokkuḷ eṉpaṉa, parumaṉ paṟṟiya vevvēṟu arumpu vakikaḷik kuṟippaṉa.
mūvakik kāyppunili :
iḷaṅkāy piñcu; mutirntatu kāy; paḻuttatu paḻam allatu kaṉi.
āṅkilam mikunta vaḷarcciyuṟṟu, ēṟattāḻa mūṉṟilakkam coṟkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉum iṉṉum atil kāyik kuṟikkac col amiyavilli; paḻukkāta paḻam eṉṟu vaṭṭa vaḻiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.
cila vakip piñcukaṭkuc ciṟappup peyarumuḷa. teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ piñcu kurumpi; māmpiñcu vaṭu; palāppiñcu mūcu; vāḻip piñcu kaccal;
iṅṅaṉamē nilittiṇiyiṉ (tāvarattiṉ) ellāvuṟuppuk kaḷiyum, nuṇpākupāṭu ceytu, vevvēṟu peyariṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻap potu makkaḷ.
iṭaṅkar (Alligator), karām (Gavial), mutali (Crocodile) eṉa mūvakiyāka mutalikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kutiri mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷukku muṉpē arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu kumari nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. atu ayalnāṭṭu vilaṅkēṉum, atip palvēṟu vakippaṭutti vevvēṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
puravi, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṉṉi, kutiri, pari, kantukam eṉṉum eṇvakik kutirikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappilakkaṇa mum, parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa (naripari. 87-94).
pāṇṭiyaṉ kutiri kaṉavaṭṭam; cōḻaṉ kutiri kōram; cēraṉ kutiri pāṭalam; kuṟunila maṉṉar kutiri kantukam.
uṉṉi, kaṇṇukam, kalimā, kiḷḷi, kuntam, kūntal, kokku, koyyuḷi, kōṭi, kōṇam, turaṅkam, tūci, pāymā, mā, maṇṭilam, vayamā mutaliyaṉa kutiriyiṉ potup peyarkaḷ. maṭṭam eṉpatu ciṟuvakik kutiri. taṭṭu eṉpatu nāṭṭuk kutiri. maṭṭattiṉum ciṟuvaki iḷamaṭṭam. tēci eṉpatu peruṅ kutiri.
kutiri eṉpatu oru ciṟappu vakiyiṉ peyarākak kūṟappaṭ ṭiruppiṉum, ulaka vaḻakkiṟ potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkuvatu aḵtoṉṟē. kutippatu kutiri. kutittal tāṇṭutal. kūṟṟaṅ kutittaluṅ kikūṭum eṉṉum kuṟaḷaṭiyi nōkkuka. 
(kuṟaḷ. 269).
uṇmi, vāymi, meymmi eṉṉum muccollum, uḷḷam vāy mey (uṭampu) eṉṉum mukkaraṇattiyum muṟiyē taḻuviṉavākac collappaṭuvatuṇṭu. ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟu poruṭkaraṇiyamum uḷa.
collutaliṉ vakikaḷik kuṟikka, ēṟattāḻa nāṟpatu coṟkaḷ tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. avi yāvaṉa:-
	col	ciṟappup poruḷ
	acittal	-	aciyaḻuttattuṭaṉ collutal.
