oruvaṉiṭattil oṉṟik kēṭkum pōtu, tāḻntavaṉ, ī eṉṟum, ottavaṉ, tā eṉṟum, uyarntavaṉ, koṭu eṉṟum, collavēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻ marapu.
oṉṟupaṭuttal
yāṉikkum tēṉ vaṇṭiṟkum mukattiṉ muṉ ōr uṟiñci (Proboscis) uḷḷatu. inta oppumiyik kaṇṭa mutaṟkālat tamiḻar, iraṇṭiṟkum tumpi eṉap potup peyar iṭṭaṉar. tūmpu - tumpu - tumpi = tūmpu (kuḻāy) pōṉṟa uṟuppiyuṭiyatu.
iṉappaṭuttal
oru poruḷi, uṭampiṉ allatu uṟuppiṉ vaṭivil, otta vēṟu poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭa paṇṭit tamiḻar, muṉṉataṉ peyarkku aṭi koṭuttup piṉṉavaṟṟiṟkiṭṭu, avaṟṟi yellām ōriṉappaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.ṭu : vēmpu, kaṟivēmpu, nāyvēmpu, nilavēmpu, nīrvēmpu.
vēmpip piṟavaṟṟōṭu oppunōkki nalla vēmpu eṉṟaṉar.
iṭukuṟiyiṉmi
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (640). āṉāl, pala coṟkaṭkup pārtta maṭṭil poruḷ tōṉṟātu. avaṟṟi āḻntu ārāya vēṇṭum. itaṉiyē, moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (877) kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
palā, paṉi, poṉ, maram mutaliyavaṟṟi moḻi nūlaṟivum collā rāycciyumillā ilakkaṇavuriyāciriyar, iṭukuṟi yeṉṟu kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.
palā parutta paḻattiyuṭiyatu. pal - paru - parumi.
o. nō : cil - ciṉ - ciṟu. ciṉmi = ciṟumi.
ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻam tūṅki yāṅku.  (kuṟun. 18).
paṉi karukkumaṭṭiyuṭiyatu. pal - paṉ = aṟuvāṭ pal, paṉṉaṟuvāḷ, paṉvittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. paṉ - paṉi = kūriya paṟpōṉṟa karukkuḷḷatu.
poṉ poṟpu allatu polivuḷḷatu. pol - poli - polivu. pol - poṟpu = aḻaku. pol - polam - polaṉ. pol - poṉ. poṟṟa = aḻakiya (cīvaka. 270). pollā = aḻakillāta (auviyār). poṟṟatu = polivuṟṟatu 
(cīvaka. 649).
maram uṇarcciyaṟṟatu. kāl uṇarcciyaṟṟāl, marattup pōyviṭṭatu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. uṇarcciyaṟṟavaṉi maram pōlirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpar. matamatappu = uṇarcciyiṉmi, timir. matam - maram. matattal = marattal.
o. nō : viti - viri.
iṭukuṟiyillāmalē oru perun tāymoḻiyi ākkiyatu, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
iyaṟkic collākkam
tamiḻil ellāc coṟkaḷum iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil amintavi. vaṭamoḻiyil ā eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭāl etirpporuḷ viṉiccoṟkaḷi amittuk koḷvar.
e.ṭu : kaccati = celkiṟāṉ. ākaccati = varukiṟāṉ. tattē = koṭukkiṟāṉ, ātattē = eṭukkiṟāṉ. ittakiya ceyaṟkiyamippu tamiḻil illi.
tūymi
kumarik kaṇṭattil tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavilli. veḷināṭṭiliruntu poruḷkaḷ variṉ, avaṟṟiṟku uṭaṉuṭaṉ tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷ amikkap paṭṭaṉa.
karumpu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu atikamāṉiṉ muṉṉōrāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu.
amararp pēṇiyum āvuti yaruttiyum
 aruppeṟaṉ marapiṟ karumpivaṭ ṭantum
 nīraka virukki yāḻi cūṭṭiya
 toṉṉili marapiṉniṉ muṉṉōr pōla
 eṉṟu auviyār pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka (puṟam. 99) cīṉa nāṭṭi vāṉavar nāṭeṉpatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. karuppu niṟamāṉatu karumpu.
miḷakāy amerikkāviṉiṉṟu vantatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. miḷaku + kāy = miḷakāy. miḷakupōṟ kāramuḷḷa kāy miḷakāy. ivvaḻakkip piṉpaṟṟiyē, uruḷikkiḻaṅku, pukiyili, nilakkaṭali, vāṉkōḻi mutaliya peyarkaḷum piṟkālattil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ilakkaṇa amippu
irutiṇi : poruḷkaḷiyellām, pakuttaṟivuḷḷatu, pakuttaṟivillatu eṉap pakuttatu tamiḻap potumakkaḷē. kāḷiyum āvum aḵṟiṇiyil āṇpālum peṇpālu māyiṉum, kāḷi vantāṉ, āvu vantāḷ eṉṟu yārum collum vaḻakkamilli. kāḷi vantatu, āvu vantatu eṉṟu iraṇṭiyum oṉṟaṉpāliṟ kūṟuvatē marapu.
murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi vantāḷ eṉṟu āṇpāl peṇpāl (pakuttaṟivuḷḷa) makkaḷukkē vakukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulakiṟ pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷum iṟivaṉumē uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum, piṟavellām uyiruḷḷaviyāyiṉum illaviyāyiṉum tāḻnta vakuppē yeṉṟum, ēṟkeṉavē potumakkaḷ vakutta iru vakuppiṟkum, ilakkaṇiyar muṟiyē uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟu peyar maṭṭum iṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu, ēṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa moḻiyamitikaḷuḷ nallavaṟṟi maṭṭum koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkup peyariṭṭuk kāṭṭum muṟiyēyaṉṟi, putitāka ēṟpaṭṭa pulavar paṭippaṉṟu. pakuttaṟivi aḷaviyākak koṇṭu poruḷkaḷip pakutta iḵtoṉṟē, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ akak karaṇa vaḷarcciyik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
ōriyaloḻuṅku
yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ; nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ; nīṉ, nīm, nīṅkaḷ; tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ eṉṉum mūviṭap pakarap peyarkaḷum;
avaṉ	ivaṉ	uvaṉ	evaṉ
avaḷ	ivaḷ	uvaḷ	evaḷ
avar	ivar	uvar	evar
atu	itu	utu	etu
avi	ivi	uvi	evi
eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyar viṉāp peyarkaḷum; oṉṟumutal toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatu varippaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, koṇṭuḷḷa ōriyalamippi vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇamuṭiyātu.
poruṭpāl
āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl īṟupaṟṟiya pālamippiṉṟi, poruḷkaḷiṉ āṇmi peṇmiyum orumi paṉmiyum paṟṟiya pālamippē tamiḻiluḷḷatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil, maṉiviyik kuṟikkum tāra, pāryā, kaḷattira eṉṉum muccoṟkaḷuḷ, mutalatu āṇpāl; iṭiyatu peṇpāl; kaṭiyatu alippāl. pottakattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, kiranta āṇpāl; cruti peṇpāl; putaka alippāl. itiṉiṉṟu ammoḻiyiṉ oḻuṅki aṟintu koḷka.
pālicivu
tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarkaḷil, eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyum perumpālum tiṇipāl eṇ iṭam ottēyirukkum. tiṇipāl eṇ iṭa mayakkam iruppiṉum teḷivāyt teriyum.
vaṭamoḻiyil, tat kaccati eṉpatu avaṉ pōkiṟāṉ, avaḷ pōkiṟāḷ, atu pōkiṟatu, eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭaramippu tamiḻil illi.
pulamakkaḷ amippu
urinaṭi, ceyyuḷ eṉa moḻinaṭi iruvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ ceyyuḷ pulamakkaḷ amippākum. atu pāṭṭum pāvum eṉa iru vakiyām. urinaṭi paṇpaṭṭu icippāṭṭākavum, icip pāṭṭu paṇpaṭṭuc ceyyuḷāka vum, tiruntum, ākavē, ceyyuḷē moḻiyiṉ ucca niliyām.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar, ceyyuṭ kaliyiṉ koṭumuṭi yēṟi, aṟuvaki veṇpākkaḷiyum, nālvaki akavaṟpākkaḷi yum, nālum aintum āṟumāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiyuṭiya nāl vakik kalippākkaḷiyum, iruvaki vañcippākkaḷiyum, yāttirun taṉar. yāttal = kaṭṭutal.
veṇpāvum kalippāvum pōṉṟa ceyyuḷ vakikaḷi, vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇṭalaritu.
paṇṭit tamiḻar ceyyuṭkaliyiṟ ciṟantiruntataṉāl, ceyyuḷukkum nūṟpāviṟkum poruḷkūṟum uri uṭpaṭa, ellā ilakkiyattiyum ceyyuḷi lēyē iyaṟṟiyiruntaṉar.
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
...............................................................................................................
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (cey.78) kūṟutal kāṇka.

2. tuppuravu
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ viṭikāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ, ūrukku veḷiyē ceṉṟu kālikkaṭaṉ kaḻittu, āṟṟil allatu kālvāyil allatu kuḷattil kāl kaḻuvuvar. āṟṟiṟkup pōtal, kālvāykkup pōtal, kuḷattiṟkup pōtal, kollikkup pōtal, veḷikkup pōtal eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakku iti yuṇarttum. ittakiya vaḻakkum oṉṟukkup pōtal iraṇṭukkup pōtal eṉṉum iṭakka raṭakkalum tamiḻar nākarikattik kāṭṭum.
kalvit toḻilāḷar, kāvalar, vaṇikar mutaliya uṭaluḻip pillā vakuppār kāliyilum; uḻavar, kittoḻilāḷar, kūlikkārar mutaliya uḻippāḷi vakuppār māliyilum; nāḷtōṟum kuḷittu vantaṉar. aḻukkit tēyppataṟku āṭavar pīrkkaṅ kūṭṭip payaṉpaṭuttuvatuṇṭu. peṇṭir mañcaḷ, cuṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟit tēyttuk kuḷippar.
cuṇṇam eṉpatu palavaki naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷic cērttu iṭikkum poṭi. ati iṭittu vippatu paṇṭik kālattu iḷamakaḷirkkup peruvaḻakka māyiruntatu. cuṇṇamiṭikkum pōtu pāṭṭup pāṭuvar. icceyal māṇikkavācakar maṉattik kavarntatiṉāl, tiruppoṟcuṇṇam eṉṉum tiruvācakap pāṭal tilliyil aruḷic ceyyappeṟṟatu.
araṇmaṉiyil vāḻum araca makaḷirum, māḷikikaḷil vāḻum celvap peṇṭirum, mātavi pōlum nāṭakak kaṇikiyarum, kōṭṭam, turukkam, takaram, akil, cantaṉam eṉṉum aivaki viriyum (naṟumaṇap poruḷum); nāval allatu pūvanti, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, āl, aracu, atti, itti, muttakkācu allatu karuṅkāli, māntaḷir eṉṉum pattu vakit tuvarum; ilavaṅkam, paccili, kaccōlam, ēlam, nākaṇam, kōṭṭam, nākam, matāvarici, takkōlam, naṉṉāri, veṇkōṭṭam, kācaṟi (kattūri), vēri, ilāmiccam, kaṇṭil veṇṇey, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, tuttam, vaṇṇak kaccōlam, arēṇukam, māñci, cayilēkam, puḻuku, puṉṉi naṟuntātu, puliyukir, caraḷam, tamālam, vakuḷam, patumukam, nuṇṇēlam, koṭuvēri eṉṉum muppattiruvaki ōmālikiyum; ūṟavitta naṉṉīriṟ kuḷittu vantaṉar.
āṭavar aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) kāriyum (caṉiyum), peṇṭir cevvāy veḷḷiyum, oḻuṅkāy eṇṇey tēyttut tali muḻukiṉar. kuḷippu nīrāṭṭu eṉṟum, talimuḻukku neyyāṭṭu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. neyyāṭṭil eṇṇeyiyum uṭam paḻukkiyum pōkka, cīyaṟkāy ariyal, arippu ariyal, ucilit tūḷ, pāci (pacci)p payaṟṟumā, kaḷimaṇ mutaliya vaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
cīyaṟkāy allatu cīkkāy eṉpatu, aḻukkip pōkkuṅ kāy eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu; cikikkāy eṉpataṉ marūu aṉṟu.
cīttal = 1. tuṭittal. meṉpūñ cemmaloṭu naṉkalañ cīppa  (maturik. 685).
2. pōkkutal. iruḷcīkkuñ cuṭarēpōl  (kalit. 100 : 24).
3. tuppuravākkutal (cūṭā).
ūrkkum aṇittē poyki poykikkuc
cēyttum aṉṟē ciṟukāṉ yāṟē
kūḻik kērmaṇ koṇarkañ cēṟum
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷ (113), paṇṭit tamiḻa makaḷir ūrukkaṇittāṉa kuṭinīrniliyiṟ kuḷikkāmal caṟṟu appā luḷḷa kāṭṭāṟṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu kuḷittatiyum, kūntalaḻukkup pōkkak kaḷimaṇ eṭuttuc ceṉṟatiyum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
iru pālārum kuḷittavuṭaṉum talimuḻukiyavuṭaṉum, veḷutta tuvarttu muṇṭiṉāl taliyiyum uṭampiyum tuvartti, veḷuttāṭi allatu puttāṭi yuṭuppar. māṟṟāṭi yillāviṭiṉ, muṉpu aṇintirunta āṭiyit tuvittuk koḷvar.
peṇṭir kārakiṟ pukiyālum cantaṉak kaṭṭip pukiyālum tam kūntaliṉ īram pularttuvar.
kāliyiṟ kuḷippiṉum kuḷiyāviṭiṉum, pal tulakkāmal oruvarum uṇpatilli.
cōṟuṇpavar, vāyum mukamum kikālum kaḻuviya piṉ, tuppuravāṉa iṭattil taṭukkil allatu palakiyil amarntu cappaḷittiruntu (cammaṇaṅkūṭṭi uṭkārntu), kaḻuviya vāḻi iliyil allatu veṇkala vaṭṭilil valakkiyālēyē eṭuttu uṇpar. aracar poṟkalattilum celvar veḷḷik kalattilum uṇpatu vaḻakkam. peṟṟōrum peṟṟa ciṟu piḷḷi kaḷumāyiruntāloḻiya, orē kalattil allatu iliyiṟ palar uṇpatilli. uṇṭeḻuntapiṉ, uṇṭa iṭam iliyakaṟṟit taṇṇīr allatu cāṇa nīr teḷittut tuppuravākkappeṟum. iliyilviṭṭa miccilip peṟ ṟōrum maṉiviyarum irappōrum tavirap piṟar uṇṇār. cila uṇavu vakiyāl ēṟpaṭum vāy nāṟṟattip pōkkutaṟku, naṟumaṇac carakki vāyiliṭutal, veṟṟili tiṉṉutal, mārpiṟ cantaṉam pūcutal mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāḷvar. ivaṟṟic cerimāṉattiṉ poruṭṭeṉṟu colvatu muṇṭu.
viṭintavuṭaṉ peṇṭir muṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ cāṇanteḷittu vīṭu vācalip perukkik kōlamiṭuvar. cevvāyum veḷḷiyum tappātu vīṭu muḻutum āviṉ cāṇattāl meḻukuvar. āṇṭiṟ koru muṟiyum tirumaṇam nikaḻumpōtum, vīṭu muḻutum oṭṭaṭi pōkki veḷḷiyaṭittuc cemmaṇ kōlamiṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṇṭiṟkoru muṟiyeṉṟatu poṅkaṟ paṇṭiki. poṅkaṟku muntiṉa nāḷ, iṉṟupōl aṉṟum vīṭṭiluḷḷa aḻukkuk kantalkaḷiyum utavāp poruḷkaḷiyum koḷutti viṭuvar. atōṭu taṅkaḷip piṭitta pīḻi pōy viṭṭatu eṉṉuṅ karuttil, atip pōki eṉṟaṉar. pōkutal pōtal, pōkiyatu pōki. ac colli vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) peyarākak koṇṭu, avaṉi nōkkic ceyyum viḻā eṉpatu poruntātu. ti piṟantāl vaḻi piṟakkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi, pōki eṉṉuñ coṟporuḷi valiyuṟuttum.
vāṭaki vīṭukaḷiṟ putuk kuṭi pukumpōtum, oṭṭaṭi pōkki veḷḷiyaṭikkappeṟum.
veḷic ceṉṟu vanta vīṭṭārum veḷiyārum, vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukumuṉ pātattik kaḻuviviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu oḻukka neṟi.
veppamillāta nāḷilum vēḷiyilum muḷḷillāta iṭattilum kūṭa, vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷic celliṉ, aṭiyil maṇṇum mācum paṭātapaṭi ceruppaṇintē celvar uyarn tōr. ceruppu vācaṟku veḷiyē allatu vācalaṇṭitāṉ viṭappeṟum. ek karaṇiyam paṟṟiyum uḷḷē koṇṭu cellappaṭuvatilli.
piḷḷi peṟṟa vīṭṭiṟku ēḻu nāḷum, mātaviṭāy vantava ḷukkup paṉṉiru nāḷum, iḻavu vīṭṭiṟkup patiṉintu nāḷum tīṭṭām.
tīṭṭuḷḷa peṇṭir kalantoṭā makaḷir (puṟam.299) eṉappaṭuvar.

3. ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉ mutal uṇavi nākarikamāyc camittuṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē. ēṉiya nāṭṭārellām, tam nāṭṭil viḷinta uṇavup poruḷkaḷip pacciyākavum cuṭṭum veṟumiyāy avittum uṇṭuvanta kālattil uṇavic cōṟum kaṟiyum eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, nellariciyic cōṟākac camittum, kaṟi allatu kuḻampu vakikaḷi, cuviyūṭṭuvaṉavum uṭampi valuppaṭuttuvaṉavum nōyvarātu taṭuppaṉavumāṉa palavaki maruntuc carakkukaḷi ucili (macāli) yākac cērttu ākkiyum, uyarvāka uṇṭu vanta perumi tamiḻaṉatākum.
kāy vakikaḷik kāmpu kaḷintu cīva vēṇṭupavaṟṟic cīvik kaḻuviyum; pittamuḷḷavaṟṟi avittiṟuttum; mañcaḷ, miḷaku allatu miḷakāy vaṟṟal, cīrakam, kottumalli, tēṅkāy mutaliya vaṟṟi mipōl arittuk kalantum; iñci, veṅkāyam, veḷḷippūṇṭu, peruṅkāyam, karāmpū (karuvāppū), karuvāppaṭṭi, ēlam, cōmpu mutaliyavaṟṟi vēṇṭumaḷavu cērttum; ventapiṉ puḷi allatu elumiccañcāṟu viṭavēṇṭiyavaṟṟiṟku viṭṭum; kaṭuku, kaṟivēppili mutaliyaṉa tūvi āviṉ neyyil tāḷittum, mutaṉ mutaṟ kuḻampu kāyccappeṟṟatu tamiḻakattilēyē. tāḷippu ilakkiya vaḻakkil kuy eṉappaṭum. kuyyuṭi yaṭicil (puṟam. 127).
ulakil ariciyuḷḷa tavacaṅkaḷiṟ palavakiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nellaricic cōṟupōl vēṟoṉṟum cuviyāyk kuḻam poṭu poruntuvatilli. tamiḻakattilēyē, kāṭik kaṇṇi yarici, kutirivāliyarici, cāmiyarici, tiṉiyarici, varakarici eṉa aivaki yariciyuḷa. avi nellaricikkīṭākā. kampum cōḷamum kañciyum kūḻumē camittaṟkutavum. kēḻvarakāl kaḷiyum kūḻumtāṉ ākka muṭiyum. kōtumi, vāṟkōtumi pōṉṟa piṟa nāṭṭut tavacaṅkaḷ (tāṉiyaṅkaḷ), appamum (roṭṭiyum) ciṟṟuṇṭiyumtāṉ aṭavum cuṭavum ēṟkum.
nellariciyuḷḷum palavaki yuḷa. avaṟṟuḷ tali ciṟantatu campā. atuvum aṟupatu vakippaṭṭatām. avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantaṉa cīrakac campāvum ciṟumaṇic campāvum.
paccarici, puḻuṅkalarici eṉṉum iru vakiyuḷ, tamiḻar virum piyatu piṉṉatē. atu cūṭṭit taṇippataṉāl veppanāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟatākum; kuḻampiṉ cuviyiyum mikuttuk kāṭṭum. ataṟkākavē, ataṟkuriya taṉi muyaṟciyi mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum valuk kuṟiviṟkuk kaṟi vakikaḷālum neyyālum īṭu ceyyap peṟum.
iruṅkā ḻulakki yirumpumukan tēytta
avippumāṇ arici amaliveṇ cōṟu  (ciṟupāṇ. 193-4).
eṉpatu, tūya veḷḷiyāy arici tīṭṭappaṭṭatit terivikkum.
potuvāka, kālic ciṟṟuṇṭiyum naṇpakal mālip pēruṇṭiyum āka mūvuṇṭikaḷē paṇṭit tamiḻar uṇṭu vantaṉar.
kāliyuṇavum naṇpakaluṇavum, uḻavarkkum kūliyāḷarkkum paḻañcōṟum paḻaṅkaṟiyum ūṟukāyumākum. putuk kaṟiyāyiṉ neruppil vāṭṭiyatum tuviyalumāyirukkum. irāvuṇavē avarukkup putuc camiyalākum.
meṉpulattu vayaluḻavar
vaṉpulattup pakaṭuviṭṭuk
kuṟumuyaliṉ kuḻiccūṭṭoṭu
neṭuvāḷip palluviyaṟ
paḻañcōṟṟup pukavarunti
 eṉpatu puṟam (395).
uḻavar maṅkala viṉināḷum viḻā nāḷum viruntu nāḷum pōṉṟa ciṟappu nāḷilaṉṟi, mūvēḷiyum nellaricic cōṟuṇpatilli. varaku, cāmi, kampu, cōḷam, kēḻvaraku mutaliya avvakkālattu viḷinta tavacattiyē avvak kālattup pakaluṇavāy uṇpar.
illattilum niḻalilumiruntu vēli ceyvōrellām, perumpālum, kāliyiṟ paḻiyatum naṇpakal māliyiṟ cuṭucōṟum, uṇpar. aracarum celvarum ellā nāḷum, piṟarellām ciṟappunāḷum, kāliyiṟ palakāramum poṅkalum pōṉṟa cuṭu ciṟṟuṇṭi vakikaḷi uṇpar.
iruvēḷip pēruṇṭikaḷuḷ, tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantatu naṇ pakalatē. kuḻampuc cōṟum miḷaku nīrc cōṟum mōr allatu tayirc cōṟum eṉa, mukkaṭaviyuḷḷatu pēruṇṭi. ati aṟucuviyuṇṭiyeṉpatu vaḻakku. mukkaṉikaḷuḷ ētēṉu moṉṟu iṉippum, pākaṟkāy allatu cuṇṭivaṟṟal kacappum, kāravaṭi uṟippum, puḷikkaṟiyum tayirum ūṟukāyum puḷippum, mātuḷaṅkāy allatu kaccal tuvarppum, uppēṟi uvarppum, ākum. ittakiya uṇṭi, tamiḻariṉ cuvi mutircciyiyum taliciṟanta nākarikattiyum maruttuva aṟiviyum uṭalnala vuṇarcciyiyum oruṅkē kāṭṭum. aṟucuviyuḷḷum tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantavi iṉippum puḷippum ākum. itu veppa nāṭṭiyalpu.
muḷitayir picinta kāntaḷ melviral
kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaḻāa tuṭīik
kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ kuyppuki kamaḻat
tāṉtuḻan taṭṭa tīmpuḷip pākar
iṉiteṉak kaṇavaṉ uṇṭaliṉ
nuṇṇitiṉ makiḻntaṉ ṟoṇṇutaṉ mukaṉē.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷum (164).
vēḷi veṇpū veṇṭayirk koḷīi
āymakaḷ aṭṭa ampuḷi mitavi  (215)
puṟavukkaru vaṉṉa puṉpula varakiṉ
pāṟpey puṉkam tēṉoṭu mayakkik
kuṟumuyaṟ koḻuñcūṭu kiḻitta vokkaloṭu
.....................................................................................................................
amalik koḻuñcō ṟārnta pāṇarkku  (34)
pāliṟ peytavum pākiṟ koṇṭavum
aḷavupu kalantu mellitu paruki   (381)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa. puṉkam = cōṟu. mitavi = kūḻ.
tamiḻar viruntuḷ taliciṟantatu tirumaṇa viruntu. atiṟ patiṉeṇvakik kaṟiyum kaṉṉalum (pāyacam) paṭikkap peṟum. patiṉeṇ vakik kaṟikaḷ : aviyal (uviyal), kaṭiyal, kummāyam, kūṭṭu (ventāṇam), tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, poriyal, vaṟuval, puḷikkaṟi, paccaṭi (āṇam), appaḷam, tuviyal, ūṟukāy, vaṟṟal, uḻuntuvaṭi, kāravaṭi, tēṅkuḻal, mukkaṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpaṉa.
kummāyam kuḻiya venta paruppu, allatu payaṟu. ventāṇam (venta āṇam) eṉpatu iṉṟu veñcaṇam eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
tiṇmi nilipaṟṟi nīr, cāṟu, tīyal, iḷaṅkuḻampu, kuḻampu, kūṭṭu eṉak kuḻampu palavakippaṭum. cōṟum, carukkarip poṅkal (akkāraṭali, akkāravaṭicil), veṇpoṅkal, miḷakuppoṅkal, neyppoṅkal, tēṅkāyccōṟu, pāṟpoṅkal, kaṭumpuccōṟu, ūṉcōṟu (pulavu), ūṉṟu viyaṭicil eṉap palavakippaṭum.
ūṉcōṟu allatu pulavu paṇṭik kālattil tamiḻakatti lēyē tōṉṟip piṉpu vaḻakkaṟṟatu; mukammatiyar vantapiṉ avariṭamiruntu putitāyk kaṟṟuk koṇṭataṉṟu.
maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum miviṭi vīḻppavum
aṭṭāṉ ṟāṉāk koḻuntuvi yūṉcōṟum
peṭṭāṅ kīyum peruvaḷam paḻuṉi
eṉṟum (113),
pularāp paccili piṭiyiṭupu toṭutta
malarā mālip pantukaṇ ṭaṉṉa
ūṉcōṟ ṟamali pāṇkaṭum paruttum
cemmaṟ ṟammaniṉ vemmuṉi yirukki
eṉṟum (33), varum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷik kāṇka.
amiḻtaṉa marapiṉ ūṉṟuvi yaṭicil
eṉpatu (puṟam. 390 : 17), pulaviṉ ciṟappu vakiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟicci vakikaḷuḷ uṭumpiṟicciyit taliciṟantatākat tamiḻar koṇṭami, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāṟkāṭi, arik kōḻi, kālāṭu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl teriya varukiṉṟatu.
uṭumpiḻu taṟutta oṭuṅkāḻp paṭalic
cīṟiṉ muṉṟil kūṟucey tiṭumār
koḷḷi vitta koḻuniṇa nāṟṟam
maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum matuki maṉṟattu
eṉṉum puṟap pāṭṭaṭikaḷum (325 : 7-10)
nāykoṇṭāl, pārppārum tiṉpar uṭumpu

cerumiku cēeyniṟ pāṭunar kiyē.
eṉṟu kūṟiya viṭiyāl (puṟam. 14 : 12-19), kaṭik kaḻakak kālattup pirāmaṇaṉum ūṉuṇṭami aṟiyappaṭum.
neykaṉi kuṟumpūḻ kāya māka
ārpatam peṟuka tōḻi yatti
peruṅkal nāṭaṉ varinteṉa avaṉetir
naṉṟō makaṉē yeṉṟaṉaṉ
naṉṟē pōlum eṉṟurit tōṉē.  (kuṟun. 389).
eṉpatil, neyyiṟ poritta kuṟumpūḻk kaṟi kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ 214ām puṟappāṭṭiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭāṉ. kuṟumpūḻ = kāṭi. maṉikkōḻi 395ām puṟappāṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṭṭil veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṭāviyē paṇṭittamiḻar cuviyuḷḷateṉa virumpiyuṇṭaṉar.
māṭan tōṟum miviṭi vīḻppa  (puṟam. 33).
maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum miviṭi vīḻppavum  (puṟam. 113).
naṟavun toṭumiṉ viṭiyum vīḻmiṉ  (puṟam. 262).
miviṭi yirumpōttuc centīc cērtti  (puṟam. 364).
viṭivīḻttuc cūṭukiḻippa  (puṟam. 366).
marantōṟum mivīḻppa  (maturik kāñci, 754).
mallaṉ maṉṟattu mataviṭi keṇṭi  (perumpāṇ. 143).
ceṅkaṇ maḻaviṭi keṇṭi  (perumporuḷ viḷakkam).
mi = kārāṭu, veḷḷāṭu. viṭi = kaṭā, veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṭā.
ikkālattil marakkaṟi vakiyic cērnta mōrk kuḻampu, akkālattil ūṉkaṟi vakiyākavum iruntatu.
cempuṟ ṟīyaliṉ iṉṉaḷip puḷittu
....................................................................................................................
iravalark kīyum vaḷḷiyōṉ nāṭē.
eṉṟu kapilar pāriyiṉ paṟampunāṭṭip paṟṟip pāṭiyiruttal (puṟam. 119) kāṇka. aḷi = mōr.
viṭi eṉpatu muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭinta iḷa vilaṅkiṉ allatu paṟaviyiṉ peyar. viṭittal = paruttal, viṟittal muṟukkāyiruttal. vilaṅkiṉattil āṇiyum paṟaviyiṉattil irupāliyum viṭiyeṉpatu marapu.
neyyiṟ poritta kaṟiyit tamiḻarellārum virumpi yuṇṭaṉar. veḷḷarip piñcik kuṟukkēyaṟuttāl tōṉṟum viti pōṉṟa veḷiṟiya naṟuneyyiyē avar payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy vāḷpōḻn tiṭṭa
kāḻpōl nalviḷar naṟuney  (puṟam. 246)
eṉṟu pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ tēvi kūṟutal kāṇka.
poritta kaṟiyōṭu uṇpataṟkup pulavaricic cōṟupōl nīṇṭa parukkic cōṟṟiyē virumpiṉar. nīṇṭa parukki kokkiṉ viral pōliruttalāl, kokkukir nimiral eṉappaṭṭatu.
maṇṭiya kaṇṭa māṉvaṟik karuṉi
kokkukir nimiral okkal āra  (puṟam. 398 : 24 - 25).
tamiḻakattil, mutalil, tuṟaviyarē pulāluṇavi nīkki vantaṉar. tiruvaḷḷuvar, aruḷuṭimi pulāṉ maṟuttal kol lāmi eṉṉum mūvatikāraṅkaḷiyum, tuṟavaṟaviyalil vit tiruppatum kaṇṇappanāyaṉār paṭiyalum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa. camaṇam tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vanta piṉṉarē, illaṟat tārum, ciṟappākac civaneṟiyar, pulāluṇavi nīkkat talip paṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, marakkaṟiyuṇavu civam eṉappaṭṭatu.
marakkaṟi yuṇaviṉarāyiṉum ūṉuṇaviṉarāyiṉum, pāl, tayir, mōr, veṇṇey, ney eṉṉum āṉinti mikap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.
naṟuneyk kaṭali vicippa  (puṟam. 120 : 14).
kaṟi porikkak kāycciya neyyuliyōcikku matayāṉiyiṉ perumūcciyum, kotikkiṉṟa neyyil tuḷḷi vēkum kaṟiyōcikku āḻnta niṟi kuḷattil perumaḻit tuḷikaḷ viḻum ōciyiyum paṇṭippulavar uvami kūṟiyiruppatu mika makiḻntu pārāṭṭattakkatu.
miyal yāṉi ayāvuyirt taṉṉa
neyyuli  (puṟam. 261 : 8-9).
neṭunīra niṟikayattup
paṭumārita tuḷipōla
neytuḷḷiya vaṟi  (puṟam. 386 : 13).
yāṉikkuk koṭukkum kavaḷamum neyyil mitittut tiraṭṭappaṭṭatiṉāl, neymmiti eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. camiyal vakikkellām āviṉ neyyiyē akkālattil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. nalleṇṇey eṉṉum eḷ ney tali muḻukkiṟku maṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
aracarum maṟavarum pāṇarum kūttarum porunarum pulavar cilarum uḻavarum uḻippāḷiyarum, kuṭippu vakikaḷuḷ kaḷḷiyē ciṟantatākak koṇṭaṉar. kaḷ eṉṉuñ col, veṟinīr, patanīr (teḷivu allatu paṉañcāṟu), maṭṭu (carpattu), tēṉ eṉṉum nālvakiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum. inta nāṉkiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar paṇṭit tamiḻar. ivaṟṟuḷ veṟinīr vakiyē vaḷḷuvarāl kaḷḷuṇṇāmi eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kaṇṭikkappaṭṭatu.
veṟinīr iyaṟkik kaḷḷum ceyaṟkik kaḷḷum eṉa iruvakittu. paṉaṅkaḷḷum teṉṉaṅkaḷḷum īccaṅkaḷḷum iyaṟki; aricic cōṟṟu nīrip puḷikka vittu arikkap paṭṭa ariyalum, mēṉāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu puṭṭikaḷil vanta matuk kaḷḷum ceyaṟki.
yavaṉar, naṉkalan tanta taṇkamaḻ tēṟal  (puṟam. 56 : 18).
veppa nāṭākiya tamiḻakattil, kāliyiliruntu katiravaṉ aṭiyum vari kāṭṭil kaṭuveyilil varuntiyuḻikkum uḻavarkku, uḻippu nōvip pōkkavum nīr vēṭkiyit taṇikkavum, veṟippum kuḷircciyum puḷippāṉa kuṭippu vēṇṭiyatāyiruntatu. ataṉāl, avarkkeṉṟu taṉik kaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu.
kaḷamark karitta viḷiyal veṅkaḷ eṉpatu (puṟam. 212 : 2) itiyuṇarttum.
kaḷḷiṉ puḷippiyum veṟippiyum mikukka, atik kaṇṇāṭik kalaṅkaḷilum mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykaḷilum iṭṭu mūṭip paṉṉāḷ maṇṇiṟ putittu vippatum vaḻakkam. attakiya kaḷḷiṉ kaṭumikkut tēṭkoṭṭum pāmpuk kaṭiyum uvami kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nilamputip paḻuṉiya maṭṭiṉ tēṟal  (puṟam. 120).
tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal  (puṟam. 392 : 16).
pāppukkaṭup paṉṉa tōppi  (akam. 348 : 7).
tēṟal = teḷinta kaḷ. tōppi = neṟ kaḷ.
iṉkaṭuṅkaḷ (puṟam. 80) eṉpataṉālum, pūkkamaḻ tēṟal (porunar. 157) eṉpataṉālum, ceyaṟkikkaḷ iṉimiyum maṇamum ūṭṭap peṟṟiruntami aṟiyalām.
nāṭpaṭāta iyaṟkik kaḷḷi uṭaluḻippāḷikaḷ veṟi yuṇṭākāvāṟu aḷavāka uṇpatāṟ kēṭilli yeṉpatum naṉṟeṉpatum, ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. vēṉiṟ kālattiṟkuk kuḷircciyāṉa paṉaṅkaḷḷiyum mārik kālattiṟku veppamāṉa teṉṉaṅkaḷḷiyum, iyaṟki vakuttiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
4. uṭi
tamiḻakattiṟ paḻaṅkālattilēyē necavu tōṉṟiviṭṭateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vayavar yōvāṉ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) tam mokoñcō-tārōvum cintu nākarikamum (Mohenjodaro and the Indus Civilization) eṉṉum nūlil ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ mēṉāṭukaṭku intiyāviṉiṉṟē tuṇi ēṟṟumatiyāṉa teṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattintiyā tamiḻakamē.
parutti toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭil viḷintu varukiṉṟatu. mūtāṭṭiyarum kimpeṇṭirum paṇṭik kālattil mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilkaḷuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu nūlnūṟṟal. ataṉāl avar paruttip peṇṭir eṉappaṭṭār.
paruttip peṇṭir paṉuva laṉṉa  (puṟam. 125 : 1).
āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta
nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval  (naṟ. 353).
pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuval iḻiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu  (naṉ. pāyiram).
paṉuval = nūl.
nūṟpataṟkup pañcic curuḷākat tiraṭṭi vippataṉāl, nūlnūṟṟalik koṭṭi nūṟṟal eṉpatu vaḻakku. nūṟkuṅ karuvi, cuṟṟum cakkara muṭimiyāl iṟāṭṭu allatu iṟāṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
iṟattal = vaḷital, iṟaku = vaḷinta tūvu.
iṟavu = kūriyiṉ cāyppu, iṟa - iṟappu = kūriyiṉ cāyppu. itu iṟavāṇam, iṟavāram eṉṟum collappaṭum.
iṟavu - iṟavuḷ = maliccarivu.
iṟi = 1. vīṭṭiṟappu, kuṟiyiṟik kurampi  (puṟam. 129). 2. peṇṭiriṉ vaḷinta muṉki. elvaḷi yiṟiyū rummē  (kalit. 7 : 16).
iṟital = vaḷital, vaṇaṅkutal. iṇiyaṭi yiṟimiṉ  (patiṉō, āḷu. tirukkalam. 48).
iṟi - iṟiñcu. iṟiñcutal = vaṇaṅkutal.
iṟa - iṟā = vaḷinta peruṅ kūṉi.
iṟā - iṟāl = 1. vaṭṭamāṉa tēṉkūṭu. 2. peruṅkūṉi.
iṟā - iṟavu = 1. peruṅkūṉi. kaṭaliṟaviṉ cūṭutiṉṟum (paṭṭiṉap. 63). 2. tēṉkūṭu (ñāṉavā.)
iṟā - iṟāṭṭu = 1. peruṅkūṉi. 2. tēṉkūṭu. 3. nūṟkum cakkaram.
iṟāṭṭu - iṟāṭṭi = nūṟkum cakkaram.
iṟāṭṭu - iṟāṭṭiṉam = 1. nūṟkum cakkaram. 2. nīriṟikkum uruḷi. 3. ēṟiviḷiyāṭum kuṭiyiṟāṭṭiṉam allatu iṟāṭṭiṉa vūñcal.
nūṟkum cakkaram kiyiṉāṟ cuṟṟappaṭuvataṉāl, atu kiyi ṟāṭṭu allatu kiyiṟāṭṭi, allatu kiyiṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṟu collappaṭum. iṟāṭṭu eṉṉum collē, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyil illi. irāṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻūuc col maṭṭum, tēṉkūṭu eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṟāṭṭu, iṟāṭṭi, iṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṉum mūvaṭivum, intiyil rahaṭ allatu rahaṇṭṭā eṉṉum vaṭivil nūṟkuṅ karuviyik kuṟittu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, intic collē tamiḻc coṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉi akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
nūl eṉṉuñ collē nuṇṇiyatu eṉṉum vērp poru ḷuṭiyatu. nuṇaṅku nuṇ paṉuval eṉpatu mika nuṇṇiya nūlik kuṟikkum.
akkālattil, paṭṭu, paruttip pañcu, vilaṅku mayir ākiya mūvakik karuvi yālum āṭi neytu vantaṉar. itu,
nūliṉu mayiriṉum nuḻinūṟ paṭṭiṉum
pālvaki teriyāp paṉṉū ṟaṭukkattu
naṟumaṭi ceṟinta aṟuvi vītiyum
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷāl (14 : 205 -7) aṟiyalām. mayiriṉum eṉpataṟku eli mayiriṉālum eṉṟu uri varin tuḷḷār aṭiyārkku nallār.
mayir niṟinta oruvaki maliyeli paṇṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭi lirunta teṉpatum, ataṉ mayirāl ciṟanta tāvaḷi (kampaḷam) neyyappaṭṭa teṉpatum,
pukaḻvaric ceṉṉimēṟ pūciyiṟ periyaṉa
 pavaḻamē yaṉiyaṉa paṉmayirp pēreli.
cenne ruppuṇuñ cevve limmayi
 ranne ruppaḷa vāypoṟ kampalam.
eṉṟu cīvakacintāmaṇi (1898, 2686) kūṟuvatāl aṟiyappaṭum. āyiṉum, elimayirāl maṭṭumaṉṟi āṭṭumayirālum āṭi neyyap paṭṭami.
elippūm pōrviyoṭu mayirppaṭam virittu
eṉṉum peruṅkati yaṭiyāl (uñcik 47 : 179) aṟiyalām.
paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭil neyyappaṭṭa āṭi vakikaḷuḷ, kōcikam, pītakam, paccili, arattam, nuṇṭukil, cuṇṇam, vaṭakam, pañcu, iraṭṭu, pāṭakam, kōṅkalar, kōpam, cittirak kammi, kuruti, kariyal, pēṭakam, pariyaṭṭakkācu, vētaṅkam, puṅkark kāḻakam, cilliki, tūriyam, paṅkam, tattiyam, vaṇṇaṭi, kavaṟṟumaṭi, nūlyāppu, tirukku, tēvāṅku, poṉṉeḻuttu, kuccari, tēvakiri, kāttūlam, iṟañci, veṇpotti, cempotti, paṇippotti eṉa 36 vakikaḷ aṭiyārkku nallārāṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷa. iviyallāmal, kampalam, kaliṅkam, kāḻakam, cīri, tukil, tūcu, paṭam mutaliya palavuḷa.
aṟukkappaṭuvataṉāl aṟuvi eṉṟum, tuṇikkappaṭuvataṉāl tuṇi eṉṟum, cavaṇṭiruppataṉāl cavaḷi yeṉṟum, āṭi pala potup peyar peṟum. cavaḷutal tuvaḷutal. meṉ kāṟṟilum āṭuvatu (acivatu) āṭi.
cavaḷi eṉṉum tamiḻccol, tjavaḷi eṉṟu teluṅkilum javaḷi eṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum eṭuppoliyuṭaṉ olikkappaṭuva tālum, tamiḻilum aṅṅaṉam iṟṟit tamiḻar olippatālum, vaṭacolleṉṟu tavaṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyil iccol illi.
caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
caḷicakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu
cantu cataṅki caḻakkāti īriṭattum
vantaṉavāṟ cammutalum vi.
eṉṉum mayilinātar eṭuttukkāṭṭuc ceyyuḷāl, cavaḷi eṉpatu tūya teṉcollātal aṟiyappaṭum.
akkālattu mellāṭi, pāmpuc caṭṭi pōṉṟum, mūṅki liṉ uṭpuṟa mīntōl pōṉṟum, puki virintāṟ pōṉṟum, nīrāvi paṭarntāṟ pōṉṟum, iḻi yōṭiyatu teriyāmalum, pūttoḻiluṭaṉ nuṇṇitāy neyyap paṭṭiruntateṉṟu paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nōkku nuḻikallā nuṇmiya pūkkaṉintu
 aravuri yaṉṉa aṟuvi  (porunar. 82-83)
pāmpuri yaṉṉa vaṭiviṉa kāmpiṉ
 kaḻipaṭu coliyiṉ iḻiyaṇi vārā
 oṇpūṅ kaliṅkam  (puṟam 383 : 9 : 11)
kāmpucolit taṉṉa aṟuvi  (ciṟupāṇ. 236)
pukivirin taṉṉa poṅkutukil
 āvi yaṉṉa avirnūṟkaliṅkam  (perumpāṇ. 469)
kaṇṇuḻi kallā nuṇṇūṟ kiviṉi
 vaṇṇa aṟuviyar  (maṇi. 28 : 52 - 3)
iḻimaruṅ kaṟiyā nuḻinūṟ kaliṅkam  (malipaṭu. 561)
 nīlak kaccip pūvā rāṭi  (puṟam. 274)
pōtuviri pakaṉṟip putumala raṉṉa
 akaṉṟumaṭi kaliṅkam  (puṟam. 393)
tirumala raṉṉa putumaṭik koḷīi  (puṟam. 390.)
ikkālak kāñcipurap paṭṭuc cēli pōṉṟē, akkālap paṭṭāṭiyum muṉṟāṉi yōrattil aḻakiya maṇi pōṉṟanūṉ muṭiccukkaḷi yuṭiyatā yiruntatu.
koṭṭik kariya paṭṭuṭi nalki  (porunar. 155).
akkālattil, uyarkuṭi āṭavar vēṭṭiyum mēlāṭiyum, uyarkuṭip peṇṭir cēliyum kaccum (iravikkiyum), aṇintiruntaṉar. tāḻkuṭi yāṭavar vēṭṭiyoṭu tali muṇṭum, tāḻkuṭip peṇṭir kacciṉṟiccēli muṉṟāṉi yālaminta māryāppum, koṇṭiruntaṉar. ūrāṭcit talivarum periyōrum mēlāṭiyoṭu vaṭṭat talip pākiyum aṇintiruntaṉar.
aracarkkum araciyal atikārikaṭkum araṇmaṉi aluvalarkkumē, caṭṭiyaṇiyum urimiyiruntatu. caṭṭi uṭampiṟkup pi pōṉṟiruppatāl, meyppi eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
nuṇviṉik kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara
meyppi pukku viyaṅkunaṭic celaviṟ
kikkōṟ kollaṉi  (cilap. 16 : 106 - 8).
īcaṉuṅ kaṟṟuccolvōr piṉvara vikaliṟ kūṭit
tēcuṭic caṭṭi cātti
eṉṉum perum paṟṟap puliyūr nampi kūṟṟu piṟkālattatēṉum, caṭṭi eṉṉum col muṟkālattatē. kañcuka mākkaḷ eṉṉum cilappatikārac coṟku caṭṭiyiṭṭa piratāṉikaḷ eṉṟu aruñcollurikārar varintiruttal kāṇka. pēṭik kōlat tārum aracakulap peṇṭirum, vaṭṭuṭi eṉṉum oru vakic ciṟappuṭiyi, ariyiṉiṉṟu muḻantāḷaḷavāka akkālattaṇin tiruntaṉar. (maṇi. 3 : 122; peruṅ 3. 4 : 122).
vēṭar, ariyiṉiṉṟu aṭit toṭiyaḷavāka, kāḻam eṉṉum oru vaki yuṭiyi aṇintiruntami kāḻamiṭṭa kuṟaṅ kiṉaṉ eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl (kamparā. kaṅkip. 32) teriya varukiṉṟatu. atu callaṭam pōṟ kuṟukiya arikkāṟ caṭṭi pōlum!
veyiṟ kālattilum vaṟaṇṭa iṭattilum cellum veḷip pōkkarum vaḻip pōkkarum, illaṟattārāyiṉ toṭutōl eṉṉum ceruppum, tuṟaviyarāyiṉ mitiyaṭi eṉṉum pātak kuṟaṭum, aṇintu ceṉṟaṉar.
caṭṭiyiṭṭum mēṉāṭṭāṟpōṟ pātakkūṭu (Boots) aṇintum kāṭṭuvaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟatākap perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaṇikar, veḷināṭṭār pōl terikiṉṟatu.
aṭiputi yaraṇam eytip paṭampukku
................................................................................................
uṭampiṭit taṭakki ōṭā vampalar  perumpāṇ. (69 - 76).
aṭiputiyaraṇam = aṭiyi maṟikkiṉṟa pātakkāppu. paṭam = caṭṭi.
5. aṇi
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, ciṟanta vakiyil virumpiya aḷavu aṇikalam ceyvataṟkēṟṟa poṉ, veḷḷi, muttu, maṇi mutaliya karuvikaḷum tērnta poṟkollarum, aṇipavarum, iruntaṉar.
ikkālattum, ulakil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭikkumiṭam āttirēliyāvum teṉṉāppirikkāvumākum. ivi muḻukip pōṉa paḻantamiḻakamāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattic cērntavi. ātalāl, kumari nāṭṭilum tamiḻarkku ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭittirukka vēṇṭum.
kumarik kaṇṭam muḻukiya piṉpum, koṅku nāṭṭil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭittatu. atu koṅkup poṉ eṉak kalveṭṭiṟ ciṟappāyk kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟu taṅkac curaṅkamuḷḷa kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) koṅku nāṭṭic cērntatē. annāṭṭil tōṉṟum kāviriyāṟu, paṇṭināḷiṟ poṉ maṇalik koḻittataṉāl poṉṉi yeṉappeṟṟatu. koṅku nāṭu paṇṭit tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyākum.
maturiyilirunta veḷḷiyampalamum tilliyilirunta poṉṉampalamum, paṇṭit tamiḻakattiṉ veḷḷi poṉ vaḷattik kāṭṭum.
mutaṟkālattil ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭittataṉāl poṉṉālēyē kācaṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, kāciṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi, tātu (ulōka) vakikaṭkum poṉ eṉpatu potup peyarāyiṟṟu veṇpoṉ = veḷḷi, cempoṉ = cempu, karumpoṉ = irumpu. poṉ = tātu (Metal). itaṉāl, tamiḻakattil irumpuk kālattiṟku muṉ poṟkālam oṉṟu iruntatō eṉa aiyam eḻukiṉṟatu.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulavar cilarkku aḷikkappaṭṭa poṟparicilē, akkālattup poṉvaḷattik kāṭṭap pōtumāṉatu.
kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral mītu patiṟṟuppattiṉ 4ām pattip pāṭi, nāṟpatu nūṟāyiram poṉṉum avaṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamum peṟṟār. poṉ eṉpatu poṟkācu.
kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār, āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ mītu patiṟ. 6ām pattip pāṭi, aṇikalaṉuk keṉṟu oṉpatu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum peṟṟār.
kapilar, celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ mītu patiṟ. 7ām pattip pāṭi, nūṟāyiram poṟkācum avaṉ mali mītēṟik kaṇṭu koṭutta nāṭum peṟṟār.
aricil kiḻār, takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēralirumpoṟi mītu patiṟ. 8ām pattip pāṭi, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram poṟkācum āṭciyurimiyi maṟuttu amiccurimiyum peṟṟār.
peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār, kuṭakkō iḷañcēra lirumpoṟi mītu patiṟ. 9ām pattip pāṭi, muppattīrāyiram poṟkācum pavavakip paricilum peṟṟār.
kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār, karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ mītu paṭṭiṉappāliyip pāṭi, patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ peṟṟār.
patiṟṟup pattil mutaṟpattum 10ām pattum iṉṟiṉmiyāl, avaṟṟip pāṭiya pulavar peṟṟa paricum teriyavilli.
mātavipōl āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiya nāṭakak kaṇikiyiṉ māli vili āyirat teṇ kaḻañcu poṉṉāka matikkap paṭṭiruntatu.
kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar kiviṉi muṟṟiya
nuṇviḷik kollar  (cilap. 16 : 105 -6)
mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiram kaṭṭiya
pattik kēvaṇap pacumpoṟ kuṭiccūl
cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉi yellām
poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ nōkkik
kōpperun tēvik kallati yiccilampu
yāppuṟa villi yeṉa  (cilap. 16 : 117 - 122).
palavuṟu kaṇṇuḷ cilakōl avirtoṭi  (kalit. 85 : 7)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷ akkālattu aṇikala viṉittiṟattik kāṭṭum.
malipayanta maṇiyum kaṭaṟupayanta poṉṉum
kaṭalpayanta katirmuttamum  (puṟam. 377 : 16 - 7).
eṉpatu tamiḻakattiṉ poṉ maṇi muttu vaḷattik kāṭṭum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭic cērnta kīḻikkaṭal muttu, toṉṟu toṭṭu ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟatu.
viḷintu mutirnta veṇmuttiṉ
ilaṅkuvaḷi iruñcērik
kaṭkoṇṭik kuṭippākkattu
naṟkoṟkiyōr nacipporuna  (maturik. 135 - 138)
maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum
koṟkiyam peruntuṟi muttiṉ aṉṉa  (akam. 27 : 8-9).
ivartiri tanta īrṅkatir muttam
kavarnaṭip puravik kālvaṭut tapukkum
naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟki muṉṟuṟi  (akam. 130 : 9 - 11)
viṉinavil yāṉi viṟaṟpōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ
pukaḻmali ciṟappiṟ koṟki muṉṟuṟi
avirkatir muttamoṭu valampuri corintu  (akam. 201 : 3-5).
ilaṅkirum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki
valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉtimiṟ paratavar
olitalip paṇilam ārppak kalleṉak
kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa iḻitarum  (akam. 350 : 10-13).
kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaluḻta laṉṟiyum īṇṭunīr
muttuppaṭu parappiṟ koṟki muṉṟuṟi.  (naṟ. 23 : 5 - 6)
teṉkaṭal muttum kuṇakaṭal tukirum  (paṭṭiṉap. 189).
aṇikalattiṟkuriya poṉ, iyaṟkiyākat tūyatum, puṭamiṭap paṭṭut tūyatākkap peṟṟatum, eṉa iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ōṭṭaṟṟa poṉ eṉappaṭum.
ōṭṭaṟṟa cempoṉ pōlē  (īṭu, 1, 10, 9)
māṟṟaṟiyāta ceḻum pacum poṉ
eṉṟu irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ kūṟiyatum ituvē.
puṭamiṭappaṭṭatu māṟṟuyarnta poṉ eṉappaṭum. pattari māṟṟut taṅkam ciṟantateṉak kūṟuvatu ulakavaḻakku. tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ itip pattumāṟṟut taṅkam eṉpar.
pattumāṟṟut taṅka mākkiyē paṇikoṇṭa  (ciṉmayā. 7)
aparañci eṉṉum āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟup poṉṉum uḷḷatāka nūlkaḷ kūṟum,
āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟiṉ aparañci  (maccapu. tārakācurava. 26).
ayōkkiyar aḻaku aparañcic cimiḻil nañcu.
eṉṟoru piṟkālap paḻamoḻiyum uḷatu.
poṉṉiṉ māṟṟurikkuṅ kal kaṭṭaḷikkal eṉṟum, urikkum kampi uriyāṇi eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
mātavi, viralaṇi (kāṉ mōtiram), pariyakam (kāṟcavaṭi), nūpuram (cilampu), pāṭakam, cataṅki, ariyakam (pāta cālam), kuṟaṅkuceṟi (kavāṉceṟi), viriciki, kaṇṭiki (māṇikka vaḷi), tōḷ vaḷi, cūṭakam, kivaḷi (poṉvaḷi), pariyakam (pācittā maṇi, kiccari), vālvaḷi (caṅka vaḷi, veḷḷi vaḷi), pavaḻavaḷi, vāḷip pakuvāy mōtiram, maṇi mōtiram, marakatattāḷ ceṟi (marakatak kaṭi ceṟi), caṅkili (maṟat toṭari), nuṇñāṇ, āram, kayiṟkaṭi yoḻukiya kōvi (piṉṟāli), intiranīlak kaṭippiṇi (nīlak kutampi), teyvavutti (tiruttēvi), valampuri, toyyakam (talippāḷi, pūrappāḷi), pullakam (teṉpalliyum vaṭapalliyum) eṉa 27 vaki aṇikaḷ aṇintiruntatākac cilappatikārak kaṭalāṭu kāti kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkāta vēṟu cila aṇikalam akkālattiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cūṭi allatu cūṭāmaṇi. peṇṭir arippaṭṭiki maṭṭum mēkali, kāñci, kalāpam, parumam, viriciki eṉa aivakip paṭṭiruntatu.
eṇkōvi kāñci eḻukōvi mēkali
paṇkoḷ kalāpam patiṉāṟu - kaṇkoḷ
parumam patiṉeṭṭu muppat tiraṇṭu
viriciki yeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu.
eṉpatu paḻañ ceyyuḷ.
ikkāla aṇikaḷē ēṟattāḻa aimpatu vakippaṭṭiruppatāl, mutaṉmiyāṉa aṇiyiṉiyē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṟit tiruttal vēṇṭum. ikkālattu arippaṭṭiki oṭṭiyāṇa mākum. iṅṅaṉamē cila aṇikaḷ vaṭivum  peyarum māṟiyuḷa.
ikkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻa nākarika makaḷir pōl, akkālattil ellāt tamiḻap peṇṭirum kātu vaḷarttiruntaṉar. kātu vaḷarkkumpōtu aṇivatu kutampiyum, vaḷarttapiṉ aṇivatu kuḻiyum kaṭippiṇiyu mākum. kutampi iṉṟu kuṇukku eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
koṭuṅkuḻi tuṟantu vaṭintuvīḻ kātiṉaḷ  (cilap. 4 : 50)
vaḷḷit tāḷpōl vaṭikā tivikāṇ
 uḷḷūṉ vāṭiya uṇaṅkal pōṉṟaṉa  (maṇi. 20 : 53-4).
eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
akkālattil āṭavarum aṇikalaṉ aṇivatu peruvaḻakku. vaṇikar kātiṟ kuṇṭalamum tōḷil (puyattil) kaṭakamum, mārpil maṇik kaṇṭikiyum, aṇintiruntaṉar. piṟa vakuppār kātiṟ kaṭukkaṉum, kiyiṟ kāppum aṇintiruntaṉar. kaṭukkaṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṟkālak kamalam (kammal) eṉṉum makaḷir kātaṇi eḻuntatu. maṟavar tōḷiṟ kaṭakamum kāliṟ kaḻalum aṇintiruntaṉar. kaḻal piṟkālattil veṇṭiyam eṉappaṭṭatu. ki mōtiram iru pālārkkum potuvām.
aracar, pāṇaṉukkup poṟṟāmariyum, avaṉ maṉiviyākiya pāṭiṉikku allatu viṟalikkup poṉṉaṇikalamum, paricilāka aḷittu vantaṉar. viṟali viṟal (cattuvam)paṭa āṭupavaḷ. viṟal uḷḷak kuṟippāl uṭampil tōṉṟum vēṟupāṭu. avi kaṇṇīr vārtal, meymmayir cilirttal mutaliyaṉa.
13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭi nāṭṭip pārkka vanta mārkkō pōlō eṉṉum veṉīcu nakara vaṇikar, cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ kaḻuttil vilimatikka voṇṇāta paṉmaṇi māliyum, mārpil viliyēṟap peṟṟa irumaṇiyāramum, tōḷil mūṉṟu paṉmuttak kaṭikiyum, kāliṟ kaḻalum, kāl viralkaḷil mōtiramum aṇintirunta tākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mitīr pacumpoṉ māṇṭa maṇiyaḻuttic
ceyta teṉiṉum cerupputtaṉ kāṟkēyām
eytiya celvatta rāyiṉum kīḻkaḷic
ceytoḻilāṟ kāṇap paṭum.  (nālaṭi. 347)
eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷāl, paṇṭi mūvēntariṉ ceruppu ettakiya tāyirukkum eṉpati uyttuṇaralām.
mayilukkut tōkipōlap peṇṭirkkuk kūntal aḻaku taruvatāl, akkālattup peṇṭir tam kūntali naṉṟāyp pēṇi, kuḻal, koṇṭi, paṉicci, curuḷ, muṭi eṉṉum aivakiyil aḻakupeṟa muṭittu vantaṉar. avi aimpāl eṉappaṭum. avaṟṟik kuḻal, aḷakam, koṇṭi, paṉicci, tuñci eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum; curuḷ, kural, aḷakam, tuñcu kuḻal, koṇṭi eṉac cāmuṇṭi tēvanāyakarum; ciṟitu vēṟupaṭak kūṟuvar. kūntalukku maṇam iyaṟki eṉṉum aḷaviṟku naṟumaṇam ūṭṭuvatu oru taṉik kaliyāyiruntatu. nāṟiṅ kūntal eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap. 10 : 43).
peṇṭir tam kaṇṇiṟku miyūṭṭuvatu peru vaḻakkamā yiruntatu.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōṟ koṇkaṉ
paḻikāṇēṉ kaṇṭa viṭattu.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷ (1285) itit terivikkum. miyūṭṭuvataṉ peruvaḻakkiṉāl, uṇkaṇ eṉṉun toṭarē miyuṇṭa kaṇṇik kuṟikkum.
irunōk kivaḷuṇkaṇ ṇuḷḷatu.  (kuṟaḷ. 1091).
miyūṭṭal kaṇṇiṟku aḻaku maṭṭumaṉṟik kuḷircciyum tarumeṉpatu, aṟiñar karuttu.
ikkālattu mēṉāṭṭup peṇṭir pōl, akkālat tamiḻap peṇṭirum tam utaṭukaṭkuc ceñcāyam ūṭṭi vantaṉar. atu avaraḻakic ciṟappittataṉāl peṇṭiraḻaki vaṇṇikkum iṭamellām ilavitaḻc cevvāy (cilap. 14 : 136) eṉṟum, kovvic cevvāy (tiruvāca. 6 : 2) eṉṟum, tuppuṟaḻ toṇṭic cevvāy (cīvaka. 550) eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum, kūṟuvatu pulavar vaḻakkamāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, uḷḷaṅkāṟkum cenniṟam peṟac cempañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭi vantami. alattaka mūṭṭiya aṭi (maṇi. 6 : 110) eṉpatāl aṟiyalām. alattakam, cempañcuk kuḻampu.
makaḷir mārpilum tōḷilum kātalar cantaṉak kuḻampāl variyum ōviyaṅkaḷum, irupālārum neṟṟiyilum kiyilum kuttuvikkum paccik kōlamum, ōviya vuṇarcciyiyaṉṟi nākarikattik kāṭṭā.
6. 	uṟiyuḷ
	uṟiyuḷ eṉpatu kuṭiyirukkum vīṭu.
kuṟiñcinila vāṇarāṉa kuṟavar kuṉṟavar iṟavuḷar mutaliya vakuppārum, pālinilavāṇarāṉa maṟavar eyiṉar vēṭar mutaliya vakuppārum, ili vēynta kuṭicikaḷilum kuṟṟil kaḷilum; mullinilavāṇarāṉa iṭiyar kūri vēynta ciṟṟilkaḷilum maruta nilac ciṟṟūrvāṇarāṉa uḻavar. maṇcuvark kūri vīṭukaḷilum; vatintārēṉum; marutanilap pērūrvāṇar ēntāṉa (vacatiyāṉa) paccic ceṅkaṟcuvark kūri vīṭukaḷilum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟcuvark kāri vīṭukaḷilum vāḻntuvantaṉar. cuṭṭa ceṅkal, cuṭumaṇ eṉṟum cuṭumaṭ palaki eṉṟum collappaṭṭatu. paccic ceṅkal maṭpalaki yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunakar varippil  (perumpāṇ. 405).
cuṭumaṭ palakipala koṇarvittu  (periyapu. ēyarkōṉ. 49).
ciṟu ceṅkal iṭṭiki eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṇcorīi iṭṭiki tīṟṟu pavar  (paḻa. 108).
iṭṭitu = ciṟitu (kuṟaḷ. 478). iṭṭimi = ciṟumi (tivā,). iṭṭiya = ciṟiya (aiṅkuṟu. 215).
iṭṭiki eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil iṣṭikā eṉṟu tirintu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu, ceṅkal eṉṟu maṭṭum poruḷpaṭum.
ciṟiyatum periyatum uyarntatum tāḻntatum allātu naṭu nikarttāṉa uṟiyuḷ, kuṭi, maṉi, il, vīṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. illam, vaḷamaṉi, māḷiki, nakar eṉpaṉa, peruñcelvar vāḻum ciṟanta uṟiyuḷik kuṟikkum. aracar vāḻum māḷiki araṇ peṟṟirukkumātalāl, araṇmaṉi yeṉap peṟum; aracaṉ maṉi eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kōyil eṉṟum collappeṟum.
kuṭi, nakar, māḷiki eṉṉum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
mēṉiliyuḷḷa vīṭu māṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. atu ulaka vaḻakkil māṭivīṭu eṉavum mettivīṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. irunili mutal eḻunili vari akkālattu māṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉakil āvivimmum eḻunilimāṭañ cērntum (cīvaka. 2840).
ovvoru māṭamum allatu māḷikiyum, cuṟṟuc cuvar, mukamaṇṭapam, talivācal, iṭikaḻi, (naṭi), muṉkaṭṭu, uḷmuṟṟam, piṉkaṭṭu, kūṭam, aṭukkaḷi (camiyalaṟi), puḻikkaṭi (kollippuṟam), maṉikkiṇaṟu, kuḷippaṟi, calakkappuri (kakkūcu), cālakam allatu aṅkaṇam eṉṉum pakutikaḷiyuṭiyatāyiruntatu. mēṉiliyil nilāmuṟṟa miruntatu.
vakipeṟa eḻuntu vāṉam mūḻkic
 cilkāṟ ṟicikkum palpuḻi nalil
eṉṉum maturikkāñci aṭikaṭku (357 -8).
maṇṭapam kūṭam tāykkaṭṭu aṭukkaḷi eṉṟāṟ pōlum peyarkaḷip peṟutaliṉ, vakipeṟa veḻunteṉṟār eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappuri varintiruttal kāṇka:
vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku - aṅku. o.nō : vaḷi - aḷi. aṅkutal = vaḷital, cāytal, aṅku - aṅkaṇam. o. nō : cāykaṭi - cākkaṭi.
kāṟṟu varumvaḻi, kālatar, cāḷaram, palakaṇi eṉṉum peyarkaḷip peṟṟiruntatu.
kāri vīṭukaḷ ucciyil ōṭu vēyntatum maccup pāviyum iruvakiyā yiruntaṉa. ōṭum, ilakkiya vaḻakkil cuṭumaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu.
cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ
 muṭiyara coṭuṅkum kaṭimaṉi
eṉpataṟku (cilap. 14 : 146-7), ōṭu vēyātu poṟṟakaṭu vēynta maṉi eṉṟu aruñcollurikārar ōr uri varintiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. muṭivēntarum virumpum nāṭakak kaṇikiyar maṉikaḷātaliṉ, poṟṟakaṭu vēyntatākavum iruntirukkalām. kāñci nakariṟ palar kūṭutaṟkuriya potu ampalamum poṉṉāl vēyap paṭṭiruntatāka maṇimēkali kūṟutal kāṇka.
cāliyuṅ kūṭamum tamaṉiyap potiyilum  (maṇi. 28 : 66)
maḻitōyum uyarmāṭattu
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉappāli (145) yaṭiyum,
māṭam aḻintakkāl maṟṟum eṭuppatōr
 kūṭam marattiṟkut tuppākum.
eṉṉum paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷum (71) māṭattiṉ perumiyi uṇarttum.
7. 	ūrtiyum pōkkuvarattum
	ūrtal ēṟic cellutal, ūrti ēṟic celluṅ karuvi.
	nilavūrtikaḷ aṇikam, vaṇṭi, vilaṅku eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
	aṇikam, civiki, pallakku, mēṉā, capparam eṉpaṉa.
civiki, iruvar kāvic cellum kūṇṭup pallakku. pallakku eṇmarukkuk kuṟiyātu tōḷil tāṅkic cellum tiṟanta taṇṭiyap paṭip pallakku. capparam uruḷiyiṉṟi oṟṟit taṭṭuḷḷa cappi (moṭṭi)t tēr.
civiki eṉpatu cipikkā eṉṟum, pallakku eṉpatu paryaṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. capparam iṉṟu teyvap paṭimiyik koṇṭu cellavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum.
vaṇṭi : cakaṭam, kūṭārappaṇṭi, kollāppaṇṭi, viyam, pāṇṭil, vaṅkam, tēr eṉpaṉa. cakaṭam potuvakiyāṉa māṭṭu vaṇṭi, kūṭārap paṇṭi kūṇṭu vaṇṭi. kollāp paṇṭi ciṟanta kāḷikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatum perumakkaḷ ēṟic celvatumāṉa nākarikak kūṇṭu vaṇṭi, viyam iru kutiri pūṇṭiḻukkum tēr pōṉṟa vaṇṭi. pāṇṭil īruruḷiyuḷḷa kutiri vaṇṭi. vaṅkam paḷḷiyōṭa vaṇṭi : atu paḷḷi yōṭam pōṉṟatu. tēr ikkālat tēr pōṟ ciṟiyatu.
vilaṅku : kāḷi, kutiri, kōvēṟu kaḻuti, yāṉi, oṭṭakam eṉpaṉa.
nīrūrtikaḷ : puṇi, parical, kaṭṭumaram, ōṭam, ampi, timil, paḵṟi, tōṇi. paṭaku, nīrmāṭam (paḷḷiyōṭam) nāvāy (kappal) eṉpaṉa. nāvāy, vaṅkam, kappal eṉpaṉa ciṟitu vēṟu paṭṭaviyāyumirukkalām.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilum vaṭa nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa pērūrkaṭkum kō nakar kaṭkum, taṭivaḻi eṉṉum peruñcālikaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. iccālikaḷ kāṭukaḷiṟ kūṭum kavartta iṭaṅkaḷil, avvan nāṭṭu vēntaṉiṉ viṟpaṭikaḷ, vaṇikac cāttukaṭku vaḻippaṟikkum kaḷvarālum koḷḷikkārarālum poruṭ cētamum āṭcētamum nērāvāṟu iravum pakalum kāttu niṉṟaṉa.
uṭampiṭit taṭakki yōṭā vampalar
.............................................................................................
aṇarccevik kaḻutic cāttoṭu vaḻaṅkum
ulkuṭip peruvaḻik kavali kākkum
villuṭi vippiṉ viyaṉkāṭ ṭiyaviṉ  (perumpāṇ. 76-82).
eṉṟu kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār toṇṭi nāṭṭip paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka.
nāṭu kippaṟṟaṟkum vāṇikattiṟkum ayalnāṭu pārttaṟkum, aracarum vaṇikarum potu makkaḷum cellak kūṭiya nāvāy eṉṉum peruṅkalaṅkaḷ, kīḻkaṭalilum mēlkaṭalilum aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉa.
vāṉiyinta irumunnīrp
pēeniliiya irum pavvattuk
koṭumpuṇari vilaṅkupōḻak
kaṭuṅkāloṭu karicēra
neṭuṅkoṭi miciyitiyeṭut
tiṉṉiciya muracamuḻaṅkap
poṉmalinta viḻuppaṇṭam
nāṭāra naṉkiḻitarum
āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
maḻimuṟṟiya malipuriyat
tuṟimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkit
teṇkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelliṉ
ūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava.
eṉṟu maturikkāñci (75-88), vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ cāli (cāvaka)t tīvik kippaṟṟiyati, avaṉ vaḻit tōṉṟalāṉa taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ mītu ēṟṟikkūṟutal kāṇka.

malittāramum kaṭaṟṟāramum
talippeytu varunarkkīyum

eṉpatu vaṇikam paṟṟiyatu.

vaṅkam pōkum vaṇikar tammuṭaṉ
taṅkā vēṭkiyiṉ tāṉum celvuḻi
eṉpatu, (maṇi. 16 : 4-12) ayal nāṭu kāṇac ceṉṟami paṟṟiyatu.
8. vāḻkki vaki
ulaka vāḻkki, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam taṉi vāḻkki eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
oru kaṟpuṭip peṇṇi maṇantu illattiliruntu aṟañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki illaṟamākum. ulakap paṟṟi yoḻittu vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvatu tuṟavaṟamākum. maṇañ ceyyātu ulakap paṟṟōṭu taṉiyāyiruppatu taṉi vāḻkki (Celibacy) ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ illaṟattiyē ciṟantatākak koṇṭaṉar tamiḻar. iṟivaṉ makkaḷi āṇum peṇṇu māy paṭittiruppatē, avar kūṭi vāḻtaṟ poruṭṭē. illaṟattālum oruvar vīṭupēṟṟi aṭiya muṭiyum.
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkki yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 46).
aṟaṉeṉap paṭṭatē yilvāḻkki aḵtum
piṟaṉpaḻippa tillāyiṉ naṉṟu.  (kuṟaḷ. 49).
eṉṟār tamiḻ aṟanūlāciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar. ivaṟṟiyē,
illaṟa mallatu nallaṟa maṉṟu
eṉac curuṅkac coṉṉār piṟkālattu auviyār. āṭavarum peṇṭirum kūṭātu vāḻvatu aritāyiruppatuṭaṉ, tuṟavaṟat tiṟkum illaṟamē iṉṟiya miyāta tuṇiyākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkūṟiya mūvaki vāḻkkiyuḷ, ovvoṉṟum nallatum tīyatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. illaṟattil orē maṉiviyuṭaṉ vāḻvatu nallatu; pala maṉiviyaruṭaṉ allatu pala peṇ ṭirik kātalittu vāḻvatu tīyatu. tuṟavaṟattil uṇmiyā yiruppatu nallatu; uḷḷoṉṟum puṟam poṉṟumāyiruppatu tīyatu; atu kūṭāvoḻukkam eṉappaṭum. taṉi vāḻkkiyil oru peṇṇiyum kātaliyā tiruppatu nallatu; maṟivāyk kātalittoḻukuvatu tīyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ nalla vaṟṟiyē tamiḻ mēlōr pōṟṟiṉar.
ulaka vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇiyāyiruppatāl maṉivikku vāḻkkit tuṇivi eṉṟu peyar.
illaṟam
maṇap paruvam vanta piṉpē, palavakiyilum otta ōr iḷiña ṉum iḷiñiyum, tāmākak kūṭiyō tam peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭappaṭṭō, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāki vāḻvatu, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻa marapu. maṇamāṉamikku aṭiyāḷamāka, maṉiviyiṉ kaḻuttil tāli eṉṉum maṅkalavaṇi aṇiyap peṟum.
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum eṅṅaṉam kūṭiyiruppiṉum kātal avarkku iṉṟiyamiyātatākum kātal eṉpatu iṟakkum variyum oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāmi. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭip paṭṭa kātal, kāmam eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟum. accol iṉṟu peṇṇāci eṉṉum tīya poru ḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
kāmatti orutalik kāmam, irutalik kāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉa mūvakiyāy vakuttaṉar muṉṉōr. ōr āṭavaṉum peṇṭumākiya iruvaruḷ, oruvar maṭṭum kātalippatu oru talik kāmam; yārēṉum oruvar neṟi tavaṟik kātalippatu poruntāk kāmam. ivaṟṟuḷ irutalik kāmamē ciṟantatākavum neṟippaṭṭatākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. peṟṟōrum maṟṟōrumiṉṟit tāmākak kūṭuvatellām, perumpālum irutalik kāmamākavē yirukkum.
kāmatti akapporuḷ eṉṟum, orutalik kāmattik kikkiḷi eṉṟum, irutalik kāmatti aṉpiṉ aintiṇi eṉṟum; poruntāk kāmattip peruntiṇi eṉṟum ilakkaṇam kūṟum. aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyē neṟippaṭṭatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl atiyē akam eṉṟu ciṟappittum, ēṉiyiraṇṭiyum akappuṟam eṉṟu iḻittum, kūṟuvar ilakkaṇiyar.
kikkiḷi, kuṟippu eṉṟum maṇam eṉṟum iru vakiyāyc collappaṭum. oruvaṉ kāmavuṇarcciyillāta oru ciṟumiyiṭam allatu kātalillāta oru peṇṇiṭam, cila kātaṟkuṟippuc coṟkaḷi maṭṭum tāṉē colli yiṉpuṟutal kikkiḷik kuṟippām. peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭap peṟṟa kaṇavaṉ maṉivi yaruḷ, yārēṉum oruvar kātalillāmalē icintiruppiṉ atu kikkiḷi maṇamām. kikkiḷi orupakkak kātal. ki pakkam. kiḷi kātal.
oru peṇṇi maṇavuṟavu muṟi tappiyō, valintō, ēmāṟṟiyō, tūkka niliyilō, nōy niliyilō, iṟanta piṉpō pūppu niṉṟa piṉpō kūṭuvatum, taṉpāloṭum vilaṅkoṭum kūṭuvatum, peruntiṇiyām. itu iṅṅaṉam palavakiyāyp perukiyiruppatāṟ peruntiṇi yeṉap paṭṭatu. tamiḻukkuc ciṟappāṉa poruḷilakkaṇam āriya varukikku eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē amintu viṭṭataṉāl, āriya voḻukka nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattuḷ tāṉ oṉṟē nālvaki peṟṟatiṉāl peruntiṇi yeṉap paṭṭateṉpatu, kālamalivum nūṉ malivuṅ kalanta peruvaḻuvām.
peṟṟōr ceytu vikkum maṇam, pēccu maṇamum aruñceyal maṇamum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. maṇavāḷap piḷḷi vīṭṭār pōyk kēṭka, peṇ vīṭṭār icintu peṇ koṭuppatu. pēccu maṇam; peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr kuṟitta ōr aṟaviṉiyō maṟaviṉiyō ceytu peṇṇik koḷvatu aruñceyal maṇam. paṇṭit tamiḻakattil kollēṟu taḻuvi ataṟkuriya peṇṇi maṇappatu; mulli nila vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. maṇamāṉa aṉṟē maṇamakkaḷ kūṭuvar.
koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟik kātalar tāmākak kūṭum kūṭṭam, maṟivākak toṭaṅkuvatum veḷippaṭiyāyt toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. maṟivāṉa kūṭṭam kaḷavu eṉṟum, veḷippaṭiyāṉa kūṭṭam kaṟpu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kaḷavu perumpālum irumātattiṟkuṭ paṭṭēyirukkum atu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ kaṟpām. kaṟpellām karaṇam eṉṉum tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅkōṭum pantalaṇi, maṇamuḻā, vāḻttu, varici uṟṟāruṭaṉ uṇṇum uṇṭāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum kūṭiya maṇaviḻāvoṭu toṭaṅkum. kaḷavuk kālattil kūṭṭam taṭippaṭiṉum, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr piṟarkkup peṇ koṭukka iciyiṉum, kātalaṉ kātaliyik kūṭṭik koṇṭu vēṟṟūr ceṉṟuviṭum uṭaṉ pōkkum uṇṭu. avar tirumpi vantapiṉ, kātalaṉ vīṭṭilēṉum kātali vīṭṭilēṉum vatuvi eṉṉum maṇaviḻā nikaḻum.
iṉi, kaḷavukkālattil kūṭṭam taṭippaṭuvatāl, kātali taṉ kātalaṉik kāṇappeṟāmal maṉam varunti mēṉi vēṟupaṭumpōtu, peṟṟōr vēlaṉ eṉṉum mantirakkāraṉi varavaḻittu tam makaḷ nōykkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam) viṉavuvatum, avaṉ atu murukaṉāl nērntateṉṟu kūṟi, veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṟiyum kaḷḷum vilāppuṭikka vuṇṭu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉṉum kūtti nikaḻtti annōyip pōkku vatāka naṭippatum uṇṭu. aṉṟu kātali nērākavō taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavō, taṉ peṟṟōriṭam uḷḷatic colli viṭuvāḷ. atu aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉappaṭum. taṉ kātalaṉaṉṟi vēṟu yārkkum taṉṉip pēciṉum, kātali aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ; ataṉpiṉ kātalaṉukku maṇañ ceytuvikkap peṟu vāḷ. maṇamakaṉ allatu avaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaḷukkup paricam koṭuppar. maṇamakaṉ paricam peṟum anākarika māṉaṅkeṭṭa āriya iḻivaḻakku akkālattilli.
tamiḻap peṇṭir kaṟpiṟ ciṟantavarātaliṉ, oruvari maṇantapiṉ allatu kātalitta piṉ vēṟoruvariyum kaṉavilum karutuvatilli; vēṟu evariyēṉum maṇakka nēriṉ, uṭaṉē uyiri viṭṭu viṭuvar.
kātalar kūṭum kūṭṭam, uṭampāṟ kūṭuvatum uḷḷattāṟ kūṭuvatum eṉa iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu meyyuṟu puṇarcci eṉṟum, piṉṉatu uḷḷappuṇarcci eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirkku iraṇṭum oṉṟē. itaṉālaṉṟō, tilakavatiyammiyār tamakkup pēcap peṟṟirunta kalippakiyār pōrk kaḷattiṟ paṭṭapiṉ iṟakkat tuṇintatum, ataṉ piṉ tam oṟṟik koru tampiyār tirunāvukkaracariṉ poruṭṭu uyir tāṅkiyatum, iṟuti vari maṇavātiruntatum, eṉka.
(āriyar varumuṉ) karaṇam eṉṉum tāli kaṭṭuc caṭaṅki, ūrt talivaṉ, kuṭi mutiyōṉ, maṅkala mutu peṇṭir, kulap pūcāri mutaliyōr naṭatti vittaṉar.
paṇṭiyaracarum peruñcelvarum perumpālum ciṟṟiṉpa vuṇarcci ciṟantu, pala tēviyariyum kāmak kiḻatti iṟparatti kātaṟparatti mutaliyōriyum koṇṭiruntami yāl, ōruyirum īruṭalumāṉa irutalik kāma iṉpa vāḻkki, pūtapāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōṉṟa oru cila aracak kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭiyum, uḻavarum iṭiyarum pōṉṟa potu makkaḷiṭiyum. pula makkaḷiṭiyumtāṉ perumpālum iruntu vantatu.
maṭaṅkaliṟ ciṉii maṭaṅkā vuḷḷat
taṭaṅkāt tāṉi vēntar uṭaṅkiyin
teṉṉoṭu porutum eṉpa avari
āramar alaṟat tākkit tēro
ṭavarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṉ ciṟanta
pērama ruṇkaṇ ivaḷiṉum pirika
eṉṟu pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ pakivari nōkkik kūṟiya vañciṉamum (puṟam. 71), avaṉ iṟantapiṉ uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟiya avaṉ tēvi yāṭiya,
palcāṉ ṟīrē palcāṉ ṟīrē
celkeṉac collā toḻikeṉa vilakkum
pollāc cūḻccip palcāṉ ṟīrē

peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭ ṭīmam
numakkari tākuka tilla emakkem
peruntōṭ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa arumpaṟa
vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmari
naḷḷirum poykiyum tīyum ōraṟṟē.
eṉṉum pāṭṭum (puṟam. 246), aṟiñar uḷḷatti eṉṟum urukkun taṉmiya.
oruvaṉuṭiya maṉivi urimip peṇṇāyiṉum perumip peṇṇāyiṉum uḻuvaṟ peṇṇāyiṉum, mūvakiyum ūḻiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu muṉṉōr karutiṉar. tāramum kuruvum taliviti (āciriyaṉum maṉiviyum amivatu ūḻmuṟi) eṉṉum piṟkālap paḻamoḻiyum, eṅkē muṭipōṭṭu vittiruk kiṟatō aṅkētāṉ muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkac collum, “Marriage are made in heaven,” “Marriage and hanging go by destiny” eṉṉum āṅkilap paḻamoḻikaḷum iṅkuk karutattakkaṉa. vāḻkkit tuṇiyākiya maṉivikku ūḻt tuṇi eṉṟum peyar.
ammāṉ makaḷum akki makaḷumpōl, maṇakkakkūṭiya uṟavumuṟip peṇ urimip peṇ; uṟavumuṟi yiṉṟic celvak kuṭumpattiṉiṉṟu eṭukkum peṇ perumip peṇ; iraṇṭumiṉṟi oruvaṉ tāṉē kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kātalittu maṇakkum peṇ uḻuvaṟ peṇ pala piṟappākat toṭarntu maṉiviyāy varupavaḷ uḻuvaṟ peṇ. eṉpatu, piṟavit toṭar nampikkiyāḷar karuttu. uḻuvalaṉpip payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpu eṉpar iṟiyaṉār (kuṟun. 2). ūḻāl ēṟpaṭṭa āvali uḻuval eṉṟaṉar. itit teyvappuṇarcciyeṉṟum, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
mūvakip peṇkaḷuḷḷum uḻuvaṟ peṇṇi maṇappatē kulamata nilaccārpu kaṭantatākaliṉ, teyvattāl nērntatāka vitantu kūṟappeṟum.
kātalaṉ kaḷavuk kālattil taṉ kātaliyi nōkki, ulaka muḻuvatiyum peṟṟālum nāṉ uṉṉik kiviṭēṉ eṉṟu (kuṟun.300) uṟuti kūṟiyataṟku ēṟpa, kaṟpuk kālattil, nī toṭṭatu nañcāyiruntālum eṉakkuk tēvar amutamākum. (tol, kaṟpiyal, 5) eṉṟum, nī eṉakku vēppaṅkāyit tantālum atu tīñcuvik kaṟkaṇṭu pōl iṉikkum (kuṟun.166) eṉṟum, uṉ kūntalippōl naṟumaṇamuḷḷa malari nāṉ ulakil eṅkuṅ kaṇṭatilliyeṉṟum, (kuṟun.2) palapaṭap pārāṭṭi avaḷi mēṉmēlum ūkki iṉpuṟuttu vatu vaḻakkam.
kātal maṉiviyum, taṉ kaṇavaṉit teyvam pōṟ pēṇi, avaṉ iṭṭa cūḷi (āṇiyi) niṟivēṟṟāviṭattu ataṉāl avaṉukkut tīṅku nērātavāṟu teyvatti vēṇṭik koḷvatum, avaṉ cūḷ tappavilli yeṉpatum (kuṟun.87), taṉ talivaṉ kuṟṟattip piṟar eṭutturippiṉ ati maṟuttu avaṉip pukaḻvatum, (kuṟun. 3), taṉ kaṇavaṉum tāṉum oruṅkē iṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpuvatum (kuṟun. 57), vaḻakkam.
aracarum maṟavarum pōr ceyvataṟkum, muṉivarum pulavarum tūtupaṟṟiyum, vaṇikar poruḷīṭṭaṟkum, vēṟṟūrum vēṟṟu nāṭum cella nēriṉ, avar tirumpi varumvari avar maṉiviyar āṟṟiyiruppatum, cuvariṟ kōṭiṭṭu nāḷeṇṇi varuvatum, avar kuṟitta kālattil varāviṭiṉ virintu varumāṟu teyvatti vēṇṭuvatum, iyalpām.
kaṇavaṉukkuk kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyum, peṟṟōrukku aṟivuṭi makkaḷum, ciṟanta pēṟākak karutappaṭṭaṉar.
eṉṉoṭu porutum eṉpa avari
āramar alaṟat tākkit tēroṭu
avarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṉ ciṟanta
pēramar uṇkaṇ ivaḷiṉum pirika.
eṉṟu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, cēṇāṟu nallicic cēyiḻi kaṇava eṉṟu (patiṟ. 88), kuṭakkō iḷañcēral irumpoṟiyum, ceyirtīr kaṟpiṟ cēyiḻi kaṇava eṉṟu (puṟam. 3), pāṇṭiyaṉ karuṅki yoḷvāṭ perum peyar vaḻutiyum, pārāṭṭap peṟutalum kāṇka.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉum
tiṇmiyuṇ ṭākap peṟiṉ.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (kuṟaḷ. 54).
iṉi makkaṭ pēṟupaṟṟi,
paṭippuppala paṭittup palarō ṭuṇṇum
uṭipperuñ celva rāyiṉum iṭippaṭak
kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟuki nīṭṭi
iṭṭun toṭṭuṅ kavviyun tuḻantum
neyyuṭi yaṭicil meypaṭa vitirttum
mayakkuṟu makkaḷi yillōrkkup
payakkuṟi yillittām vāḻum nāḷē.
eṉṟu (puṟam. 188) pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampiyum,
poṉṉuṭiya rēṉum pukaḻuṭiya rēṉumaṟ
ṟeṉṉuṭiya rēṉum uṭiyarō - iṉṉaṭicil
pukkaḷiyum tāmarikkip pūnāṟum ceyyavāy
makkaḷiyīṅ killā tavar.
eṉṟu pukaḻēntip pulavarum (naḷaveṇpā, kalitoṭar. 63).
peṟumavaṟṟuḷ yāmaṟinta tilli yaṟivaṟinta
makkaṭpē ṟalla piṟa.
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum (kuṟaḷ. 61), kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
kaṇavaṉ tavaṟṟālō maṉiviyiṉ pētimiyālō, cila camiyaṅkaḷil avariṭip piṇakku nērvatuṇṭu. aṉṟu maṉivi ūṭik kaṇavaṉoṭu pēcātiruppāḷ. ūṭutal caṭivu koḷḷutal. atu kaṇavaṉāl eḷitāyt tīrkkappaṭum. atu caṟṟuk kaṭumiyāṉāl pulavi eṉappaṭum. atu kuḻantiyik kaṇavaṉ eṭuttuk koṇṭu pōyk koṭuppataṉālum, vīṭṭiṟku viruntiṉar vantiruppatālum, uṟaviṉarum naṇparum taliyiṭuvatālum, tīrkkappaṭum. pulavi muṟṟi viṭṭāl tuṉi eṉappaṭum. ati oruvarālum tīrkka muṭiyātu. nīṇṭa nāṭ ceṉṟu atu tāṉē taṇiyum. paḻakap paḻakap pālum puḷippatu pōl, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa kāmaviṉpam caṟṟuc cuvi kuṟiyumpōtu ati niṟittaṟkup pulaviyum vēṇṭu meṉṟum, atu uṇaviṟku uppiṭuvatu pōṉṟa teṉṟum, ūṭal uppuk kuṟivatum tuṉi uppu mikuvatum pōṉṟavi yeṉṟum, uppu mikiyāṟ cuvi keṭuvatupōl tuṉiyāl iṉpaṅ keṭumātalāl anniliyi aṭiyātavāṟu pulaviyit taṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟuvar.
uppamin taṟṟāṟ pulavi atu ciṟitu
mikkaṟṟāl nīḷa viṭal.  (kuṟaḷ. 1302).
peṇṭir ettuṇik kalvi kaṟṟavarāyiruppiṉum, uḻattiyarum iṭicciyarum maṟattiyarum kuṟattiyarum paṇṭamāṟṟup peṇṭirum kūliyāṭṭi yarum vēlikkāriyarum vaṟiyavarumā yiruntāloḻiya, maṇamāṉapiṉ, mūppaṭiyu maṭṭum, kaṇavarōṭum peṟṟōrōṭum aṇṇaṉ akki mārōṭum māmiyārōṭum pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭimārōṭumaṉṟi, vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyē taṉiyē cellap peṟār.
kaṇavaṉip pēṇutalum camiyal ceytalum piḷḷi vaḷarttalum kaṇavaṉillātapōtu vīṭṭik kāttalumē, peṇṭirkku iyaṟkiyāl allatu iṟivaṉāl vakukkap paṭṭa paṇiyeṉpatu, paṇṭit tamiḻar karuttu. maṇamāṉa peṇ vīṭṭilēyēyiruntu vēli ceyva taṉālēyē, avaḷukku illāḷ, illak kiḻatti, maṉivi, maṉiyāḷ, maṉiyāṭṭi, maṉikkiḻatti, vīṭṭukkāri eṉṉum iṭam paṟṟiya peyarkaḷum, il, maṉi, kuṭi eṉṉum iṭavāku peyar kaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭiṟku allatu illattiṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷi yellām kaṇavaṉē īṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉpatum, ataṉāl maṉiviyum iḷa makkaḷum iṉpamāy vāḻavēṇṭumeṉ patum, paṇṭiyōr karuttu.
viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāḷnutal
maṉiyuṟi makaḷirk kāṭavar uyir.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ (135 : 1-2), itaṉip pulappaṭuttum.
ikkālattiṟ kālimutal māli vari āṭavar kaṭumiyāy uḻittum, kuṭumpattiṟkup pōtiya aḷavu poruḷ tēṭavō uṇavup poruḷ koḷḷavō muṭiyavilli. itaṉālēyē, peṇṭir veḷiyēṟi āciriyap paṇiyum araciyalaluvaṟ paṇiyum āṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ākavē, iṉṟu avar kaṭami iru maṭaṅkāyp perukiyuḷḷatu. innilimi makkaṭ perukkiyum uṇavut taṭṭiyum kāṭṭumēṉum, itaṟku aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam araciyal tavaṟē.
peṇṭirit taṉiyē vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷip pōkkāmikku iṉṉoru karaṇiyamuṇṭu. avar potuvāka āṭavarāl, ciṟappākak kāmukarāl, nukarccip poruḷākak karutappaṭum nilimi iṉṉum māṟavilli. āṭavari nōkka, avar meṉmiyar, valuvaṟṟavar. itaṉāl avarkku melliyal, aciyiyal, taḷariyal eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ilakkiyattil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tīyōrāl avarkkum avaruṟaviṉarkkum cētamum māṉak kēṭum nērāvaṇṇamē, avar tuṇiyō ṭaṉṟi veḷiyē aṉuppappaṭuvatilli. āṭavar nāṉmuḻa vēṭṭiyuṭukkum pōtu, peṇṭir patiṉeṇ muḻac cēli yaṇivatum, ik karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē.
ākavē, peṇṭiri veḷiviṭātiruppatu, avaruṭiya nalam pēṇalēyaṉṟi avaric ciṟippaṭuttalākātu. kaṭikaṭkum kōyiṟkum tiruviḻāviṟkum uṟaviṉar vīṭṭu maṅkala amaṅkala nikaḻccikaṭkum piṟa iṭaṅkaṭkum, tuṇiyoṭu pōy vara avarkku nirampa vāyppuṇṭu.
pūppaṭinta kaṉṉip peṇkaḷiyum takka tuṇiyiṉṟi veḷiyē viṭuva tilli.
kaṇavaṉ, poruḷīṭṭal paṟṟiyō tīyoḻukkam paṟṟiyō pirintiruk kumpōtu, kaṟpuṭi maṉivi taṉṉi aṇi ceytu koḷvatilli; maṅkalavaṇi tavira maṟṟavaṟṟi yellām kaḻaṟṟi viṭuvāḷ.
akkālattil, ilvāḻkkikkuriya aṟaṅkaḷiyum ceyvatilli.
aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇa rōmpalum
tuṟavōrk ketirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
viruntetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉi.
eṉṟu (cilap. 16 : 71-73), kaṇṇaki maturiyil taṉ kaṇavaṉi nōkkik kūṟutal kāṇka.
maṟumukam pārāta kaṟpeṉpatu kaṇavaṉ maṉivi yiruvarkkum potuvēṉum, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ pōṉṟa oru cilarē avvaṟattik kaṭippiṭitta āṭavarāvar; peṇṭirō paṟpallāyiravar. kātalilum āṭavar peṇṭirkku īṭākār. uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟutalum uṭaṉuyir viṭutalumē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. maṟumaṇañ ceyyāta kimmi niliyum peṇṭir ciṟappik kāṭṭum.
pulavar, aracar, vaṇikar, veḷḷāḷar ākiya vakuppukkaḷic cērnta peṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ iṟanta piṉ, eṭṭām nāḷ iṟutic caṭaṅkil maṅkalavaṇiyum piṟavaṇikaḷum nīkkappeṟuvar. atu tāliyaṟuppu eṉappaṭum. ataṉ piṉ veḷḷāṭiyaṇintu vēṟevariyum maṇavāmal tam eñciya kālattik kaḻippar. avar uyarkuṭip piṟantavar eṉappaṭuvar.
vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟi uḻavum kittoḻilum aṅkāṭi viṟpaṉiyum kūli vēliyum teruppaṇṭamāṟṟum ceyyum piṟa vakuppup peṇṭirellām, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ tāliyaṟuppiṉum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvar. atu aṟuttuk kaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. iṉi, cila vakuppuppeṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum pōtē tīrvi eṉṉum kaṭṭaṇattik koṭuttut tīrttuviṭṭu vēṟoruvaṉi maṇantu koḷvatum uṇṭu. atu, tīrttuk kaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. atu ettaṉi muṟiyum nikaḻum.
aracar pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟut taṟkoli ceytu koṇ ṭālum, pakivarāṟ kollappaṭṭālum, avar tēviyarum makaḷirum pakiyaracarkku aṭimiyākātavāṟu tīkkuḷittu iṟappatumuṇṭu.
kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum vari avaṉukku uṇmiyāṉa maṉiviyā yiruntu, avaṉ iṟantapiṉ vēṟoruvaṉi maṇappatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ maṟumaṇam ceyyā tiruppatum, kaṟpiṉ pāṟpaṭumēṉum, avaṟṟit tamiḻakam kaṟpeṉak koḷḷavilli. tamiḻakak kaṟpu ulakattilēyē tali ciṟantatākum.
kuṭumpat talivaṉ iṟantapiṉ, īmak kaṭaṉum iṟutic caṭaṅkum avaṉ putalvarāl, putalvaṉ illāviṭṭāl avaṉ uṭamikku urimi pūṇum uṟaviṉaṉāl, naṭattappeṟum. īmam eṉpatu cuṭali.
tuṟavaṟam
paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷiyār pōl ulaka vāḻkkiyiṉ niliyāmiyi uṇarntō, tāyumāṉavar pōl kuruviṉāl aṟivuṇarttap peṟṟō, civappirakācar pōl iyalpākavē illaṟattil veṟuppuk koṇṭō, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōl iḷamiyilēyē kaṭanta aṟivaṭintō, iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ pōl uṭaṉ piṟantārkkuk kēṭuvarātu taṭuttaṟ poruṭṭō, kōvala kaṇṇakiyar tantiyarum mātavi maṇi mēkaliyarum pōl kaṇṇaṉṉa uṟaviṉar neṭum pirivit tāṅka muṭiyāmalō, tuṟavu pūṇuvatu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamākum.
kuṭumpac caṇṭiyāṟ caṭivu koṇṭum, kuṭumpap poṟuppi nīkkik koḷḷavum, tuṟavu pūṇuvatumuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ciṟappuṭiyataṉṟu; piṉṉatu peruṅkuṟṟa mākum.
āṇṭi, paṇṭāram, aṭikaḷ, muṉivaṉ, cittaṉ eṉat tuṟaviyar palavakiyar. āṇṭi irappōṉ; paṇṭāram aṟivu nūlkaḷi nirampak kaṟṟa paṇṭitaṉ; aṭikaḷ uḷḷat tūymiyum āvik kuriya (Spiritual) ceytikaḷiṟ paṭṭaṟivum vāyntavar, paṭṭiṉat tārum tāyumāṉavarum irāmaliṅkarum pōl. immūvarum nāṭṭiliruppavar. muṉivarum cittarum mali yiliruppavar.
muṉivar allatu muṉivar ulakappaṟṟi muṟṟum veṟuttavar. muṉital, veṟuttal. muṉital, veṟuttal.
illaṟattāruḷ oruvaṉukku aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, tanti, tāy, tamiyaṉ eṉṟu aivakip periyōriruppatāl, avar aiṅkuravar eṉṟum; avaruṭ ciṟanta peṟṟōriruvarum irumutu kuravar eṉṟum; aḻikkappeṟuvar. kuravaṉ periyōṉ.
tamiḻar illaṟattil veṟṟi kaṇṭatu pōṉṟē tuṟavaṟattilum, aruḷuṭimi, pulāṉ maṟuttal, kaḷḷāmi, vāymi, vekuḷāmi, iṉṉāceyyāmi, kollāmi, avāvaṟuttal eṉṉum aṟuvaki yaṟaṅkaḷiyum; nōṉpu (tavam), meyyuṇartal (ōkam) eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻikaḷiyum, kaṭippiṭittu veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻat tuṟaviyaruḷ taliciṟantovar cittar. avar vāṉvaḻi iyaṅkutal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭu pāytal, tōṉṟi maṟital, maṇṇuḷ iruttal, uṇṇātu vāḻtal mutaliya pala āṟṟal kaḷi aṭintavar. tirumūlar oru cittar. paṭṭiṉat tārum irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷum, iṟutiyil cittanili aṭintatāka avar varalāṟu kūṟum.
nōṉpu (tavam) eṉpatu illaṟattārkkum tuṟavaṟattārkkum potuvām.

9. camayavoḻukkam
camayam eṉpatu matam. atu oruvakic camivik kuṟittalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camital nukarccikkup patamātal. arici cōṟākac camivatu uṇpataṟkup patamātal. peṇpiḷḷi maṅkiyākac camivatu maṇa nukarccikkup patamātal. ātaṉ (āṉmā) iṟiyaṭimiyākac camivatu, vīṭupēṟṟiṟku allatu iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷi yaṭivataṟkup patamātal.
camiyam eṉpatu vēḷiyik kuṟikkumpōtum, oru viṉikkup patamāṉa allatu takka kālanili eṉṉum poruḷatē. nalla camiyam, takka camiyam, ēṟṟa camiyam eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka. palavakic camivukaḷuḷḷum ātaṉ iṟiyaṭimi yākac camivatu taliciṟantatātalāl, camivu eṉṉum viṉi, ataṉiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. āyiṉum, poruḷ mayakkamillāvāṟu, cōṟu camivatu camiyal eṉṟum, peṇ camivatu camital eṉṟum, nēram camivatu camiyam eṉṟum, ātaṉ camivatu camayam eṉṟum, vēṟupaṭuttic collap peṟum.
camiyam eṉṉum col amiyam eṉṉum colliṉ mutaṉmiki (Prothesis). amital poruntutal allatu takutiyātal. nallatoṉṟu vāyppiṉ, amintu viṭṭatu eṉpar. amiyum nēram amiyam.
uyir mutaṟcoṟkaḷ coṉmutal meykaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum peṟṟuc coṟkaḷip piṟappittal, collākka neṟi muṟimi yām.
e-ṭu : uruḷ - curuḷ, ēṇ - cēṇ.
ami eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum am eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu tirintatākum. itaṉāl, camayam eṉṉum col tūya teṉ collātal teḷika. atu vaṭa colleṉṟu vaṭamoḻic coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyilum kuṟittiruppatu koṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
āriyar varumuṉpē, tamiḻar camayat tuṟiyilum tali ciṟantiruntaṉar. āyiṉum, ellārum anniliyaṭiyavilli. enta nākarika nāṭṭilum, taliyāyār iṭiyāyār kaṭiyāyār eṉṉum muttiṟattār irukkavē ceyvar.
ākavē, tamiḻar matamum, ciṟu teyva vaṇakkam, perun teyva vaḻipāṭu, kaṭavuḷ neṟi eṉa munnilippaṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ
(1)	teṉpulattār (iṟanta muṉṉōr).
(2)	naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷ.
(3)	pēykaḷ (pēy, pūtam, muṉi, caṭimuṉi, aṇaṅku (mōkiṉi), cūraramakaḷir mutaliyaṉa).
(4)	tīya vuyirikaḷ (pāmpu, cuṟā, mutali mutaliyaṉa).
(5)	iṭat teyvaṅkaḷ (āṟṟut teyvam, malit teyvam, kāṭṭut teyvam. nakart teyvam, nāṭṭut teyvam).
(6)	iyaṟkip pūtaṅkaḷ (kāṟṟum tīyum).
(7)	vāṉac cuṭarkaḷ (katiravaṉum tiṅkaḷum).
(8)	celvat teyvam (tirumakaḷ).
(9)	kalvit teyvam (nāmakaḷ allatu coṉmakaḷ).
(10)	pālvari teyvam (ūḻ vakuppatu).
perun teyvaṅkaḷ
ivi aintiṇit teyvaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟiñci - cēyōṉ
mulli - māyōṉ.
pāli - kāḷi.
marutam - vēntaṉ.
neytal - vāraṇaṉ.
cēyōṉ civantavaṉ. cēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarum ap poruḷatē. murukaṉ, vēlaṉ, kumaraṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum ivaṉuk kuṇṭu. civaṉ eṉpatu cēyōṉ eṉpataṉ ulaka vaḻakku vaṭivam. ippeyar vaṭivu vēṟupāṭṭik koṇṭu, piṟ kālattil āriyar orē teyvatti iraṇṭākkit tantiyum makaṉumākak kūṟi viṭṭaṉar. kumaraṉ eṉpataṟkum cēy eṉṉum kuṟukkat tiṟkum makaṉ eṉṟu tavaṟākap poruḷ koṇṭatē itaṟku aṭippaṭi.
civaṉ eṉṟu āriyat teyvam oṉṟumilli. civa eṉṉum col, nalla allatu maṅkala eṉṉum poruḷil, urut tiraṉukkum intiraṉukkum akkiṉikkum potuvāṉa aṭimoḻiyākavē  āriya vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
puṟakkaṇ kāṇa muṭiyātavaṟṟiyum neṭuntoliviluḷḷa vaṟṟiyum kaṇṭaṟiyum ōr aṟivukkaṇ pōṉṟa uṟuppu, kumari nāṭṭu makkaḷ neṟṟiyiliruntateṉṟum, ataṉālēyē avartam iṟivaṉukkum (civaṉukkum) oru neṟṟik kaṇṇip paṭittuk kūṟiṉareṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. veḷḷimali (kili) yirukkiyum kāḷiyūrtiyum civaṉik kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākak kāṭṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ talimiyamiccarum pirāmaṇarum ciṟanta civaṉaṭiyārum vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi vallunarumākiya māṇikkavācakar,
pāṇṭi nāṭē paḻampati yākavum  tiruvācakam, 2: 118
teṉṉā ṭuṭiya civaṉē pōṟṟi  (4:164)
eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭiyē civaneṟip piṟappiṭamākak kuṟittal kāṇka. iṉi, civaperumāṉ aṟupattu nāṉku tiruviḷiyāṭal kaḷiyum maturiyil aruḷic ceytatiyum, muttoḻil allatu aintoḻil tirunaṭattit tilliyil āṟṟuvatiyum, eṇmaṟac ceyalkaḷ ceyta iṭaṅkaḷ (aṭṭavīraṭṭam) tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruppatiyum , ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
māyōṉ kariyavaṉ. mā karuppu. māya kiruṣṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkkum itaṟkum yātoru toṭarpumilli. kaṇṇa pirāṉ piṟakkumuṉpē, kiruṣṇa eṉṉum col karuppu eṉṉum poruḷil irukku vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kiruṣṇa pakṣa (karumpakkam), kiruṣṇa sarppa (karumpāmpu) eṉṉum peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. kiruṣṇa eṉṉum coṟku vēta moḻiyil vērilli; tamiḻilēyē uḷḷatu.
kaḷ-karu-karuḷ= 1. karumi karuḷ taru kaṇṭattu... kiliyār   (tēvā.337, 4) 2. iruḷ (piṅ.) 3. kuṟṟam. karuḷ tīrvaliyāl  (cētupu. muttīrt. 5).
karuḷ = kruṣ. ḷakara meyyīṟu vētamoḻiyil ṣakara meyyīṟākat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: cuḷ-cuṣ (to dry) uḷ - uṣ, (to burn). 
māyōṉ, māl, viṇṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
māl = karumi, mukil, tirumāl.
viṇṭu = mukil, vāṉam, tirumāl,
viṇ = vāṉam, mukil, mēlulakam.
viṇ - viṇṭu.
āriya vētattil viṣṇu eṉṉum peyar katiravaṉik kuṟittatu. kāli eḻucciyiyum naṇpakaṟ celaviyum ēṟpāṭṭu (cāyuṅkāla) vīḻcciyiyumē mūvaṭiyāl (mūṉṟu eṭṭāl) ulaka muḻuvatiyum viṣṇu (katiravaṉ) aḷappatāka mutaliṟ koṇṭaṉar vēta āriyar.
kuṟiñci nilattār tam teyvatti maliyil tōṉṟum neruppiṉ kūṟākavum, mulli nilattār tam teyvatti vāṉattiṉiṉṟu poḻiyum mukiliṉ kūṟākavum, koṇṭaṉar. itaṉālēyē, civaṉukkut tīvaṇṇaṉ, antivaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tirumālukkuk 
kārvaṇṇaṉ, maṇivaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tōṉṟiṉa.
kāḷi karuppi. kaḷ- kaḷvaṉ = kariyavaṉ. kaḷ-kāḷ- kāḻ = karumi. kāḷ- kāḷam = karumi. kāḷ- kāḷi. kāḷi kūḷikaṭkut talivi. kūḷi pēy. pēy karuppeṉṟum iruḷ eṉṟum peyar peṟum. karuppi, karuppāy eṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarkaḷi, iṉṟum peṇṭirkkiṭuvatu peruvaḻakku. tāy eṉṉum poruḷil ammi (ammaṉ) aiyi eṉṟum, iḷiñi eṉṉum poruḷil kaṉṉi, kumari eṉṟum, veṟṟi tarupavaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil koṟṟavi (koṟṟavvi) eṉṟum, kāḷikkup peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum. aṅkāḷammi eṉpatu aḻakiya kāḷi yammi eṉṉum poruḷatu.
āriyar varumuṉ vaṭa intiyā muḻutum tiraviṭar paraviyirunta tiṉāl, vaṅka nāṭṭil kāḷik kōṭṭam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ = aracaṉ. mutaṟkālattil aracaṉum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ. ataṉāl, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉukkum kaṭavuṭkum, kōyil eṉṉum peyar araṇmaṉikkum teyvak kōṭṭattiṟkum, potuvāyiṉa. vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ vāṉavarkkum aracaṉāki vāṉulakat tiṉiṉṟu maḻiyip poḻivikkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu paṇṭi maruta nilamakkaḷ karutiyatāl, maḻit teyvatti vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
āriyar varumuṉpē, moḻi vēṟupāṭṭiṉāl vēntaṉukku vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta peyar intiraṉ eṉpatu. intiraṉ aracaṉ. ākavē iraṇṭum oru poruṭ coṟkaḷē. vēta āriyar intira vaṇakkatti, vaṭanāṭṭut tirāviṭarip piṉ paṟṟiyē mēṟkoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mēliyāriya nāṭukaḷil intira vaṇakkamē yiruntatilliyeṉṟu, mākacu mullar (Max Muller) kūṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
kaṭik kaḻakak kālam vari kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil naṭi peṟṟu vanta intira viḻāvum, tamiḻarvēntaṉ viḻāvē.
vāraṇaṉ = kaṭal teyvam. vāraṇam, kaṭal. kaṭalukku oru teyva miruppatākak karuti, ati neytal nilamakkaḷ vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
mutaṟkālat tiraviṭar vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu kirēkka nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ, vāraṇam eṉṉum col (Ouranos) eṉat tirintatu. appeyart teyvam mutaliṟ kirēkkarkkuk kaṭal teyvamākavēyiruntu, piṉpu, vāṉat teyvamāyiṟṟu. kirēkkat  tiṟku mika neruṅkiya moḻiyip pēcivanta kīḻiyāriyaruḷ oru pirivārāṉa vēta āriyar, kaṭaliyaṟiyāmal neṭukavum nilavaḻiyākavē vantatiṉāl, maḻit teyvattiyē varuṇā eṉa aḻittaṉar. avar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭarōṭum teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻarōṭum toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē, varuṇaṉik kaṭal teyvamākak karutat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, iṉṉum maḻikkāka varuṇaṉi vēṇṭuvatē pirāmaṇar vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇalulakamum (akat. 5) eṉṟu, vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum peyari vaṭamoḻi vaṭiviṟ kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum. atu vāraṇaṉ mēya ērmaṇa lulakamum eṉṟiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aintiṇit teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ, cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭum māyōṉ vaḻipāṭum piṟkālattil iruperuñ camayaṅkaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭavuḷ neṟi
ūrpēr kuṇaṅkuṟiyaṟṟu, maṉamoḻi meykaḷik kaṭantu eṅkum niṟintiruttal, ellām aṟintiruttal, ellām vallatātal, eṉṟumuṇmi, aruḷ vaṭivuṭimi, iṉpa niliniṟṟal, oppuyarviṉmi, mācumaṟuviṉmi ākiya eṇ kuṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiyatāy ellāvulakaṅkaḷiyum paṭittuk kāttaḻittuvarum oru param poruḷuṇṭeṉṟu nampi, ataṉi vaḻipaṭuvatē kaṭavuḷ neṟiyām. itu cittamatam eṉavum paṭum.
ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantiruppataṉālēyē iṟivaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. āriyar vanta piṉ, iccol mutaliṟ perunteyvaṅkaṭkum, piṉpu ciṟuteyvaṅkaṭkum, iṟutiyil makkaḷāṉa muṉivarkkum vaḻaṅki iḻivaṭin tuḷḷatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar tam nūṉmukattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu uruvamaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttē. tamiḻariṉ uruvavaṇakka mallāk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭi uḷiyiṭṭa kalliyum  eṭṭut ticiyum eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭaliyum aṅkiṅkeṉātapaṭi. paṇṇēṉ uṉakkāṉa pūci eṉṉum tāyumāṉavar pāṭaliyum nōkkiyuṇarka.
kaṭavuḷiyum maṟumiyiyum nampāta oru ciṟu kūṭṭattārum akkālat tiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, aṟiñar avarik kaṇṭittaṉar.
ulakattār uṇṭeṉpa tilleṉpāṉ viyat
talakiyā vikkap paṭum.  (kuṟaḷ. 850)
nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum
illi yeṉpōrk kiṉaṉā kiliyar  (puṟam. 29: 11-12)
maṟumiyoṉ ṟuṇṭō maṉappaṭṭa ellām
peṟumāṟu ceymmiṉeṉ pārē- naṟuneyyuḷ
kaṭṭi yaṭiyik kaḷivittuk kaṇcorīi
iṭṭiki tīṟṟu pavar.  (paḻamoḻi, 108).


10. toḻilkaḷ
1.  uḻavu
uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam
uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē  (puṟam. 18)
uḻuvār ulakattiṟ kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟā
teḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu  (kuṟaḷ. 1032)
irappōr cuṟṟamum purappōr koṟṟamum
uḻaviṭi viḷippōr  (cilap. 10: 151-2)
uḻavar. ātalāl, oru nāṭṭu vāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭi uḻavu eṉpatip paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷ uṇarntiruntaṉar.
uḻavut toḻiṟku iṉṟiyamiyātavi nilam, nīr, viti, erutu eṉṉum nāṉkām.
paṇṭit tamiḻar, aintiṇikaḷuḷ marutattiyum neytali yum meṉpula meṉṟum, kuṟiñciyiyum, mulliyiyum vaṉpula meṉṟum, vakuttiruntaṉar. marutanilatti nāṭu eṉṟum, maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷik kāṭu eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. kuṟiñci nilattil uḻattakka iṭatti ērkkāṭu eṉṟum, uḻat takātatik kottuk kāṭu eṉṟum pakuttaṉar. kuṟiñciyilum mulliyilu muḷḷa viḷinilaṅkaḷ kolli allatu puṉam eṉṟum, marutattiluḷḷa viḷinilaṅkaḷ cey eṉṟum, putuk kolli iti eṉṟum, paḻaṅ kolli muti eṉṟum, ciṟitu ceyyap peṟṟa cey puṉcey eṉṟum, naṉṟāyc ceyyappeṟṟa cey naṉcey eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
ceytal eṉpatu tiruttutal allatu paṇpaṭuttutal. puṉmi ciṟumi.
kolli eṉpatu vāṉavārik kāṭeṉṟum, puṉcey kiṇaṟṟup pāycca leṉṟum, naṉcey āṟṟu allatu ērip pāyccaleṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. eḷ koḷ mutaliyaṉa kollip payir;  kēḻvaraku cōḷam mutaliyaṉa puṉceyp payir; nel karumpu mutaliyaṉa naṉceyp payir.
naṉceykaḷuḷ, paḻamiyāṉatu paḻaṉam eṉṟum, pōraṭikkum kaḷamuḷḷatu kaḻaṉi eṉṟum collap peṟum. paṇṇi eṉpatu paṇṇappaṭṭatu (paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭatu) eṉṉum poruḷatēṉum, vaḻakkil kaḷamar allatu ceṟumar eṉṉum paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ kuṭiyiruntu vēli ceyyum periya vayaṟ parappiyē (Farm) kuṟikkum. cēṟuḷḷamiyāl ceṟu eṉṟum, vippup pōṉṟamiyāl vayal eṉṟum, naṉceykkup peyarkaḷuṇṭu.
puṉamāyiṉum puṉceyāyiṉum, paṇṭit tamiḻar mēṭṭu nilattiṟ payir ceyya virumpavilli. mēṭu cuval eṉṟum paḷḷam aval eṉṟum peyar peṟum. mēṭṭup puṉceyi uḻutavaṉum keṭṭāṉ, mēṉi miṉukkiyi maṇantavaṉum keṭṭāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
naṉceyp pācaṉattiṟku, āṟṟunīr illāviṭaṅkaṭkuk kaṇṇāṟukaḷum kālvāykaḷum veṭṭip pāycciṉar. atu iyalāviṭattu ērikaḷi veṭṭiṉar. ērt toḻiṟku utavuvatu ēri. kuḷippatu kuḷam. iṉṟu ēriyit tavaṟākak kuḷameṉpar oru cārār. iyaṟkiyāka uṇṭāṉa ēri allatu kuḷam poyki eṉappaṭum. mulli nilattiṟ puṉceyp pācaṉattiṟkuk kiṇaṟukaḷi veṭṭiṉar.
erutukaḷik koṇṭu kiṇaṟṟu nīri iṟikkum ēṟṟam kammāli eṉappaṭṭatu. am, nīr. am - kam = nīr. kam + āli = kammāli. āli cuṟṟi varuvatu. karumpāli eṉpati nōkkuka. ālutal āṭutal. mutaṟ kālattil erutukaḷ oru marat tūṇic cuṟṟi vantaṉa. kammāli eṉpatu iṉṟu kamali eṉṟum kavali eṉṟum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉṟum kamaliyāṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. iṉṟu erutukaḷ nērākac celvatāl kavali yōṭṭutal eṉṟum kūṟuvar. kamali yēṟṟattik kapili yēṟṟam eṉṟu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum, kapili (va) yeṉpatu kurāl eṉṉum āvaki. āvik kaṭṭi nīriṟippatu vaḻakkattiṟku māṟākum.
vitikkeṉṟu mutaṟ kāyppiyum ciṟanta maṇikaḷiyum otukki vittaṉar. akkālattu uḻavar poruḷīṭṭalik kuṟik koḷḷātu, uṇavu viḷittaliyē kuṟikkoṇṭu patiṉeṇ kūlaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ vakikaḷiyum, āṇṭu tōṟum viḷittu vantaṉar. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa nelvakikaḷ viḷikkap peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉavaṟṟi iṉṟu kaṇṇālum kāṇa muṭiyavilli; kātālum kēṭka muṭiyavilli. poṉ tiṉi, centiṉi, karuntiṉi eṉṉum mūvakiyuḷ, iṉṟu poṉṟiṉiyē kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. avari vakikaḷuṭ pala āṇṭutōṟum ovvoṉṟāy maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu āṭciyi naṭattupavarukkup pataviyik kākkavēṇṭu meṉ patēyaṉṟi, viti vakikaḷip pēṇa vēṇṭumeṉṉum kali nōkkilli.
tamiḻakattil uḻavut toḻiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇiyāka, toṉṟu toṭṭup payaṉpaṭṭuvarum vilaṅku erutākum. ērt toḻiṟku utavuvataṉāl kāḷi eruteṉap paṭṭatu. ēr-ērtu- erutu. kāṭṭu māṭṭip piṭittup paḻakki vīṭṭu vilaṅkākki ērt toḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
erutiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi nōkkiyē ērt toḻilip pakaṭu eṉṟaṉar.
pakaṭupuṟan tarunar pāram ōmpi  (puṟam 35)
pakaṭu naṭanta kūḻ  (nālaṭi. 4)
uḻavut toḻilum pāṇṭiyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pāṇṭiyañ ceyvāṉ poruḷiṉum  (kalit. 136).
pāṇṭi erutu.
erutukaḷi niṟam paṟṟiyum tiṟam paṟṟiyum pala vakiyāka vakuttu, avaṟṟuḷ nālvakiyic ciṟappāka iṟakka variciyil eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar. atu muḻuppulli, mukkāl mayili, aric civappu, kāl karuppu; eṉap paḻamoḻiyāy vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
paṇṭiyuḻavar ellāp payir paccikaḷiyum paruvamaṟintē viḷittu vantaṉar. (cittirimāta vuḻavu pattari māṟṟut taṅkam. paṭṭan tappiṉāl naṭṭam āṭip paṭṭam tēṭi viti,  eṉpaṉa piṟkālattup paḻamoḻikaḷākum.) mārikkāla vēḷāṇmiyik kālam eṉṟum, vēṉiṟkāla vēḷāṇmi  yik kōṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
iṉṟu naṭipeṟṟuvarum vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷ pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu toṭṭē paṇṭāṭu paḻanaṭiyāy varuvaṉa vākum.
kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal, kal poṟukkutal, eruviṭutal, āḻa vuḻutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (tāḷiyaṭittal, palliyāṭu tal, ūṭṭittal, paṭaliḻuttal). puḻutiyuṇakkal, vitittal, kaḷiyeṭuttal, kāval kāttal, aṟuvaṭi ceytal, kaḷañ cērttal, cāṇiyaṭital (cūṭupōṭutal pōramittal), cāṇi pirittal, kāyap pōṭutal, piṇiyalaṭittal (kaṭā viṭutal, ataritirittal), vikkōl allatu cakki allatu kappi nīkkal, poli tūṟṟal, poliyaḷattal, vitikkeṭuttal, kaḷañciyañ cērttal eṉpaṉa vāṉavārip putuk kolli vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷām, kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal putuk kollikkē nikaḻum.
paḻaṅkolliyāyiṉ, urampōṭutaṟku eruviṭutalōṭu kiṭiyamarttalum kuppiyaṭittalum nikaḻum.
puṉcey vēḷāṇmiyāyiṉ, puḻutiyuṇakkaṟkup piṉṉum kaḷiyeṭuttaṟku muṉṉum, nāṟṟup pāval, pātti piṭittal, nīr pāycci naṭutal oṉṟarivāṭam nīr pāyccal ākiya viṉikaḷ nikaḻum.
naṉcey vēḷāṇmiyāyiṉ, nāṟṟup pāval, nīr pāyccal, toḷi(cēṟu)k kalakkal, kuḻi mitittal, naṭutal eṉṉum viṉikaḷum, avaṟṟiṟkuppiṉ kaḷiyeṭuttal mutaliya vāṉavāri vēḷāṇmi viṉikaḷum, nikaḻum,
akam - akaram = marutanilattār. o.nō: L.agros, field E.acre.

ēr eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmi kavaṉikkattakkatu.

3. kammiyam
karuttal ceytal. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa oru tamiḻ viṉiccol.
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ karuniṟamum pukar (brown) niṟamum poṉṉiṟamum uḷḷavar toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. poṉ ṉiṟattic civappeṉṟum veḷḷiyeṉṟum colvatu vaḻakkam. poṉmi karumi ākiya iruniṟam paṟṟiyē, veṇkaḷamar karuṅkaḷamar, veḷḷāḷar, kārāḷar, veḷ ḷokkalar kārokkalar eṉṉum colliṇikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. kāyp pēṟumāṟu viṉi ceyyiṉ karuppar akaṅki karuppatum civappar akaṅki civappatum iyalpu. itupaṟṟi viṉi ceytalik kuṟikka, karuttal ceytal eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. kikarukkumāṟu ceyvatu karuttal; civakkumāṟu ceyvatu ceytal.
karu-karumam-ceyki. karu-karuvi= ceykikku vēṇṭum tuṇip poruḷ. karu-karaṇam= ceyki, karuvi. aṇam or īṟu.
iṉi, karumic coṟkup perumip poruḷumiruttalāl, karuttal = mikuttal eṉṟumām. putitāyc ceyyap peṟum oru poruḷ ēṟkeṉavēyuḷḷa ataṉ vakiyi mikuttal kāṇka. make eṉṉum āṅkilac colliyum magnus (great) eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirippar āṅkilac collārāyc ciyāḷar. taṉviṉiyum piṟaviṉip poruḷ payappatu collākka marapiṟ kottatē.
o.nō : veḷuttal = veḷḷiyātal (taṉviṉi), veḷḷiyākkutal (piṟaviṉi). vaṇṇāṉ tuṇikaḷi veḷukkiṟāṉ eṉpatil, veḷuttal viṉi piṟaviṉiyā yiruttal kāṇka.
karumam-kammam= ceyki. toḻil, kit toḻil, kol toḻil, kammiyat toḻil.
kammam = kammiyat toḻil.
kammam = kammiyat toḻil.

kammam- kamam.
ivviru vaṭivum mutalil toḻil eṉṟē poruḷpaṭṭaṉa. ulakil mutalil tōṉṟiya toḻil uḻavē. toḻil, ki eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarum, cey, paṇṇi eṉṉum nilap peyarum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.

kamam - kam = 1. toḻil īmum kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum  (tol, eḻut. 328) 2. kammiyat toḻil, (naṉ, 223, virutti.)
kam+āḷaṉ kammāḷaṉ. kammāḷar eṉpār kollaṉ, taccaṉ, kammiyaṉ (ciṟpi), taṭṭāṉ, kaṉṉāṉ eṉṉum aiṅkollar. kammiyaṉik kaltaccaṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
kam-kammiyam = 1. kittoḻil. 2. kammāḷat toḻil.
kammāḷaṉ eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē, kammiyaṉ eṉṉum collum piṟa kollariyum kuṟikkum.
kammiyaṉ = 1. toḻilāḷi. kammiyarum ūrvar kaḷiṟu (cīvaka. 495) 2. kammāḷaṉ (ti.vā.). 3. poṟkollaṉ. ācil kammiyaṉ mācaṟap puṉinta (puṟam. 353)

o.nō:  perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, marumakaṉ - marumāṉ.
karu eṉṉum viṉimutaṉili vaṭamoḻiyil kru eṉat tiriyum. karaṇam eṉpatik kāraṇa eṉa nīṭṭiyum, kru eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu kārya eṉṉum colli ākkiyum, karumam eṉpatik karmaṉ eṉat tirittum, uḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.

āṅkilattilum, taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷ ceyyum taccaṉi joiner eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
taccaṉ eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil takṣa eṉṟum, kirēkkattil tekton eṉṟum tiriyum. uṇmi iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyit tokutta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar takṣa eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu taccaṉ eṉpatu vantatākak kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyiṉ vaṭṭakkaccik kaṭṭil, piṉvarumāṟu yāṉi maruppiṉāl (tantattiṉāl) ciṟanta vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟṟatāka, neṭunalvāṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
nāṟpatāṇṭu akaviyuḷḷatum, muracupōṉṟa periya kālkaḷiyum aḻakiya pukar niṟinta mattakattiyum uṭiyatum, pōrukkuc ciṟantateṉṟu peyar peṟṟatum, pōr ceytu iṟantatumāṉa, yāṉiyiṉ tāṉē vīḻnta kompi irupuṟamum kaṉamum cemmiyum oppac cetukki, kūriya ciṟṟuḷiyālē periya ilit toḻili iṭiyilē amittu; niṟicūli yiṉ pālkaṭṭiya mārpu pōlat tiraṇṭa kuṭattiyum, uḷḷip pūṭu pōṉṟa kaṭiccaliyum, koṇṭa kālkaḷip porutti; mūṭṭuvāy māṭcimippaṭat takaṭukaḷi āṇikaḷāl tittu, aḻakākat toṭutta muttuk kuñcaṅkaḷic cuṟṟivarat toṅka viṭṭu; puliyiṉ variyik koṇṭa polivu peṟṟa kaccālē takaṭumaṟiya naṭuviṭam muḻutum piṉṉi; kuṟṟamaṟṟup pēraḷavu koṇṭu perum peyarpeṟṟa vaṭṭakkaccik kaṭṭil
tacanāṉ keytiya paṇimaruḷ nōṉṟāḷ

takaṭukaṇ putiyak koḷīit tukaḷtīrntu  (neṭunal. 115-128).
nilattiṉ mēlōṭum vaṇṭiyum tērum pōṉṟē nīrmēlōṭum marakkalaṅkaḷum, mutaṉ mutal tamiḻnāṭṭiltāṉ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa. ōṭam ceypavaṉukku ōṭāvi (ōṭāḷvi) eṉṟum, nāvāy pōṉṟa peruṅkalam ceypavarukkuk kalañcey kammiyar (maṇi. 25 - 124); kalampuṇar kammiyar (maṇi. 7:70) eṉṟum, peyar. ivar kappal taccar.
kalattiyuṭiya neytal nila makkaḷ kalavar, kalatti ōṭṭum talivaṉ nīkāṉ (nīkāmaṉ, mīkāmaṉ mīkāṉ (Captin)). kalattil viṉi ceypavar ōcunar (Sailors). kalattiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceypavaṉ kaṭalōṭi.

eṉṟu auviyār kūṟiyatu kāṇka.
koṭittēr vītiyum tēvar kōṭṭamum
eṉṟu maṇimēkali (21:120) kūṟuvatāl, teyva uruvatti vittirukkum perun tērum akkālat tiruntirukkalām.
ikkālattuḷḷa tērkaḷuḷ tiruvārūrt tēr mikap peritu. atuvum ariyē yarikkāl tērēṉṟum, muṉpu erintu pōṉatu mukkāl tēreṉṟum, kūṟuvar. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ataṟkum muṉpē muḻuttēr iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. muḻut tēr ēḻu taṭṭum mukkāltēr aintu taṭṭum arittēr mūṉṟutaṭṭum uḷḷaṉavākac collappaṭum. ivi muṟiyē nūṟaṭiyum, eḻupattintaṭiyum aṟupataṭiyum uyaramuḷḷaṉa eṉpar.
maṉṉārkuṭi matilaḻaku... maṉiyaḻaku, tiruvārūrt tēraḻaku, tiruviṭimarutūrt teruvaḻaku eṉṟoru paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vāṉaḷāvum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirunta paṇṭik kālat til, māperun tērkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruntatil viyappilli.
teyvat tēriṟ pala aṇiyuṟuppukkaḷum tēvar muṉivar paṭimikaḷum yāḷi kutiri yuruvaṅkaḷum ceytu vikkap peṟutalāl, tērttaccar taccuk kaliyil taliciṟantavarāvar.
kaṭṭumaram mutal kappalvari palvakik kalaṅkaḷiyum ceyyum taccar kalampuṇar kammiyar (kappal taccar) eṉappaṭṭār.
kāṭci kālkōḷ nīrppaṭi naṭukal
eṉṉum veṭcittiṇit tuṟikaḷum (tol. puṟat. 5). ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṉimaliyiṟ kalleṭuttuk kaṇṇakikkup paṭimañ camippittatum, kaltaccu viṉikkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
maṇṭapaṅkaḷi yellām kallāṟ kaṭṭuvatē toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkam. nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum viḻāppaṭi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum attakiya. kaṟ kōyil kaṟṟaḷiyeṉappaṭṭatu. taḷikōyil, teyvappaṭimikaḷ (cilikaḷ) aivakik kollarālum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.
4. kottam
kottam eṉpatu kottaṉ vēli allatu kaṭṭaṭat toḻil.
5. kala viṉi
kala viṉi eṉṟatu kuyavaṉ ceyyum kalattoḻil.
