eṉṉum mutupālic ceyyuḷ (puṟam. 256), iruvaruṭalik koḷḷattakka mutumakkaḷ tāḻiyum akkālattiṟ ceyyap paṭṭamiyi uṇarttum.
6. piṟa toḻilkaḷ
pāy necavum muṭivum, tiyal, tōl pataṉiṭal, cerupput tittal, tōluṟi ceytal, peṭṭi cuḷaku (kūṭi muṟam) muṭitalum piṉṉutalum, kaṭṭiṟ piṉṉutal, tuṇiyālum neṭṭiyālum palvēṟu kavarccip poruḷkaḷum viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum aṇikaḷum ceytal, vaṇṇam pūcutal, vaṇṇa ōviyam varital, maṇpāvi ceytal, naṟu maṇap poruḷ kūṭṭutal, cuṇṇa miṭittal, cekkāṭṭutal, āṭiveḷuttal, māli kaṭṭutal, caṅkaṟuttal, pavaḻa maṟuttal, maṇi kōttal, mīṉ piṭittal, muttuk kuḷittal, uppu viḷittal, kaḷḷiṟakkutal, kaḷ camittal, āṭu mēyttal, māṭu mēyttal, tayir kaṭital, vēṭṭiyāṭutal, koṭikkāl vittal. cāṇikkal ceytal, palvakic ciṟṟuṇṭi ceytal, kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ (palvēṟu icik karuvikaḷ) ceytal mutaliyaṉa.



11. vāṇikam
nākarika makkaḷ vāḻkkikku, uṇavu pōṉṟē uṭi, kalam, uṟiyuḷ, ūrti mutaliya poruḷkaḷum, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya celvamum, iṉṟiyamiyātiruppatāl, poruḷāṭcit tuṟiyil, oru nāṭṭiṉ nalvāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāṉatu uḻavut toḻileṉṟum, ataṟkaṭuttavi iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumāṉa viḷiporuṭkaḷāṟ ceyyap paṭum palvēṟu kittoḻilkaḷeṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkaṭutta nilavaḻiyum nīrvaḻiyum naṭattappeṟum vaṇikameṉṟum, paṇṭit tamiḻar naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, mūvēntarum iruvaki vaṇikattiyum ūkkutaṟkuc cālikaḷum tuṟi mukaṅkaḷum amittum, avaṟṟip pātukāttum vantaṉar.
vaṇikam eṉpatu vaṇij eṉṟum, vāṇikam eṉpatu vāṇijya eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
nāṭṭiṟku uḷḷiruntu āṭciyic cevviyāyc ceytaṟku oru marutanilat talinakarum, nāṭṭōrattiṟ kaṭaṟkariyi liruntu nīr vaṇikatti ūkkuvataṟku oru neytal talinakaru māka, iru talinakari mūvēntarum toṉṟutoṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.
vēntaṉ  	maruttalivar  	neytal talinakar
pāṇṭiyaṉ	maturi	koṟki
cōḻaṉ	uṟiyūr	pukār (kāvirippūm
			   paṭṭiṉam)
cēraṉ	karuvūr (karūr)	vañci
mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum muṟṟum aḻintu pōṉamiyāl, pāṇṭiyariṉ iṭikkaḻakakkāla marutanilat talinakarum talikkaḻakakkāla neytal nilat talinakarum, aṟiyap paṭavilli. cērar koṅkunāṭṭi iḻanta piṉṉar, vañcikkē karuvūrp peyari iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar.
kōnakarkaḷilum tuṟinakarkaḷilum iruntu nila vaṇikamum nīr vaṇikamum ceytu vanta nakarattār allatu nakara māntar 
eṉṉum vakuppiṉari, eṇpērāyaṅkaḷuḷ ōrāyamākak koṇṭu, avari maṉṉar piṉṉōr eṉṟu ciṟappittum, avaruḷ talimiyāṉavarkku eṭṭip paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyum, mūvēntarum ūkki vantaṉar.
eṭṭip paṭṭac ciṉṉamāka oru poṟpū aḷikkappeṟum.
eṭṭippūp peṟṟu  (maṇi. 22: 113)
eṭṭip paṭṭam, atip peṟṟa vaṇikaṉiṉ maṉivikkum, allatu makaṭkum matippuravup paṭṭamāka (Title of  Courtesy) vaḻaṅki vantatākap peruṅkati kūṟum.
eṭṭi kāvitip paṭṭan tāṅkiya
mayiliyaṉ mātar   (peruṅ. ilāvā. 3: 144)
eṭṭip paṭṭattārkku, eṭṭippuravu eṉṉum nilamāṉiyamum aḷikkappaṭṭatāka mayilinātar uri kuṟikkum (naṉ. 158)
kōṭiyum tēṭik koṭimaramum naṭṭi
eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkut toṭarmoḻiyāl, kōṭip poṉ tēṭiya celvākkuk koṭiyum oṉṟu koṭukkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
eṭṭutal = uyartal. eṭṭam = uyaram. eṭṭi = uyarntōṉ. eṭṭi-ceṭṭi (mutaṉmeyp pēṟu).
o.nō: ēṇ- cēṇ. ēmam- cēmam. ceṭṭiyiṉ taṉmi ceṭṭu.
cirēṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacol, tiru eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripāṉ crī (srī) eṉpataṉ uccattara (superlative degree) vaṭiviṉiṉṟu tirintatākum.
opputtaram	uṟaḻtaram	uccattaram
crī (srī)	crēya	crēṣṭa
nila vāṇikam
nilavāṇikar vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷik kutirikaḷ mēlum kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷ mēlum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyk kāṭṭu vaḻiyē tamiḻakattiyaṭutta vaṭuka nāṭṭiṟkum neṭuntolivāṉa vaṭa nāṭṭiṟkum, kāvaṟ paṭiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu ērāḷamāyp poruḷīṭṭi vantaṉar. avvaṇikak kūṭṭaṅkaṭkuc cāttu eṉṟu peyar. cāttu kūṭṭam, cārttu - cāttu. cārtal cērtal.
cāttu = 1. kūṭṭam. curivaṉic cāttu niṟimati tavaḻum (kallā. 63, 32) 2. vaṇikak kūṭṭam. cāttoṭu pōntu taṉittuya ruḻantēṉ (cilap. 11, 190)
cāttu eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil sārtta eṉṟu tiriyum.
vaṇikac cāttukkaḷiṉ kāval teyvamākiya aiyaṉārkkuc cāttaṉ eṉṟu peyar. ataṉāl, vaṇikarkkuc cāttaṉ cāttuvaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟ peyarāy vaḻaṅkiṉa. aiyaṉār kōyilil, vaṇikac cāttik kuṟittaṟku maṇkutiri yuruvaṅkaḷ ceytu vittiruttalik kāṇka. cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvap peyar vaṭamoḻiyil cātā eṉat tiriyum.
iṉi, vaṇikac cāttiṉ talivaṉum cāttaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ip peyarum sārtta eṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyil cāttik kuṟikkum coṟkum cāttiṉ talivaṉik kuṟikkum coṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉmiyum, cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvattik kuṟikkum col vēṟupaṭṭiruppatum, kaṇṭu uṇmi teḷika.
kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum

ulakukiḷarn taṉṉa urukeḻu vaṅkam
 pulavuttirip peruṅkaṭal nīriṭip pōḻa
eṉpatāl (akam. 255 : 1-2), kaṭalip piḷantu cellum māperuṅ kappalkaḷ tamiḻakattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭami aṟiyappaṭum.
kappalkaḷ taṅkum tuṟimukattic cērppu, koṇku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku.
kīḻ kaṭaṟkariyil koṟki, toṇṭi, pukār (kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam) eṉṉum tuṟinakarkaḷum, mēl kaṭaṟkariyil vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi eṉṉum tuṟinakarkaḷum iruntaṉa. iṭik kaḻakak kālattil pāṇṭiyar tuṟinakar kumariyāṟṟiṉ kayavāyil (estuary) amintiruntatu. ataṉ tamiḻp peyar (alivāyil?) maṟintu, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa kapāṭa puram eṉṉum vaṭacollē iṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkiliruk kiṉṟatu.
iravil vaḻitappic cellum kalaṅkaṭku vaḻikāṭṭuvataṟku, ovvoru tuṟinakarilum kalaṅkari viḷakkam (Light house) iruntatu.

kaṭaṟkari uḷnāṭṭi nōkka mikat tāḻnta maṭṭattiliruppa tāl, tuṟinakarkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
patital tāḻntiruttal. paḷḷamāṉa nilattip patinta nilam eṉpar. pati + aṉam = pataṉam - pattaṉam - paṭṭaṉam - paṭṭiṉam.
takaram ṭakaramāvatu peruvaḻakku.
o.nō:	kottu maṇveṭṭi- koṭṭu maṇveṭṭi.
		kaḷikkottu- kaḷikkoṭṭu.
		pottu - poṭṭu - poruttu.
		vīrattāṉam (va.) - vīraṭṭāṉam.
		pataṉam - paṭaṉam = nōyāḷiyip pātukāttal.
iṉi, pataṉam eṉpatu, kalaṅkaḷ kāṟṟiṉālum koḷḷik kārarālum cētamiṉṟip pātukāppāyirukkumiṭam eṉṟumām. pataṉam, pātukāppu.
paṭṭiṉam eṉpatip paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. ik kālattiṟ paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ceṉṉiyic ciṟappāyk kuṟittal pōl, akkālattil paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu pukāric ciṟappāyk kuṟittatu. paṭṭiṉappāli, paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷiyār eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.
oru tiṇikkum ciṟappāyuriyataṉṟi nakarap potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkum pati eṉṉum col, makkaḷ pativāy (niliyāy) irukkum iṭattik kuṟikkum. patital niliyāyk kuṭi yiruttal.
kappalkaḷ tuṟimukattiṟku vantu cērumpōtum ati viṭṭup puṟappaṭumpōtum, muracaṅkaḷ muḻakkappaṭṭaṉa.

mēli yāriyak kalap peyarkaḷ
kalaṅkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻakattilēyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, pala kaṭal tuṟic coṟkaḷum kalat tuṟic coṟkaḷum mēli yāriyattilum kīḻi  yāriyattilum tamiḻāyi rukkiṉṟaṉa.
vāri = nīr, periya nīr niliyāṉa kaṭal. L. mare, Skt. vari (vāri)
vāraṇam = kaṭal. L. marinus,  E. marina Skt. varuna (vāruṇa)
vārtal = nīḷtal. vār-vāri, o.nō:  nīḷ- nīr.

12. araciyal
mutaṟ kālattil, muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭam muḻuvatum pāṇṭi nāṭākavum, kumarimuṉi mutal paṉimali variyum kuṭapāti cēra nāṭākavum kuṇapāti cōḻa nāṭākavum, iruntaṉa.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭu, teṉpāli nāṭu mutal kumariyāṟu vari eḻu nūṟu kātam (2001 kal) nīṇṭiruntatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ atiyiḻanta piṉ, piṟkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭākiya nelli maturi mukavi māvaṭṭap pakutiyi, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kippaṟṟi yāṇṭāṉ. itaṉ uṇmiyi,
malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vuvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ
eṉṉum mullikkalit taravālum (4).
aṅṅaṉamākiya nilak kuṟikkuc cōḻa nāṭṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēramāṉāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamumeṉṉu mivaṟṟi, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka vāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār uriyālum (cilap. pak. 303), aṟika.
kumarikkaṇṭam muḻuki, vaṭanāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻ tiraviṭamākat tirintu vaṭanāṭu moḻipeyar tēyamāṉa piṉ tamiḻakam mikac curuṅkiviṭṭatu. mutaṟkālattil pāṇṭi nāṭu aimmaṇṭalaṅ kaḷākavum, cōḻa nāṭu puṉal maṇṭalam (puṉṉāṭu) toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, cēranāṭu mali maṇṭalam koṅku maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ aintuṇiyaracarik koṇṭu āṇṭatāl, pañcavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
paḻiyoṭu paṭarāp pañcava vāḻi (cilap 20:33).  añcavaṉ eṉṟa collē pañcavaṉ eṉṟu tirintiruttal vēṇṭum.
o.nō: appaḷam - appaḷamu - appaṭamu (te);  appaḷa - pappaḷa (ka.)  pappaṭam (ma.) parppaṭṭa (va); appā - E. papa. 
appaḷit turuṭṭuvatu appaḷam. appaḷittal - camaṉākat tēyttal.
pāṇṭiyaṉik kuṟikkum pañcavaṉ eṉṉum peyar orumi yeṉṟum, pāṇṭavarik kuṟikkum pañcavar eṉṉum peyar paṉmi yeṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
toṇṭi maṇṭalam cōḻar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉatu pōṉṟē, koṅku maṇṭalamum cērar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu piṟ kālattil nīṅki viṭṭatu. koṅku maṇṭalam mutaliṟ cēra nāṭṭiṉ pakutiyāyiruntami, kollic cilampaṉ eṉṉum cēraṉ peyarālum, cērar kuṭiyiṉaṉāṉa atikamāṉ takaṭūri āṇṭatiṉālum, cērar koṅkuvi kāvūrnaṉāṭatil  eṉṟu aruṇakirinātar pāṭiyiruppatālum, vañcikkuk karuvūr eṉṉum peyaruṇmiyālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
ovvoru nāṭum pala kōṭṭam allatu vaḷanāṭu eṉṉum perum pirivukaḷākavum, ovvoru perum pirivum pala kūṟṟam allatu nāṭu eṉṉum ciṟu pirivukaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭi runtaṉa.  muttamiḻ nāṭum orē vēntaṉatu āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa piṟkālattil, ovvoru tamiḻnāṭum oru peru maṇṭalamākak karutappaṭṭatu. innilimi muṟkālattil illi. āṭci paṟṟi munnāṭum vēṟupaṭṭavēṉum, moḻi paṟṟit tamiḻakam eṉa oṉṟupaṭṭē yiruntu vantaṉa. ovvoru kūṟṟamum ati yotta nāṭum pala taṉiyūrkaḷākavum pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ cērnta paṟṟukkaḷākavum  pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. taṉiyūr ūrāṭciyum paṟṟu ūrāṭci yoṉṟiyamum ottaṉa.
ovvoru taṉiyūrilum allatu paṟṟilum, ūravi eṉṟa aṭippaṭi yāṭcikkuḻu iruntatu. atu āṇṭutōṟum tiruvuḷac cīṭṭup pōṉṟa kuṭavōliyāl tērnteṭukkap paṭṭatu. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷum poṟuppaṟṟavarkaḷum atil iṭam peṟavilli. ūrāṭciyiṉ ovvoru tuṟiyiyum kavaṉikka, vāriyam eṉṉum uṭkuḻu amikkappaṭṭatu. av vāriyaṅkaḷ ēri vāriyam, kaliṅku vāriyam, kaḻaṉi vāriyam, tōṭṭa vāriyam, kuṭumpu vāriyam, poṉ vāriyam, kaṇakku vāriyam, kōyil vāriyam, taṭivaḻi (peruñcāli) vāriyam, pañcavāra vāriyam, āṭṭi vāriyam mutaliyaṉavākum. ūravi naṭavaṭikki kaḷiyum kaṇakkiyum eḻuti vikka ūrkkaṇakkaṉ eṉṉum aluvalaṉ iruntāṉ.
pañcavāra vāriyam varittaṇṭalik kavaṉippateṉṟum, piṟa vāriyaṅkaḷi mēṟpārppateṉṟum. palavāṟākak kūṟuvar. atu iyaṟkiyākavō ceyaṟkiyākavō uṇṭākum uṇavup pañcattit taṭukka ēṟpaṭṭatākavum irukkalām. vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟi ceytal āṭṭi vāriyattiṉ kaṭamiyākum. urimi. (Civil) vaḻakku, kuṟṟa (Criminal) vaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭiyum kēṭṭu, kolit taṇṭam ceyyavum av vāriyattiṟku atikāramiruntatu. maṉṟupāṭu, taṇṭā, kuṟṟam eṉat taṇṭaṉi mūvakip paṭṭiruntatu.
ami - avi, o.nō: ammi - avvi, kumi - kuvi. amital = neruṅkutal, poruntutal, kūṭutal. ami= kūṭṭam (mutaṉilit toḻilāku peyar). o.nō: imi-kumi. (va.).
ovvoru kūṟṟattiyum kavaṉikka nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉṟa atikāriyum, ovvoru kōṭṭattiyum mēṟpārkka nāṭu kaṇkāṭci eṉṟa atikāriyum iruntaṉar.
ūrami ceyyum vāriyap perumakkaḷōm  (S.I.I.i, 117). 
avi - cavi - capā (va.). o.nō:  vaṭavi - vaṭavā (va.) - paṭapā(va.).
aracaṉ talinakariliruntu āṇṭu vantāṉ. aracaṉukkuk kāval toḻilē ciṟappātalālum, kāvalaṉ puravalaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṇmiyālum, aracu eṉpatu araṇ eṉṉum colliṟku iṉamāy, pātukāppu allatu kāval eṉṉum karutti aṭippaṭiyāyk koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. aracu- aracaṉ.
Gk. archon, L. rex, regis, Skt. rajan.
aracaṉ - aricaṉ- ariyaṉ - rāyaṉ (kocci) - rāyalu (te).
E. roy, king, as in Viceroy. ME. royal (adj.) f. OF. roial. 
aracar kuṟunila maṉṉarum perunila maṉṉarum eṉa iru tiṟattār. cilavūrt talivarāṉa kiḻavarum palavūrt talivarāṉa vēḷirum porunarum kuṟunila maṉṉar. perunāṭṭut taliva rāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar mūvarum perunila maṉṉar. avar muṭi cūṭiyatāl vēntar eṉappaṭṭār. kuṟunila maṉṉar avaruk kaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracar.
vēyntōṉ- vēntaṉ. vēytal- muṭiyaṇital. koṉṟi vēyntōṉāṉa civaṉik koṉṟi vēntaṉ eṉṟu auviyār kūṟutal kāṇka.
mūvēntarum avar kuṭumpattiṉarum potuvākak kōkkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. kuṭavarkō, kōpperuñcōḻaṉ, kōyil, kōpperuntēvi, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
kōvaṉ - kōṉ = iṭiyaṉ, iṭiyaṉ āṭukaḷik kāppatu pōl makkaḷik kākkum aracaṉ.
kōṉ - (Turk. Khan) (kāṉ) eṉpar kālṭuvel. kōṉ - kō.
mūvēntarum, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, uṟutic cuṟṟam eṉa mūvakip paṭṭa patiṉeṇkaṇat tuṇivaruṭaṉ, āṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
amiccar, pōṟṟiyar, paṭittalivar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉpavar aimperuṅ kuḻuvār, amiccar araciyal viṉikaḷi amippavar allatu aracaṉukku neruṅkiyiruppavar. amital- neruṅkutal. pōṟṟiyar tamiḻap pūcāriyar. pirāmaṇar vantapiṉ, pōṟṟiyar iṭattil purōkitar amarttappaṭṭār. tūtaṉ eṉpatu muṉ celpavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉ col. tūtu - tūtaṉ. ivviru collum vaṭamoḻiyil tūta (duta) eṉṟu tiriyum.
kaṇakkar, karumat talivar, poṉ cuṟṟattār allatu pokkaca cāliyar, vāyiṟ kāvalar, nakara māntar, paṭittalivar, kutiri maṟavar, yāṉi maṟavar eṉpavar eṇpērāyattār. itiṟ paṭit talivar eṉpār tērppaṭit talivarāyirukkalām.
āruyir naṇpar, antaṇar, camiyaṟkārar, maruttuvar, kaṇiyar eṉpavar uṟutic cuṟṟattār.
iṉi, aṟivālum akaviyālum mūtta periyōri aracar aṟivuriyāḷarāka amarttik koḷvatumuṇṭu. avar mutukaṇ eṉappaṭuvar.
ovvōr araciyal tuṟiyum tiṇikkaḷam (department)  eṉappaṭṭatu. araciṟit tiṇikkaḷat talivaṉ puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷa nāyakam eṉappaṭṭāṉ. nilavari, toḻilvari, vaṇikap pōkkuvarattu vari, nallā nallerutu mutaliya iyaṅku tiṇivari, tirumaṇa vari (kaliyāṇak kāṇam) kāval vari mutaliya palavaki varikaḷ kuṭikaḷiṭam vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭukūṟu eṉpavaṉ nilat tīrviyit tiṭṭañ ceypavaṉ. variyilār eṉpavar varukiṉṟa varit tokikaḷi vāṅkip pativu ceypavar. varik kūṟiṭuvār eṉpavar pativu ceyta varit toki 
kaḷip paṅkiṭupavar allatu pākupāṭu ceypavar. varip pottakam eṉpavaṉ nilavarik kaṇakkaṉ. puravu vari eṉpavaṉ piṟa varik kaṇakkaṉ. nilavari viḷivil āṟiloru paṅku kūlamākavum, piṟa varikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa paṇat tokiyākavum, vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ūraviyār avaṟṟi vāṅkit talinakari luḷḷa paṇṭārattiṟkum pokkaca cālikkum aṉuppi vantaṉar. nilavariyit tiṭṭañ ceyya nilaṅkaḷ ellām tulliyamāy aḷakkap paṭṭaṉa.
vēntaṉ amitik kālattil ovvoru nāḷum pakalil mutaṟ pattu nāḻiki koṭikkum, iṭippattu nāḻiki vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟi ceyvataṟkum, kaṭip pattu nāḻiki taṉ illaṟa iṉpattiṟkum celaviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṟiyappaṭum. eṭṭar eṉpavar, avaṉukku nāḻiki yaṟivippavar. akavar (cūtar) eṉpavar, niṉṟu koṇṭu avaṉ muṉṉōr perumiyik kūṟi avaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭupavar. ōvar (mākatar) eṉpavar, iruntu koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam pāṭupavar. ēttāḷar piṟa camiyaṅkaḷiṟ pukaḻntu pāṭupavar.
vēntaṉ taṉ kolu maṇṭapattiṟkuc cellumpōtum, aṅku amarntirukkum pōtum, atiṉiṉṟu mīḷumpōtum, mey kāvalar pakkat tuṇiyāvar. piṟa nāṭṭaracarum avar tūtarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum patiṉeṇ kaṇattārum tiṇikkaḷat talivarum nāṭṭup perumakkaḷum kūṭiya kolu maṇṭapattil, aracu kaṭṭil eṉṉum ariyaṇiyil vēntaṉ vīṟṟirukkum kolu ōlakkam eṉappaṭum. kālik kolu nāḷōlakkam eṉṟum, aritāyk kūṭum mālik kolu allōlakkam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. koṭi vēḷi koṭi muracum muṟi vēḷi muṟi muracum aṟintu terivikkappeṟum.
vēntaṉ araciyaṟ paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭirukkum pōtellām, cila atikārikaḷ avaṉuṭaṉiruppar. avar uṭaṉ kūṭṭattār eṉappaṭuvar.
vēntaṉ avvappōtu iṭum kaṭṭaḷikaḷit tiruvāyk kēḷvi eṉpavar kēṭṭu vantu, eḻuttāḷariṭam aṟivippār. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷi eḻutupavar tirumantiravōli eṉpār. avarukkut talivarāyiruppavar tirumantiravōli nāyakam eṉappaṭuvar. nāḷtōṟum naṭappavaṟṟi nikaḻccik kuṟippil eḻutivippavar paṭṭōlip perumāṉ. ūravi kaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟa atikārikaḷiṭattiruntum varum ōlikaḷip paṭittup pārttu, avaṟṟiṟkut takka viṭiyaṉuppuvōr viṭiyilār eṉpōr. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷ pativu ceyyap paṭum pottakam kēḷvi vari eṉappaṭum.
nāṭṭut talinakarilum kōṭṭat talinakarilum, nilap pativu ceyyum āvaṇak kaḷariyum aṟaṅkūṟaviyam eṉṉum vaḻakkut tīrppu maṉṟamum, iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. aracar vaḻakkuk kaḷiyum aṟaṅkūṟaviyattāriṉ tavaṟāṉa tīrppukkaḷiyum, vēntaṉē kavaṉittu vantāṉ.
varip paṇamum putiyalum ciṟṟaracariṭum tiṟiyum tōṟṟup pōṉa pakiyaracar koṭukkum taṇṭamum, vēntaṉ varuvāy kaḷākum.
paṇam, acciṭṭa kācākavum niṟip poṉṉākavum iruvaki yil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. akkam, kācu, kāṇam, poṉ, māṭi mutaliyaṉa kācu vakikaḷ. poṟkācukaḷiyum poṟ kaṭṭikaḷiyum nōṭṭañ ceyyum atikārikaḷ vaṇṇakkar eṉṉappaṭṭār. kācu- E. cash 
kōnakar kāvalum pāṭikāval eṉṉum ūrk kāvalum pōkkuvarattuc cālikkāvalum, iravum pakalum oḻuṅkāy naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
nilaṉakaḻ uḷiyar kalaṉnaciik koṭkum
kaṇmā ṟāṭavar oṭukkam oṟṟi
vayakkaḷiṟu pārkkum vayappuli pōlat
tuñcāk kaṇṇar añcāk koḷkiyar

acivilar eḻuntu nayamvantu vaḻaṅkaliṉ
kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkum kiyaṟu kaṅkulum
accam aṟiyā tēma mākiya
maṟṟi yāmam pakaluṟak kaḻippi
eṉṉum maturik kāñcip pakuti (641-53), maturiyil ūrkāvalar perumaḻi poḻinta naḷḷiravilum ūkkamāyc cuṟṟi vantu ūr kāttamiyit terivikkum.
marutanilat talinakarellām, akaḻiyālum pala vakip poṟi kaḷik koṇṭa kōṭṭi matilālum cūḻap peṟṟiruntaṉa. kirēkkarum urōmarum matil vāyiṟ kāvalarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
aṭalvāḷ yavaṉar  (cilap. 14: 66-7)
karipari tērkāl eṉṉum nālvakip paṭikaḷ vēntaṉuk kiruntaṉa. terinteṭukkappeṟṟa ciṟanta porunarik koṇṭa taṉip paṭippirivu kaḷumiruntaṉa. avi terinta eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyāl vitantu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: uttama cōḻat terinta antaḷakattāḷar  (S.I.I.ii , 97) 
antaḷakam, meymmaṟi (kavacam).
iṉi, vēntaṉ mēl aḷaviṟanta paṟṟuṭiyavarāy, avaṉukkut tuṉpam nērnta vēḷiyil uyirik koṭuttuk kākkavum uṭaṉ māyavum cūḷiṭṭuk koṇṭa pōr maṟavarumiruntaṉar. avar vēḷikkārar eṉappaṭṭār. avar uyir koṭuttal ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭaṉ kaḻittal eṉappaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ, allatu tuṟantapiṉ, allatu kaḻiperu mūp paṭintapiṉ, avaṉ mūttamakaṉ, mūtta makaṉ illāviṭṭāl iḷiya makaṉ, makaṉē illāviṭṭāl makaḷ, makaḷum illā viṭṭāl takutiyuḷḷa neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ, muṭicūṭṭap paṭṭār. taṭātaki yeṉṉum kayaṟkaṇṇiyār pāṇṭi nāṭṭi yāṇṭatiyum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār cēranāṭṭi yāṇṭatiyum kāṇka. muṭicūṭṭu viḻāvilum vēntaṉ piṟanta nāḷākiya veḷḷaṇi viḻāvilum, piṟa ciṟanta nikaḻccikaḷilum, kōpperuntēviyum uṭaṉ kolu viruppāḷ.
kāviti eṉpatu ciṟanta amiccaṉukkum, ēṉāti eṉpatu ciṟanta paṭit talivaṉukkum, vēḷ allatu piḷḷi eṉpatu ciṟanta kuṟunila maṉṉarukkum, māvariyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟanta araciyaṟ karumat talivaṉukkum, eṭṭi eṉpatu ciṟanta vaṇikaṉukkum, ciṟutaṉam, peruntaṉam, talikkōl eṉpaṉa nāṭakak kaṇikiyarkkum, vēntaṉ aḷikkum paṭṭaṅkaḷākum.
ēṉi + ari = ēṉāri (yāṉikaḷi aḻippavaṉ) -  ēṉāti.      ta-ra oṉṟiṟ koṉṟu pōliyāka varum. e-ṭu:  viti- viri, kuralvaḷi- kutavaḷi (kocciyulaka vaḻakku). māvariyaṉ - māvariyam- mārāyam.
mārāyam  peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yāṉum  (tol. puṟat. 8)
araciyal viṉiñar ūtiyattiṟkuk kimmāṟākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu, nellum poṉṉumāyiṉ campaḷam eṉṟum, nilamāṉiyamāyiṉ umpaḷam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
13. kalvi
kalvi oru kulattārkku maṭṭum eṉṟu variyaṟukkappaṭātu, ellāt toḻil vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. iti,
tantimakaṟ kāṟṟum naṉṟi yaviyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷālum (67),
īṉṟupuṟan tarutal eṉṟalik kaṭaṉē
cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē
vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷikkuk kaṭaṉē.  (puṟam. 312)
vēṟṟumi terinta nāṟpā luḷḷum
kīḻppāl oruvaṉ kaṟpiṉ
mēṟpā loruvaṉum avaṉkaṭ paṭumē  (puṟam. 183)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭukkaḷālum, palvēṟu toḻilār kaṭik kaḻakak kālattup pulavarāyiruntamiyālum, aṟiyalām.
kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalic cāttaṉār, poṉ vāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār, aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār, iḷam poṉ vaṇikaṉār, maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār, taṇ kāṟpūṭ kollaṉār, ōlikkaṭik kaṇṇam pukuntārāyat taṉār, vaṭama vaṇṇakkaṉ peruñcāttaṉār mutaliya pulavar palvēṟu toḻilarā yiruntami kāṇka.
kalvi peṇṭirkku vilakkappaṭavilli. okkūr mācāttiyār, auviyār, kākki pāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār, kāvaṟ peṇṭu, kuṟamakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, nakkaṇṇiyār, pāri makaḷir, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, pēymakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, māṟōk kattu nappacaliyār, veṇṇik kuyattiyār, veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār mutaliyōr peyar veḷivanta kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulattiyar. akkālattuk kuṭittaṉap peṇṭir tam kalviyaṟivi yellām illaṟattiṟkē payaṉpaṭuttiyamiyāl, avaruṭ perumpālār peyar veḷivaravilli. kārikkālammiyār ciṟanta pulattiyarā yiruntum, kaṇavarāl vilakkap paṭumvari, avar pāvaṉmi veḷippaṭā tiruntami kāṇka. civappirakāca aṭikaḷ kālamāṉa patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tirukkāṭṭup paḷḷiyil teruvil uppu viṟkum peṇ orutti, aṭikaḷ niṟiyavuḷatō  eṉṟu pāṭi viṉaviya veṇpāviṟku viṭiyāka,
teṉṉōṅku tillic civappirakā capperumāṉ
poṉṉōṅku cēvaṭiyip pōṟṟiṉōm - aṉṉōṉ
tirukkūṭṭam attaṉikkum teṇṭaṉiṭṭōm tīrāk
karukkūṭṭam pōkkiṉōm kāṇ.
eṉṟu kaṭuttup pāṭiṉami kāṇka. kaṭuttal - virital.
pukaḻēntip pulavar kālamāṉa 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, uḻavaṉ, kollaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, taccaṉ, mañcikaṉ (muṭi tiruttāḷaṉ) mutaliya palvakit toḻilāḷarum ciṟanta ceyyuḷ ceyyum āṟṟal peṟṟiruntamiyāl, paṇṭikkālak kalvip parappu aṟiyappaṭum. āṟalikkum kaḷvar kūṭa akkālattiṟ kaṟṟuvalla pāvalarā yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ikkālattiṟpōl kalvit tuṟi eṉṉum araciyal tuṟi akkālattilli. makkaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil, āciriyaṉukkup poruḷ koṭuttum toṇṭu ceytum kaṟṟu vantaṉar.
uṟṟuḻi yutaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum
 piṟṟinili muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟē
eṉṟu āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka. (puṟam. 183).
kaṟpippōr kaṇakkāyaṉ eṉṟum āciriyaṉ eṉṟum kuru allatu kuravaṉ eṉṟum mūvakiyar. kaṇakkāyaṉ eḻuttum ciṟṟilakkiyamum uriccollum (nikaṇṭum) kaṇakkum kaṟpip pōṉ; āciriyaṉ, piṟkālattil aintāka virikkappaṭṭa mūvilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṟ keṭuttuk kāṭṭāṉa pērilakkiyamum kaṟpippōṉ; kuravaṉ camaya nūlum paṭṭāṅku (tattuva) nūlum kaṟpippōṉ.
kaṟṟōrkkuc ceṉṟaviṭamellām ciṟappiruntatu. vēntarip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pulavarkkuc ciṟanta paricum muṟṟūṭṭum (carva māṉiyamum) aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pāṇṭiyar taliciṟanta pulavari yellāṅ kūṭṭi, tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuvip pōṟṟiṉar.
pulavariṉ pāṭalkaḷum nūlkaḷum, pāṇṭiyar tamiḻk kaḻakattil kuṟṟamaṟṟaviyeṉa oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭāloḻiya, ulakil vaḻaṅkā. aṅṅaṉam oppam peṟutal araṅkēṟṟam eṉap paṭṭatu.
nāṭakak kaṇikiyariṉ āṭal pāṭalkaḷum vēntar muṉṉili yilēyē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. araṅkēṟiya kaṇikiyar talik kōṟ paṭṭamum āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu paricamum peṟṟaṉar.
ittakiya araṅkēṟṟa muṟiyāl, akkālattuk kalvi tali ciṟantiruntatu. arip paṭippiṉarum tiripuṇarcciyarum tali yeṭukka iṭamilli, pala tuṟiyilum pulami peṟṟa pēraṟiñar, tavaṟākak kaṟpittu makkaḷik keṭuppavarik koṭikaṭṭi aṟikūvi varavaḻittu, coṟpōr purintu tōṟkaṭittut taṇṭittu aṟivu pukaṭṭuvatum akkāla vaḻakkam.
palkēḷvit tuṟipōkiya
tollāṇi nallāciriyar
uṟaḻkuṟit teṭutta uyarkeḻu koṭi  (paṭṭiṉap. 167-171)
kalvi eṉṉum col, kal eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum. kallutal, tōṇṭutal. māntaṉ mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavut toḻilātalāl, kalvi eṉṉum col mutalil uḻavut toḻiliyē kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. āṅkilattil nilap paṇpāṭṭiyum uḷappaṇpāṭṭiyum oruṅkē uṇarttum culture, cultivation eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum, col eṉṉum orē ilattīṉ vērc colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.

14. kalikaḷ
payiṟciyāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa kalikaḷ.
(1) ici
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar nuṇmāṇ nuḻi pulattarāyum tali ciṟanta nākarikarāyum eḵkuc ceviyarāyu miruntatiṉāl, ēḻu pēriciyum aintu ciṟṟiciyumākiya paṉṉīriciyi (curatti) yum kaṇṭu āyappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil eḻupālip paṇkaḷit tirittatumaṉṟi, ap paṉṉīriciyiyum vaṭṭap pāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 24 ākavum, tirikōṇappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 48 ākavum caturappāli eṉṉum muṟiyil 96 ākavum, nuṭpamāyp pakuttu elliyaṟṟa icip pēriṉ patti nukarntirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṉṉīricikku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇṇi ciyiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟum nūlē ici nuṇukkam pōlum!  ēḻiciyum kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟṟici ākaṇam eṉṟum, kuralum iḷiyumallāta pērici antaram eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
tiri, caturam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkappeṟum. catur eṉpatu, nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup poruḷilaṉṟi, nāṟ kōṇam eṉṉum vaṭivup poruḷil vaṭamoḻiyililli.
akanili marutam, puṟanili marutam, arukiyal marutam, perukiyal marutam eṉṉum paṇ vakuppum, kiḻami niṟikuṟi eṉpaṉavum, tamiḻariṉ ici nuṇukkattik kāṭṭum.
ikkālattu āriya iciyaṟiñar, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ aṟivu nuṇukkatti ōrātu, tampōl avariyuṅ karuti 96 icikaḷ pāṭaṟkiyalātavi eṉpar.
icik karuvikaḷ tōl,tuḷi, narampu, veṇkalam (kañcam) eṉa nālvakip paṭṭiruntaṉa.
tōṟ karuvikaḷ āṭal muḻā, pāṭal muḻā, potu muḻā eṉṟum;  akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇami muḻavu, nāḷ muḻavu, kāli muḻavu, eṉṟum; maṇappaṟi, piṇappaṟi eṉṟum;  vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. paṟi eṉpatu tōṟkaruvip potup peyar. teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi (tol. 964).
iṉṉicit tuḷik karuvikaḷ pullāṅ kuḻal, icikkuḻal (nātacuram), mukavīṇi eṉṉum ēḻicik kuḻalkaḷākavum; ottu (ūmik kuḻal), eṉṉum ōricik kuḻalākavum;  iruvakip paṭṭiruntaṉa. tāri, ciṉṉam, vāṅkā, kompu, caṅku mutaliya ōricit tuḷik karuvikaḷ, ūrvalam pōr mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷil āravāra icik karuvikaḷāyp payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. makuṭi eṉpatu nalla pāmpi mayakkutaṟkeṉṟē amikkappaṭṭa ēḻicic ciṟappiṉṉicik kuḻalākum.
narappuk karuvikaḷ oru narampuḷḷa curiyāḻiliruntu āyiram narampukaḷ peruṅkalam (ātiyāḻ) vari, palvēṟu vakip paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum nāṉkum ciṟantavi. avaṟṟuḷḷum taliciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ. atuvē piṉṉar vīṇiyākat tirintatu. āyiram narampeṉpatu, āyiraṅkāṟ pūcci eṉpatiṟ pōl, narampiṉ peruntokiyiyē kuṟikkum.
iṉṉici vīṇiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl eṉṉum tiruvācakak kūṟṟu (369) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇiyeṉṟu ciṟappup peyar peṟṟamiyiyē kāṭṭum. ellā narappukkaruvikaḷum yāḻē. vīṇi eṉṉum peyarum viṇ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉcollē. atu vaṭamoḻiyil vīṇā eṉṟu tiriyum. viṇṇeṉal, narampu teṟittolittal. viṇ-vīṇi mutukuṉṟam (paḻamali) viruttācalameṉṟu peyar māṟiyatāl vēṟu nakaramākiviṭātu. aṅṅaṉamē vīṇiyeṉappaṭum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum eṉka.
iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cilappatikārattil nārataṉ vīṇi nayanteri pāṭalum eṉṟum, 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa appar tēvārattil mācil vīṇiyum eṉṟum, varutalāl, vīṇi 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpatu poruntātu. vēta kāla nāratar tamiḻnāṭu vantē ici kaṟṟup pañcapāratīyam eṉṉum icit tamiḻ nūli iyaṟṟiṉār. pattām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉatāṉa cīvaka cintāmaṇik kāntaruvatatti yilampakattil, yāḻeṉṟum vīṇiyeṉṟum orē karuvi kuṟikkap paṭuvatāl, yāḻ vēṟu vīṇi vēṟu alla. ikkālattil curiyāḻum vīṇiyeṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tiruñāṉa campantar pāṭiya yāḻmurip paṇṇiṉ iyalpu iṉṟu evarukkum teriyāmiyāl, yāḻmuri vīṇikkamiyum eṉpatum poruntātu.
tāḷak karuvi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatiṉāl, atu veṇkalak karuviyeṉappaṭṭatu. cal allatu callari eṉṉum ōciyuṭimiyāl, tāḷak karuviyiṟ periyatu callariyeṉṟum ciṟiyatu cālar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kural eṉpatu ma eṉṉum 5ām iciyeṉṟum, oru narampil orēyici yeḻuum narappuk karuviyē tamiḻaratu eṉṟum, kūṟuvār tamiḻarāyiṉum icit tamiḻ aṭippaṭiyē aṟiyātār āvar. `kural mutalēḻum eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷum (cilap. 5 : 35), mutaṟṟāṉamākiya kuralilē eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟu uriyāciriyarum (puṟam. 11), mūvēḻtuṟiyum muṟiyuḷik kaḻippi eṉṟu vaṉparaṇarum (puṟam. 152), kūṟiyiruttaliyum, avar kavaṉittilar.
kural eṉṉum collē, iyalpāṉa kuralākiya mutaliciyiyē kuṟikkum. curiyāḻum peruṅkalamumē vilyāḻ (Harp) pōl meṭṭiṉṟi ōricikkoru narampu koṇṭavi. pēriyāḻ mutaliya narampu koṇṭavi. pēriyāḻ mutaliya piṟa yāḻellām meṭṭukkaḷōṭu kūṭi, orē narampil pala vici yicikkakkūṭiya paṇmoḻi narampukaḷ koṇṭaṉa vākavē terikiṉṟaṉa.
paṇkaḷi, ēḻiciyumuḷḷavi paṇ eṉṟum, āṟiciyuḷḷa paṇṇiyal eṉṟum, aiyiciyuḷḷavi tiṟam eṉṟum, nāliciyuḷḷavi tiṟattiṟam eṉṟum, nālvakiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar. in nālvakip paṇkaḷum mottam 11,991 eṉak kaṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
iciyāvatu, narappaṭivāl urikkappaṭṭa patiṉōrā yittut toḷḷā yirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya ātiyici kaḷum; aviyāvaṉa: uyiruyir... kaṭaṉē eṉṉuñ cūttirat tāṉ uṟaḻntu kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu cilappatikāra aruñ collurikārar (pak. 64) kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa nāṟperum paṇkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala tiṟaṅkaḷākavum ovvoru tiṟamum palavakikaḷākavum pakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
ēḻiciyum cērnta oru varici allatu kōvi niliyeṉap paṭum. ciṟanta pāṭakariṉ toṇṭiyil kīḻum iṭiyum mēlumāka munniliyiruppatāl, avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa nālvakic ciṟappiyāḻum narampum meṭṭum amikkappeṟṟaṉa. mun niliyum, muṟiyē melivu, camaṉ, valivu eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. nālvaki yāḻilum meṭṭut toki vēṟu paṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
paṇmoḻi narampu ēḻum oṟṟu narampu mūṉṟum ārppu narampu patiṉoṉṟumāka, 21 narampukaḷ pēriyāḻilum; paṇmoḻi narampu iraṇṭu kuṟintu 19 narampukaḷ makarayāḻilum; ārppu narampu aintu kuṟintu 14 narampukaḷ cakōṭayāḻilum; ārppu narampu aṭiyōṭu illātum paṇmoḻiyiraṇṭu kuṟintum iṟṟi vīṇiyiṟpōl 7 narampukaḷ ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻilum; iruntatākak karutalām. oṉpatu narampuḷḷa muṇṭakayāḻ 4 paṇmoḻi narampum 3 oṟṟu narampum 2 ārppu narampum koṇṭatu pōlum!
narappuc cittār eṉṉum mukamatiyar narappicik karuviyil, iṉṟum 11 ārppu narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟutal kāṇka. avaṟṟi ṉāl uṇṭākum atirvu iciyiṉimiyik keṭuttatiṉāl, avaṟṟi varavarak kuṟittu vantu iṟutiyil aṭiyōṭu nīkki viṭṭaṉar. iciyiṉpa vuṇarcci perukap peruka, yāḻkaḷ tirunti vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. varavarat tiruntutalē yaṉṟit tirun tāmi iyaṟkiyaṉṟu. anākarika māntar icik karuvikaḷiṉ ōci mikutiyiyum, nākarika makkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ iṉimi mikutiyi yum,  iṉṟum virumputal iyalpāyiruttal kāṇka.
yāḻppattariṉ mūṭiyākiya pōrvi, muṉpu tōlāyiruntu piṉpu pātukāppiṟkāka maramāka māṟṟap peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉ amippi aṟiya virumpuvār, tañci āpirakām paṇṭitar makaṉār varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉār ārāynteḻutiya pāṇar kivaḻi eṉṉum nūlip pārkka. aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ yāḻnūl aṭippaṭiyil tavaṟiyatāl mutaṟ kōṇal muṟṟuṅkōṇalāyp pōyiṟṟu.
ulakiṟ ciṟanta ici intiya iciyē. atiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻici. atiyē iṉṟu uḻiyiciyaṭippaṭiyil tāyp paṇkaḷi yum kiḷip paṇkaḷiyum vakuttum, paḻantamiḻk kuṟiyīṭu kaḷiyum paṇṇuppeyarkaḷiyum vaṭacollāka māṟṟiyum, karunāṭaka caṅkītam eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. kēḷviyic curuti yeṉṟum, niliyi tāy eṉṟum, moḻipeyarttiruttal kāṇka. karunāṭaka caṅkītattiṟku ilakkaṇam vakutta vēṅkaṭamakiyum (17ām nūṟ). ataṟku ilakkiyamākat teluṅku kīrttaṉikaḷ pāṭiya tiyākarācarum (18ām nūṟ.) tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻntavarē.
koṭṭu, aci, tūkku, aḷavu eṉṉum nāluṟuppukkaḷi yuṭiya tāḷatti, akak kūttiṟkuriya patiṉoru pāṇikaḷum puṟakkūttiṟkuriya nāṟpattoru tāḷamumāka, aimpattiruvakip paṭuttiyiruntaṉar.
ēḻicikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ mutaṟkālattil ēḻ uyir neṭil kaḷāyiruntaṉa. avi niṟavāḷatti eṉṉum ciṭṭiyicikku ēṟkāmiyāl ca ri ka ma pa ta ni eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ nāḷaṭiviṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivi ṣaṭjam, riṣapam, kāntāram, mattiyamam, pañcamam, tivatam, niṣātam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷākac collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik koḷki mattiyamam pañcamam eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkē ēṟkum. camaṉ paṭṭaṭi eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷiṉ muta leḻuttukkaḷē ca pa eṉpatu, cilar karuttu. iviyiraṇṭumaṉṟi, iciyiṉpattiṟkēṟṟa ēḻeḻuttukkaḷ nāḷaṭiviṟ paṭṭaṟivi ṉiṉṟu terintukoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatē, perumpāl tamiḻicivāṇar karuttām.
paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari, icippāṇarum yāḻp pāṇarum kuḻaṟpāṇarum maṇṭip pāṇarumāṉa nālvakip pāṇarē perumpālum tamiḻ nāṭṭil icivāṇarāyiruntu tamiḻiciyip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. pāṇ cēriyil pāṭṭup pāṭukiṟatā? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, tirumuṟikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapil vanta oru  peṇṇik koṇṭu tēvārattiṟku ici vakuppittamiyum, iti valiyuṟuttum. āriyak kulappiriviṉi ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, pāṇar tīṇṭātavarākit tam tolvaravup pāṇtoḻili iḻantaṉar. eḻūum icit tokiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum,
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷpaṟi
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
avvaki piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum (akat. 18), tamiḻiciyiṉ toṉmiyi uṇarttum. iṉi icit tamiḻ nūlākiya perunāri peruṅkurukum piṟavum tēva viruṭi nārataṉ ceyta pañca pāratīya mutalāvuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷu miṟantaṉa eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟṟāl, vaṭamoḻiyici nūlkaṭ kellām tamiḻnūlkaḷē mutaṉūleṉa aṟika.
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (33), tamiḻiciyiṉ yāḻc ciṟappu uṇarappaṭum.
(2) nāṭakam
nāṭakam eṉpatu teṉcollē. naḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal. naḷ - naḷi. naḷiya eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu. naḷital ottal. naḷi - naṭi. o.nō : kaḷiṟu - kaṭiṟu. naṭittal eṉpatu, iṉṉoru vaṉip pōl allatu illātati uḷḷatu pōlac ceytu kāṭṭutal.
muttamiḻ toṉṟutoṭṭu iyalici nāṭakam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkum.
nāṭakak kali kūttu, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kutittāṭuvatu kūttu. atu vēttiyal, potuviyal; ulakiyal, tēviyal; vacik kūttu, pukaḻkkūttu; varikkūttu variyamitik kūttu (variccāntik kūttu; amitik kūttu). (cāntik kūttu), vēṭikkik kūttu (vinōtak kūttu); akak kūttu, puṟak kūttu; viḷiyāṭṭuk kūttu, viṉik kūttu; veṟṟik kūttu, tōlvik kūttu; eṉap palvēṟu vakiyil ivviruvakippaṭum.
naṭaṉam allatu naṭam eṉpatu, aḻakuṟa āṭuvatu. atu nūṟṟeṭṭu uṭaṟkaraṇaṅ kaḷōṭum kikāl kaṇvāy mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷōṭum kūṭiyatu. kiviṉikaḷ eḻiṟki toḻiṟki, poruṭki eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭu, piṇṭi allatu iṇiyā viṉikki yeṉappaṭum oṟṟikki vaṇṇam muppattu mūṉṟum, piṇiyal allatu iṇikki yeṉappaṭum iraṭṭikki vaṇṇam patiṉintum, koṇṭaṉavākum.
tamiḻ naṭaṉam iṉṟu parata nāṭṭiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. parata cāttiram vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ataṟkum muntiyatu tamiḻp paratamē yeṉpati, nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiya mutalā vuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷumiṟantaṉa, eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāravurip pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟintu koḷka.
nāṭakam eṉpatu kati taḻuvivarum kūttu. atu poruḷ, kati (yōṉi), talimi (virutti), nili (canti), cuvi, vakuppu (cāti), kuṟippu, viṟal (cattuvam), naḷinayam (apinayam), col, col vaki, vaṇṇam, vari, cētam eṉṉum patiṉāṉ kuṟuppuk kaḷiyuṭiyatu.
nāṭaka araṅku nalla keṭṭi nilattil, īraṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kōlāl, eṇkōl nīḷamum eḻukōl akalamum orukōl uyaramum uḷḷatāka amikkappaṭṭu, mēlē mukaṭum, oru mukaveḻiṉi porumuka veḻiṉi karantuvara leḻiṉi eṉṉum mūvakit tiri kaḷum, pukuvāyil puṟappaṭuvāyil (Exit) eṉṉum iruvāyil kaḷum, uṭiyatāyiruntatu.
tamiḻ  nāṭakamellām icippāṭṭuḷḷaviyē (Operas).
nāṭakam eṉpatu nāṭṭakka eṉṟum, araṅku araṅkam eṉpaṉa raṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
ar - araṅku = aṟi, araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭaka mēṭi, nāṭakacāli, viḷiyāṭiṭam, paṭikkalam payilumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, āṟṟiṭik kuṟi, tiruvaraṅkam.
(3) maṭinūl 
maṭi = cōṟu, uṇavu. maṭinūl camiyal nūl.
kantukak karuttum maṭinūṟ ceytiyum  (maṇi. 2 : 22).
eṉpatu tamiḻil maṭi nūliruntamiyit terivikkum.
kāveri yūṭṭiya kavarkaṇit tūṇip
pūviri kaccip pukaḻōṉ taṉmuṉ
paṉivari mārpaṉ payanta nuṇporuṭ
paṉuvaliṉ vaḻāap palvē ṟaṭicil
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi (238 - 41) kūṟum vīmaṉ maṭinūl, tamiḻ maṭi nūlit taḻuviyatēyeṉpatu ataṉ piṉmi yālum, avaṉ pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyāṉa tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉāyirun tamiyālum, aṟiyappaṭum. naḷaṉum tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉē.
(4) maruttuvam
tamiḻ maruttuvak kali cittarāl vaḷarkkap peṟṟatu. ataṉāl atu citta maruttuvam eṉappeṟum. kaṭṭikaḷiyum piḷavi kaḷiyum karippatum, oṭinta elumpi oṭṭavip patum, mutiyavari iḷiñarākkuvatum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻvippatum, citta maruttuvam.
mikiṉum kuṟiyiṉum nōyceyyum nūlōr
vaḷimutalā eṇṇiya mūṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 941).
ātalāl, ūti (vātam) pittam kōḻi eṉṉum mun nāṭiyiyum nāṭi ataṉāl, iṟṟik karuvikaḷik koṇṭu taliciṟanta tērcci peṟṟa mēli maruttuvarum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāta.
nōynāṭi nōymutal nāṭi yatutaṇikkum
vāynāṭi vāyppac ceyal  (kuṟaḷ. 648).
citta maruttuvaṉiṉ teyvattiṟamām.
nuḷ - naḷ - nāḷam - nāḷi - nāḻi - nāṭi.
nāṭi pārkkum tiṟamillātāṉ maruntaṉēyaṉṟi maruttuvaṉā kāṉ. intiya maruttuvam citta maruttuvamē. āyurvētam eṉṉum āriya maruttuvam, citta maruttuvattiṉ vaṭanāṭṭu vakiyē. cēravēntar iruntavari citta maruttuva māyirunta maliyāḷa maruttuvam, iṉṟu āriya maruttuvamāy māṟiyirut tal kāṇka. orē nōykkup pala maruntukaḷ uḷa. maruntukaḷ iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭum. āyiṉ, maruttuvamuṟi oṉṟē.
āriya maruttuvam vērkaḷi mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, citta maruttuvam centūratti mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, cilar kūṟuvar. avar aṟiyār. vērpār, taḻipār, mella mellac centūrac cuṇṇam pār. eṉpatu citta maruttuvap paḻamoḻiyākum. āyiram pērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ. eṉpatu āyiram vērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ciṟupañcamūlam, perumpañca mūlam eṉṉum īrintu vērkaḷum, tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē viḷiyum tamiḻ maruntuc carakkām. maruntu eṉṉum peyarē maṇamuḷḷa vēriyum taḻiyiyumtāṉ kuṟikkum. maru = maṇam. maru - maruntu.
citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟanta maruntu, mūvaki uppuc cērnta muppu eṉpatākum. atu irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟip poṉṉākkavum ōrilakkam āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻac ceyyavum vallateṉa, ataṉ āṟṟali uyarvunaviṟciyākak kūṟuvar.
neṭu nāḷirunta pērum, niliyākavēyiṉum kāyakaṟpan tēṭi neñcu puṇṇāvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar kūṟiyatu, muppi nōkkittāṉ pōlum!
aṟuvi (Surgery) muṟiyum cittamaruttuvattiliruntami
mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ
ampucēr uṭampiṉar  (patiṟ. 42 : 2-5).
(kayam mūḻkic ciral peyarntaṉṉa neṭuveḷḷūci = kuḷattilē muḻukic ciccilik kuruvi eḻukiṉṟa kālattu ataṉ vāyalakip pōla, puṇkaḷi nūlāltikkum pōtu appuṇṇiṉ arattattil maṟinteḻukiṉṟa nīṇṭa veḷḷiyāṉa ūci. neṭuvaci = nīṇṭa ūcit taḻumpu. vaṭu = kāyppu).
eṉṉum patiṟṟup pattaṭikaḷālum,
uṭaliṭit tōṉṟiṟ ṟoṉṟi yaṟuttataṉ utira muṟṟic
cuṭaluṟac cuṭṭu vēṟōr maruntiṉāl tuyaram tīrvar  (kamparā. 	
kumpa. 146)
ārār talivaṇaṅkār ārārtām kiyeṭār
ārārtām cattirattil āṟātār - cīrārum
teṉpuliyūr mēvum civaṉaruḷcēr ampaṭṭat
tampipukāṉ vācalilē tāṉ.
eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭukkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
maruttuvattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi yaṟintē, paṇṭikkālattil ūrmaruntu vaṉukku iṟiyili nilam māṉiyamāka viṭappaṭṭatu. atu maruttuvappēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. (S.I.1,ii. 43).
ulakiṟ ciṟanta citta maruttuvam, ūkkuvāriṉṟi nāḷukku nāḷ maṟintum kuṟintum varukiṉṟatu.
kuḻanti maruttuvam, pēṟukāla maruttuvam, aracamaruttuvam (vilakkamaṟṟatu), nañcu maruttuvam, māṭṭu maruttuvam eṉpaṉa, citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷākum. vilakkam pattiyam. (paṟṟiyam)
nīr, karukku (kaṣāyam) kuḻampu, ney allatu eṇṇey (kirutam), kaḷimpu, meḻuku (lēkiyam), kuḷiki, nīṟu (papam) mutaliya pala vaṭivilum citta maruntukaḷ uṇṭu. ovvoru nōykkum ovvoru puṟatti uracum māttirik kaṭṭikaḷum uḷa.
kuḷiki - kuḷikā (va.) centūḷ - centūḷam - centūram - cintūram - sintūra (va).
maruttuvaṉukkup paṇṭitaṉ eṉṟum, maruttuvattiṟkup paṇṭitam eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. maruntu koṭuppavaṉ maruttuvaṉ; pala poruḷkaḷi aṟintavaṉ paṇṭitaṉ. paṇṭitaṉ eṉpatu paṇṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, paṇṭitam eṉpatu paṇṭuvam eṉṟum, maruvum.
paṇṭuvam (medical treatment) maruttuvap paṇṭuvam eṉṟum nampikkip paṇṭuvam (faith cure) eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭum. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉukkuk kaṭivāyil pāmpuṇik karuṅkal vippatum, erukkam pūvit tiṉṉak koṭuppatum, mūkkiṟ pacciliccāṟu piḻivatum, maruttuvap paṇṭuvam; mantirattiṉāl nañci yiṟakkuvatu nampikkip paṇṭuvam.
nampikkip paṇṭuvam, iṟumpūtu (miracle), nērttikkaṭaṉ; kuḷicam (amulet), maṇi, pārvi, mantiram, ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), pāṇippu (pāvakam), muṭṭi, aracak kāṭci mutaliyaṉavākap palavakippaṭum.
iyēcu perumāṉ toḻu (kuṭṭa) nōyāḷiyit toṭṭu nalappaṭuttiyatum, tiruñāṉa campantar kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ curanōyit tīrttatum, iṟumpūtu. vaḻipaṭu teyvattinōkki, oṉṟu paṭippatāka allatu ceyvatāka nērntukoḷvatu nērttik kaṭaṉ. mantira eḻuttuḷḷa takaṭṭik kiyiṟ kaṭṭik koḷvatu kuḷicam. uruttirākkam, tuḻāy (tuḷaci) maṇi, muttumāli mutaliya aṇikaḷ maṇiyākum. mantirikaṉ tēṭkoṭṭup paṭṭavaṉi allatu pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭa vaṉip pārttuk kuḻiyaṭittu mantirippatu pārvi. nañcēṟi yavaṉē mantiratti ōtuvatu mantiram. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉ kaluḻaṉi (karuṭaṉi) uḷḷuvatu ūḻkam. avaṉ taṉṉik kaluḻaṉākavē karutuvatu pāṇippu.
tiṭaṅkoḷ mantiram tiyāṉapā vakanili muṭṭi  (periyapu. 34 : 1060).
maṇimantiramātiyāl vēṇṭu cittikaḷ  (tāyumā. paripū.9).
cēramāṉ karuvūrēṟiya oḷvāṭ kōpperuñcēralirum poṟiyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, nariverūuttaliyār nalluṭampu peṟṟatāka, 5ām puṟappāṭṭiṉ koḷuk kūṟum. itu, kaṇṭa māliyi (scroula) aracaṉ toṭiṉ kuṇamākumeṉṟu karuti ataṟku aracaṉ tīṅku (king’s evil) eṉṟu āṅkilēyar peya riṭṭataṉoṭu oppunōkkat takkatu.
(5) maṇinōṭṭam
kāka pātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum
ēkiyum nīṅki iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
nūlavar noṭitta nuḻinuṇ kōṭi
nālvaki varuṇattu nalaṅkēḻ oḷiyavum
ēkiyum māliyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyavum
patumamum nīlamum vintamum paṭitamum
vitimuṟi piḻiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum
pūca uruviṉ polanteḷit taṉiyavum
tītaṟu katiroḷit teṇmaṭ ṭuruvavum
iruḷteḷit taṉiyavum iruvē ṟuruvavum
orumit tōṟṟat tivēṟu vaṉappiṉ
ilaṅkunīr viṭūum nalaṅkeḻu maṇikaḷum
kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum
tōṟṟiya kuṟṟam tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum
cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa
vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum
karuppat tuḷiyavum kalliṭi muṭaṅkalum
tirukku nīṅkiya ceṅkoṭi valliyum
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakuti (14:180-198), maṇikaḷiṉ kuṇaṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkūṟutal kāṇka.
6. ōviyam
ōviyac cennūl urinūṟ kiṭakkiyum
eṉpatu (maṇi 2 : 32), ōviyanūlit terivikkum.
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyirat toṭarum, cuvari vittukkoṇṭaṉṟō cittirameḻuta vēṇṭum? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, māṭaccuvarkaḷilellām akkālattil ōviyam variyap peṟṟiruntamiyi aṟivikkum.
vampa mākkaḷ kampali mūtūrc
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunili maṉitoṟum
miyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
evvaki uyirkaḷum uvamam kāṭṭi
veṇcuti viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
eṉṟu maṇimēkali (3 : 126 - 131) kūṟutal kāṇka.
oruvaṉ ōr aracaṉatu avikkaḷam ceṉṟu aṅku aracaṉ taṉ amiccaruṭaṉ amarntiruntatik kaṇṭu, tāṉ koṇṭuvanta kāṇikkiyi nīṭṭa aracaṉ ati vāṅkāmiyāl neruṅkic ceṉṟu atu ōr ōviyamāyirukkak kaṇṭāṉeṉṟu, oru kati vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. attakiya ōviyat tiṟavōr akkālat tiruntaṉar.
ōviyakkārarik kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟum (5 : 30). āṭi aṇikalam kaṭṭiṭam mutaliya ellāp poruḷum ōviya vēlippāṭuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntaṉa.
(7) uruvam (Sculpture)
maṇṇāl uruvañ ceypavar maṇṇīṭṭāḷar  (maṇi. 28 : 37)
marattālum kallālum poṉvakiyālum uruvañceypavar
kammiyar; cāntiṉāṟ ceypavar kottar.
kammiyanūl tolvaram pelli kaṇṭu  (tiruviḷi, tirunakaraṅ. 38).
kōyil tērum kōpuramum uruvaṅkaḷ niṟintavi. pāvi yuruvamum pūtap paṭimiyum pukārilum piṟa nakarkaḷilum iruntaṉa.
(8) kaṭṭiṭam
māṭamāḷikikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum maṇi maṇṭapaṅ kaḷum, mūvēntar talinakarkaḷilum kōnakarkaḷilum mikun tiruntaṉa.
māḷiki, kōpuram, maṇi, maṇṭapam eṉṉum nāṟcollum teṉcollē.
pakivar varavu kāṇṭaṟkuk kōpuram ciṟanta amippeṉṟu kaṇṭapiṉ, nakaric cūḻnta kōṭṭi matililum, vāyiliṟ peritākavum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟiyaṉavākavum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟiyaṉa kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
maturi nakaric cūḻnta matiliṉ nāṟpuṟattilum, vāyilum māṭamum vāṉaḷāviya kōpuramum iruntaṉa. nāṉku vāyil māṭaṅkaḷ iruntatiṉāl, maturi nāṉmāṭakkūṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. kūṭal eṉpatu tamiḻk kaḻakam. atu piṉpu iṭavaṉāku peyarāy maturiyik kuṟittatu. itiyaṟiyātu, tolkatiñar (purāṇikar) oru katiyik kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal taṇkōl vēntē  (puṟam. 58 : 13)
tamiḻnili peṟṟa tāṅkaru marapiṉ
 makiḻnaṉi maṟukiṉ maturi  (ciṟupāṇ. 66-7).
iṉi, kūṭalnakar eṉpatu nāḷaṭivil kūṭal eṉak kuṟukiṟṟu eṉiṉumām.
maturi nakarvāyil, iṭiviṭātu oḻukiya vikiyāṟu pōl akaṉṟum, iṭiyaṟāta makkaḷ pōkkuvarattu mikuntum, iruntatu.
maḻiyāṭu maliyiṉ nivanta māṭamoṭu
viyi yaṉṉa vaḻakkuṭi vāyil
eṉṟu maturikkāñci (355-6) kūṟutal kāṇka.
kōpura maṉṟi vācali māṭamākavuñ camittaliṉ, māṭameṉṟār. eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappuri iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
aracaṉukkuriya ciṟappukkaḷellām iṟivaṉukkum ceyyap peṟṟatiṉāl, kōyil tēr mikap peritāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu pōl, kōyil matiṟ kōpuramum mikapperiya eḻunili vāṉaḷāviyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟatu.  ataṉ amippum tēri ottatākum. ataṉ eḻunilikaḷum tēriṉ eḻutaṭṭukkaḷip pōṉṟavi. eḻunili allatu eḻutaṭṭuk karuttu ēḻulakam eṉṉum karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. ēḻulakak karuttum eḻutīvuk karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum.
ēḻuṭiyāṉ poḻil  (tirukkōvi, 7)
taccuk kaliyil kōyiltērpōl, kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil kōyiṟkōpuram paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ aṟiviyum āṟṟaliyum ciṟappak kāṭṭum. tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyiṟ kōpurat tiṟku, 10 kal toliviluḷḷa cārappaḷḷam eṉṉum iṭatti liruntu cāram kaṭṭiyatākavum, kaṭikālil oḻukiya nīrttuḷi kaḷi aṭittaṟkuk kuṟavi mīṉkaḷip piṭittu viṭṭa tākavum, kūṟuvar.
taṇpuṉal paranta pūcal maṇmaṟuttu
mīṉiṟ ceṟukkum yāṇarp
payaṉtikaḻ vippiṟpiṟar akaṉṟali nāṭē
eṉpatu (puṟam : 7) tañci nikaḻcciyi oruvāṟu niṉivuṟuttum.
maṇi = 1. oḷikkal, 2. nīla oḷikkal.
ivviru poruḷkaḷuḷ, mutalāvatu maṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉiyiṉiṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu maḷkutal eṉṉum viṉiyi ṉiṉṟum, tōṉṟiyaviyākum. aḻaku eṉpatu ivviraṇṭi ṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya vaḻipporuḷ.
maṇṇutal kaḻuvutal. maṇṇappaṭṭatu maṇṇi. maṇṇi - maṇi.
maṇṇi yaṟipa maṇinalam  (nāṉmaṇi. 5).
maṇṇuṟu maṇiyum  (peruṅ. 2-5  : 123)
maṇṭapaṅkaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.
māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mukappil puliyuruvam amikkappaṭṭum, pulittoṭar eṉṉum caṅkili toṅkaviṭappaṭṭum, iruntaṉa.
pulimuka māṭa malira vēṟi  (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa, 9, 69)
pulittoṭar viṭṭa puṉimāṇ nallil  (mullip. 62)
māṭa maturi (puṟam. 32), māṭamali maṟukiṟ kūṭal (muruku. 71), māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭal (maturi. 429), nāṉ māṭak kūṭal makaḷirum mintarum (kalit. 92), matimali purici māṭak kūṭal (tirumukap paracuram).
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīrik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ celuttu taṟku, karantupaṭi eṉṉum puticālakam iruntatu. atu teru naṭuviṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu yāṉik kūṭṭam mēṟcellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭappaṭṭiruntatu. atiṟceṉṟa nīr yāṉit tutikki pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
māṭaṅkaḷ maturiyil mikuntum ciṟantum iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
peruṅki yāṉi yiṉaniri peyarum
curuṅki vīti  (cilap. 14 : 64 -5).
kōnakarkaḷil, ūric cuṟṟik kōṭṭi matil iruntatu. atu purici eṉappaṭṭatu. purital vaḷital. puriciyuḷḷa nakarppeyarkaḷē mutalil puri eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaṉa. kōṭṭi eṉpatum vaḷitaṟ poruḷatē. kōṭutal vaḷital.
uyarvakalam tiṇmi arumiin nāṉkiṉ
amivaraṇ eṉṟurikkum nūl.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkēṟpa, (743) matilaraṇ matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa nālvakippaṭṭiruntatu.
mika uyaramāṉatu matil; ñāyil eṉṉum ēvaṟikaḷiyuṭiyatu eyil; cempi yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyatu iñci; ariya poṟikaḷi yuṭiyatu cō.
vāṉuṭkum vaṭinīḷa matil  (puṟam. 18)
ey il (ēvaṟikaḷ) uḷḷatu eyil.
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici
 uvarā vīkit tuvari  (puṟam. 201)
cempiṭṭuc ceyta iñcit tirunakarc celvam tēṟi  (kamparā. 	
kumpa. 160).
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅki kaṭṭaḻitta  (cilap. 17 : 35).
cō eṉpatu araṇvakiyē yaṉṟic cōṇitapuram eṉṉum nakarp peyaraṉṟu.
pāmpuri, kottaḷam (bastion), vāyiṟ kōpuram pataṇam (rampart), ñāyil mutaliya pala vuṟuppukkaḷi yuṭiyatu kōṭṭi matil. cila nakarkaḷil ēḻeyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. puṟamatilic cuṟṟi akaḻi iruntatu.
kōnakarkaḷip pakiyaracar muṟṟukiyiṭṭa kālattil maṟintōṭit tappik koḷḷa, neṭuntoliviṟkuc curaṅkam allatu curuṅkiyeṉṉum kīḻ nilavaḻikaḷum iruntaṉa.
kōṭṭi - kōṭṭa (va.) L. castrum E. caster, chester (suffixes of place names)
òu« - òu (t)., OE. burg, burh OS. burg, OHG burug ON. burg, Goth. burgs, E. borough, Sc. burgh. òÇ - òß (t.), E. bury (sfx. of place - name. pāḻi = nakar Gk. polis.
(9) poṟiviṉi (Machinery)
vēntaṉ taṉ urimic cuṟṟattōṭu iṉpamāy nīrāṭuvataṟku, vēṇṭumpōtu nīri nirappavum vaṭikkavum nīrppoṟiyaminta kuḷam iruntatu. atu ilavantiki yeṉappaṭṭatu.
ilavan tikiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōki  (cilap. 10 : 31)
niṟikkuṟiṉ niṟittup pōkkuṟiṉ pōkkum
poṟippaṭi yaminta poṅkila vantiki  (peruṅ. 1 : 40; 311-2)
muḻumutalaraṇam (puṟat. 10) eṉṟum, varupaki pēṇār āreyil (puṟat. 12) eṉṟum, tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatu, miḷi yum (kāvaṟkāṭum) akaḻiyum cūḻntu palvēṟu poṟikaḷik koṇṭa cōvaraṇiyē.
(10) poṉnūl
cāta rūpam kiḷicciṟi āṭakam
cāmpū natameṉa ōṅkiya koḷki
eṉpatu (cilap. 14 : 201-2), poṉṉiṉ vakikaḷik kāṭṭum.
jāta rūppa eṉṉum vaṭacol polinta vaṭivam eṉṟum, hāṭṭaka eṉṉum vaṭacol oḷirvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟip poṉ eṉṟum taṅkam eṉṟum collalām. jampūnata eṉṉum vaṭacol, poṉ (mēru) malikku vaṭakkil nāvaṟ paḻaccāṟu perukiyōṭuvatāyk karutappaṭṭa āṟṟiṉ peyar. itu āriyat tolkatik koḷki. itaṉpaṭi, cāmpū natatti nāvalāṟi eṉṟu collal vēṇṭum.
poṉ eṉpatu, poṉ pōṉṟa piṟa kaṉiyapporuḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu, īyam mutaliya piṟa kaṉiyaṅkaḷum paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terintiruntaṉa. īyam eṉpatu iḷakuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
īyam - sīsa (va.) iḷ - (iy) - ī - īyam.
o. nō : eḷ - ey. eyttal iḷittal.
veṇkalam tamiḻar, mutaṉ mutal amitta kalavik kaṉiyamākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ oru coṟpeyar uṟi, muṟi eṉpaṉa.
(11) itaḷ māṟṟiyam
cittar itaḷiṉāl (pātaracattāl) tāḻnta kaṉiyaṅkaḷi (ulōkaṅkaḷi) veḷḷiyākavum ciṟappākap poṉṉākavum māṟṟiṉatāka, maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. ap poṉṉākkam vaṭamoḻiyil (i)racavātam eṉappaṭum. akkaliyik kuṟikkum alchemy eṉṉum colliliruntu chemistry eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, kemiya nūli rasāyaṉam eṉṟaṉar. immuṟiyil atit tamiḻil itaḷiyam eṉalām.
poṉṉākkam ekipatu (Egypt) nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatāl, akkali arapiyil alkimiya (al-kimia) eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, al eṉpatu anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cuṭṭuc col leṉṟum, kimiya eṉpatu ekipatu nāṭṭiṉ peyareṉṟum, erutantuṟi āṅkilac curukka akaramutali kūṟum. (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English).
aḷakēcaṉ nikarāka ampoṉ mika vitta pērum nēcittu racavāta vittik kalintiṭuvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar pāṭuvatāl (paripūra. 10), itaḷ māṟṟiyak kali tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
potuvāka, itu cittar kaliyeṉappaṭum. irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ citta niliyaṭintiruntatiṉāl ikkaliyi aṟintiruntaṉar.
(12) maṟa nūl
taṉimakkaḷ pōr, paṭimakkaḷ pōr eṉap pōr iru tiṟappaṭum. cilampam, maṟpōr, kuttuc caṇṭi, vāṭpōr mutaliyaṉa taṉi makkaḷ pōrām.
mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūrmallaṉip porutu koṉṟa kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi, oru pōravi naṭatti vantāṉ; ataṉāl, pōravik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ naṭatti vantatu maṟpayiṟcik kaḷari. maṟpōril, cila uyirnāṭiyāṉa narampukaḷit toṭṭu, etiriyi vīḻttik kollavum, vīḻntavaṉi mūṉṟē mukkāl nāḻikikkuḷ eḻacceyyavum, cila marumap piṭikaḷ uḷa.
maṟkaḷari, viṟkaḷari, vāṭkaḷari eṉap pōravi allatu muraṇkaḷari palavakip paṭum.
iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi kaṉmār,
ikaliṉar eṟinta akalili murukkiṉ
perumarak kampam  (puṟam. 169 : 9 -11)
eṉpatiliruntu, eypaṭi eṟipaṭi mutaliya paṭikkalap payiṟci naṭipeṟṟa vakiyi aṟiyalām.
kutirip paṭip payiṟci naṭipeṟṟa kaḷam ceṇṭu veḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
kari pari tēr kāl ākiya nālvakip paṭippōriyum paṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
(13) ōka nūl
ōvutal = ottal, oṉṟutal. ōvu - ōku. ōvu - ōvam - ōkam = aṟivaṉ uḷattil iṟivaṉōṭu oṉṟum ūḻkam (tiyāṉam).
ukam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil yuka eṉṟum utti eṉpatu yukti eṉṟum āyatupōl, ōkam eṉpatum ammoḻiyil yōka eṉṟākum. immuṟi paṟṟi ōku eṉpatu yōku eṉappaṭum.
ōkap payiṟci eṇṇuṟuppukkaḷi yuṭiyatu. avi oḻukkam (iyamam), oḻuṅku (niyamam), irukki (ācaṉam), vaḷinili (pirāṇāyāmam), pulaṉaṭakkam (pirāttiyākāram), niṟi (tāraṇi), ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), oṭukkam (camāti) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ irukkiyum vaḷiniliyum uṭaṟpayiṟci; ēṉiya uḷap payiṟci.
piṟappāl tammi uyarvākak karutum pētimiyum cerukkumuḷḷōr, ōkappayiṟci ceyvatu muyaṟkompām. iṭikali, piṉkali, cuḻimuṉi eṉpaṉa vaḷinilit toṭarpāṉa nāṭikaḷik kuṟikkum teṉcoṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟi iṭākalā, piṅkalā, suṣumuṉā eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
ōka nūliṟ kūṟappaṭum aṟu nilikkaḷaṅkaḷ allatu narappup piṉṉalkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivari, muṟiyē, aṇṭi kuṟiyiṭi nālitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, aṇṭi koppūḻiṭi āṟitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, koppūḻ maṇṭalattil pattitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, neñcāṅkuli maṇṭalattil paṉṉīritaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, aṭinā maṇṭalattil patiṉāṟitaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, iru puruvattiṭi īritaḻt tāmari vaṭivilum, iruppatākac collap peṟum.
ōka nūl iṟantupaṭṭatāl aṟunilikkaḷa (ṣaṭātāra)p peyar kaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. irukkikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻi yiṟ palavāṟu tirikkappaṭṭum moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭum uḷa. āsaṉam eṉṉum collē teṉ colliṉ tiripu.
(14) māyam (Conjury)
itu mālam allatu kaṇ kaṭṭu.
(15) vaciyam (Enchantment)
itu makaḷiriyum piṟariyum maruntālum mantirattālum maṉap payiṟciyālum vayappaṭuttal.
vayiṉ - vayam - vacam - vaci - vaciyam.
(16) mantirak kaṭṭu
itu tī kāṟṟu mutaliya iyaṟkip pūtaṅkaḷiyum, puli nāy pāmpu mutaliya uyirikaḷiyum māntariyum, iyaṅkātavāṟum tīṅku ceyyātavāṟum, mantirattāl taṭuttal.
karaṭi vempuli vāyiyum kaṭṭalām  (tāyu. tēcō. 8)
(17) makiṭi (mōṭi)
itu mantirattāṟ poruḷkaḷi maṟittalum avaṟṟi eṭuttalumākum.
(18) pēyōṭṭal (Exorcism)
palvakip pēykaḷiyum kōṭaṅki allatu uṭukkaṭittu, pēy  kōṭpaṭṭāriṉiṉṟu ōṭṭutal pēyōṭṭal ākum.
(19) kuṟaḷi
itu kuṭṭip pēyāl ciṟu kuṟumpukaḷ ceyvittal.
(20) ceyviṉi (Sorcery or Witchcraft) 
itu pēyi āḷum mantirakkāraṉik koṇṭu, vēṇṭāta varkku nōyum cākkāṭum varuvittal.
itu cūṉiyam eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkum. cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam - cūṉya (va.).
(21) karavaṭa nūl
itu kaḷavu nūl.
mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam
tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuviyeṉ
ṟeṭṭuṭa ṉaṉṟē iḻukkuṭi marapil
kaṭṭuṇ mākkaḷ tuṇiyeṉat tirivatu.
eṉpatu (cilap. 16 : 166 - 9), karavaṭa nūliṉ kūṟukaḷit terivikkum. nimittam tantiram eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku, puḷ viraku eṉpaṉa muṟiyē nikar teṉcoṟkaḷām.
(22) uṭal nūl
meyp poruḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟeṉum paṭṭāṅku nūl muṟiyilum, maruttuva muṟiyilum, pulāluṇavu muṟiyilum, maṟpōr muṟiyilum, uṭaṟkūṟukaḷip paṇṭit tamiḻar naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar.
(23) kāval nūl
nūlvaḻip piḻiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ tērcci
 ūrkāp pāḷar
eṉṉum maturik kāñciyaṭikaḷiyum (646-7), avaṟṟiṉ kaḷavu kāṇṭaṟkum kāttaṟkum kūṟiya nūlkaḷ vaḻip pōvār eṉṉum uriyiyum, nōkkuka.
15 aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences)
perumpālum paṭippiṉāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa aṟiviyalkaḷ.
(1) ilakkaṇam
ilakku - ilakkiyam. ilakku - ilakkaṇam. ilakku = kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ. ciṟanta vāḻkkik kuṟikkōḷāṉa aṟatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu ilakkiyam. ciṟanta moḻik kuṟikkōḷāṉa amippi eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻintati nilaṉeṉak
koḷḷum eṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (akat. 47) ilakkaṇattik kuṟiyeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟum, ilakkiyattiṟku aṇaṅkiyam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. poruḷil yāppum yāppil aṇiyum aṭaṅkum. paṇṭi yilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷil iruntatiṉāl, urinaṭik keṉac ciṟappāy ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭavilli. colliṟkē poruḷiruppatāl, colliṟkup piṉpu poruḷiyē ilak kaṇap pakutiyāka eṭuttukkoṇṭaṉar. poruṭ pakutiyiṉṟit tamiḻilakkaṇam niṟivuḷḷatākātu.
tamiḻ eḻuttu kīṟeḻuttum veṭṭeḻuttum eṉa iruvakip paṭṭatu. muṉṉatu ōliyeḻuttu; piṉṉatu paṭṭaya veḻuttu. paṭṭaya veḻuttiyē  vaṭṭeḻutteṉpar.
iṉṟu acciluḷḷa ōliyeḻuttu toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.
eṉṟu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūl (98) kūṟutal kāṇka. āṇṭu eṉṟatu muṟkālatti. ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟṟatē muṟkāla eḻuttiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉpatu naṉṉūlār karuttu. avar kālattil ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟavilli; peṟṟirukkumāyiṉ ati ēṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum? ātalāl, atu uriyaṉmi aṟika. `akkālam eṉpatu iṉṟum muṟkālattik kuṟittal kāṇka.
eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil kīṟiyeḻutuvataṟku vaḷi kōṭ ṭeḻuttum, uḷikoṇṭu paṭṭayattil kuḻitteḻutuvataṟku nērkōṭṭeḻuttumē, ēṟṟatātal kāṇka.
intiya āriyarkku mutalil eḻuttilli. avar maṟi eḻutāk kiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu. vētakkālap pirāmaṇar tamiḻnāṭu vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil nūleḻutat tamiḻeḻutti yoṭṭik kiranta veḻutti amittuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ, ki.pi. 10ām allatu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu.
aṟiyappaṭṭa camaṟkiruta mutaṟ paḻaṅkalveṭṭu, kattiyavāril cuṉākar eṉṉumiṭattil oru pāṟi mēl uḷatu. atu uruttiratāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉa vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālam ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. atu nākariyil illi; paḻiya kal veṭṭeḻuttil uḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuriya pavar kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ, nākariyi nōkkiya mikunta muṉṉēṟṟattik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; atē camiyattil tantiturukkaṉiṉ ki.pi. 750ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu, iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyip peritum otta kuṟikaḷiṉ muḻut tokutiyiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmiyāṉa iṟṟi nākariyiluḷḷa mutaṟ kalveṭṭu, ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muntiyataṉṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (khÅa® cšÈa«R rk‰»Uj - M§»y mfuKjÈ - K‹Diu, g¡., XXVIII, aṭikkuṟippu).
tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttip piṉpaṟṟiyatē eṉpatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyilumilli.
tamiḻar iṉṉicik kaliyilum nāṭakakkaliyilum ciṟantirunta tiṉāl, moḻiyoṭu avviru kalikaḷiyum cērttu, tamiḻi iyalici nāṭakameṉa muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṉar, ittakiya moḻiyamippum vēṟeṅkaṇumilli.
tamiḻar iyalumiṭamellām tam viṉikaḷi iciyoṭu ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu, tālāṭṭup pāṭṭu, ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭu, naṭavip pāṭṭu, mukavip pāṭṭu, ēṟṟap pāṭṭu, ēlap pāṭṭu, vaḷḷip pāṭṭu, kaḻiyaṟ pāṭṭu, kummip pāṭṭu, kōlāṭṭappāṭṭu, ūñcaṟ pāṭṭu, vaḻinaṭic cintu, oppu (oppāri)p pāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷilum irunta ilakkaṇamellām muttami ḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷē. iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇam piṇṭam eṉṟum, muttamiḻilakkaṇam māpiṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum.
poruḷilakkaṇam poruḷkaḷi akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kātaliṉpatti akam eṉac ciṟappittu, maṟṟellāvaṟṟiyum puṟattuḷ aṭakkum. puṟattuḷ pōrttuṟi mikac ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, maṟṟaviyel lām eḻupuṟap poruḷ tiṇikaḷuḷ āṟāvatāṉa vākit tiṇiyuḷ aṭakkappeṟum. 
(2) moḻi nūl
moḻi nūṟku vittūṉṟiyavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻc coṟkaḷi, ilakkaṇa vakic col mūṉṟum poruḷ vakic col oṉṟum coṟpiṟappiyal vakic col mūṉṟumāka vakuttiruntaṉar.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.  (tol. peyar.1)
colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉieṉṟu
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē.  (tol peyar. 4)
iṭiccol kiḷaviyum uriccol kiḷaviyum
avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkil tōṉṟum eṉpa.  (tol peyar. 5)
iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol eṉa mūṉṟiyalum ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi.  (tol. ecca. 4)
annāṟ collum toṭukkuṅ kāli
valikkumvaḻi valittalum melikkumvaḻi melittalum
virikkumvaḻi virittalum tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum
nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum kuṟukkumvaḻik kuṟukkalum
nāṭṭal vaḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.  (tol. ecca. 7)
innūṟpākkaḷum, mutaṉili, iṭinili, īṟu, urupu, puṇarcci, cāriyi mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷum, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ moḻi nūlaṟivik kāṭṭum.
iyaṟcol eṉpatu vērc collum aṭiccollumāṉa iyalpāṉa col (primitive word). tiricol eṉpatu atiṉiṉṟu tirinta col (derivative word). ivi yiraṇṭum centamiḻc col. ticic col eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc col. ak kālattil vaṭacollum piṟa ayaṟcollum tamiḻiṟ kalakka villi.
(3) aṟanūl
itu poruḷkaḷi aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttu, nalloḻukkattik kūṟum.
(4) poruḷ nūl
itu ellārum tattam toḻilāṟ poruḷīṭṭuvataṟku, iṉṟiyamiyāta pātukāppuc ceyyum araciyalip paṟṟik kūṟum.
(5) iṉpa nūl
itu āṭavar peṇṭir kāmaviṉpattic ciṟappittuk kūṟum. iṉpa vāḻkkiyik kūṟuvatu akapporuḷ nūl eṉṟum iṉpat tuyppi maṭṭum kūṟuvatu kāmanūl allatu vēṇūl eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
(6) maṟi nūl (Theology)
itu perumpālum camayak kuṭumikaḷi allatu koṇmuṭipu kaḷik kūṟum. itu mantiram eṉavum vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. maṉṉum tiram (tiṟam) mantiram. maṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal. itu uṇmiyākum eṉṟu tiṇmiyāy eṇṇic colvatu mantiram.
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē  (tol.cey. 78)
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa.  (tol.cey.176)
muṉṉutal karututal. muṉṉam = maṉam. muṉ - maṉ - maṉam. iṉi, muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam eṉṟumām.
mantiram camayak koḷki paṟṟiyatum cāvippu vāḻttup paṟṟiyatum eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
camayak koḷki paṟṟiyatum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu, koṇmuṭipu nūl eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. nammāḻvār aruḷic ceyta tiruvāymoḻi kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu; tirumūlar aruḷic ceyta tirumantiram koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) nūl.
niṟimoḻi māntar perumi nilattu
maṟimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum.
eṉṟatu (kuṟaḷ. 28) ittakiya nūlkaḷi nōkkiyē.
cāvippu vāḻttu moḻikaḷi mantiram eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku; nūl vaḻakkaṉṟu.
(7) paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy)
paṭṭāṅku uṇmi. paṭṭāṅku nūl meypporuḷ nūl. māntaṉ uṭalamippup paṟṟit tamiḻar kaṇṭa meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ), 96. avi ātaṉ (āṉma) meyp poruḷ 24, nāṭi 10, nili (avatti) 5, malam 3, kuṇam 3, maṇṭalam 3, piṇi 3, tiripu (vikāram) 8, nilikkaḷam (ātāram) 6, tātu 7, ūti (vāyu) 10, uṟi (kōcam) 5, vāyil 9, eṉpaṉa.
ātaṉ meyp poruḷ 24 āvaṉa : - pūtam 5, pulaṉ 5, aṟivup pulaṉ 5, karumappulaṉ 5, karaṇam 4.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum aimpūtamāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; āṇṭavaṉ (pati), ātaṉ (pacu), ācu (pācam) eṉa mūṉṟāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; uyir, mey (uṭampu) eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; tamiḻariṉ vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkikaḷām.
ātaṉukku iṟivaṉōṭuḷḷa toṭarpumuṟi irumi (tuvitam), oṉṟiya irumi (viciṣṭāttuvitam) eṉa iruvakiyākavē, paṇṭit tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. orumi (attuvitam) tamiḻar koḷkiyaṉṟu.
tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl muṉivaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟatiṉāl, uyir, mey, uyirmey, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam, puṇarcci, mutaliya eḻuttilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; peyar, viṉimutal, eṇ vēṟṟumi, viṉi, viṉimuṟṟu, viṉiyeccam, iṟantakāla viṉi, nikaḻkāla viṉi, etirkāla viṉi mutaliya collilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; meypporuḷ nūṟkaruttum taḻuvumāṟu amikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
(8) aḷavi nūl
itu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟitaṟku ētāṉa (ētuvāṉa) aḷavikaḷi (piramāṇaṅkaḷi) eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu. aḷavikaḷ mottam pattu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉavi kāṭci karuttu oppu uri eṉṉum nāṉku. karutteṉpatu uyttuṇarvu.
(9) ēraṇam (Logic)
itu aḷavikaḷik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟiyum vakikaḷi eṭuttut kūṟuvatu.
vaṭivēl ceṭṭiyār patippitta tarkka paripāṣi eṉṉum nūliṟutiyil, akattiyar pērāl 20 tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu.
poruḷkuṇam karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
iṉmi yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu. itu poruḷkaḷi ēḻākap pakuppatu. itiyē piṟkālattil vaṭavar vicēṭikam eṉṟaṉar.
ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratam cālam  (taṉippāṭal). 
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar   (tirukkōvic ciṟappuppāyiram)
(9) vāṉa nūl (Astronomy)
vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ (naṭcattiram), kōḷ (kirakam), ōri (irāci) mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkik kūṟuvatu vāṉa nūl. irupatteḻu nāṭkaḷum eḻu kōḷkaḷum paṉṉīrōrikaḷum kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar.
eḻu kōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl eḻu nāṭ kiḻamiyi mutaṉ mutal  ēṟpaṭuttiyavar tamiḻarē. atu piṉṉar ulaka muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷatu.
koḷ - kōḷ. koḷḷutal vaḷital. koḷ - koṭku. koṭkutal = cuḻalutal, cuṟṟi varutal. koṭpatu kōḷ. kōḷkaḷ vāṉattiṟ cuḻaṉṟu cuṟṟivarum tōṟṟattāl ap peyar peṟṟaṉa. irāku kētu eṉpaṉa cāyikaḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. niṟam paṟṟi avi aṇivakiyil karumpāmpu cempāmpu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iruttal karuttal. ir - irā - irāku. cē - cētu = civappu. cētāmpal cevvāmpal. cētu - kētu. o. nō: cempu - kempu.

	kāri	(caṉi)	
vaṭamoḻik kiḻamip peyarkaḷuḷ, maṅkaḷa vāram, caṉivāram ākiya iraṇṭu tavira maṟṟaviyellām nēr moḻi peyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
āṅkilap peyarkaḷuḷ, piṉ aintum piṟkālattil māṟiviṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, eḻunāḷ eṉṉum kāla aḷavu māṟātiruttal kāṇka.
cevvāy cenniṟamuḷḷateṉṟum, viyāḻaṉ poṉṉiṟamuḷḷa teṉṟum periyateṉṟum, veḷḷi veṇmiyāṉa teṉṟum, kāri kariyateṉṟum, kaṇṭaṟintatu viyakkat takka ceytiyām.
viyal = 1. perumi. mūḻttiṟutta viyaṉṟāṉi (patiṟ. 33, 5). 
2. akalam viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē (tol.col. 354). 3. mikuti. (cilap. 3, 7, uri).
viyal - viyalaṉ - viyāḻaṉ.
viḷḷutal = virital, malartal. viḷ - viy - viyal.
pirukapati eṉṉum vaṭacol viyāḻaṉ eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppā yiruppatōṭu, peruku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripi nilimoḻi mutaṉiliyākak koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
paṉṉiru ōrikaḷ
	tamiḻ	vaṭamoḻi	ilattīṉ
	mēḻam	mēṣam	Aries
	viṭi	riṣapam	Taurus
	iraṭṭi	mituṉam	Gemini
	alavaṉ	karkkaṭakam	Cancer
	āḷi	cimmam	Leo
	kaṉṉi	kaṉṉi	Virgo
	tuli	tulā	Libra
	naḷi	viruccikam	Scorpio
	cili	cāpam (taṉucu)	Sagitarius
	cuṟavam	makaram	(Capricornus)
	kumpam	kumpam	Aquarius
	mīṉam	mīṉam	Pisces
vaṭamoḻiyil ellāp peyarkaḷum, ilattīṉil patiṉoru peyarkaḷum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ nērmoḻipeyarppā yiruttal kāṇka.
muḻuttal, tiraḷutal. muḻutta āṇ piḷḷi eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. muḻutu, mottam. muḻu - muḻā = tiraṇṭa muracu. muḻā - muḻavu - muḻavam. muḻā - miḻā = tiraṇṭa māṉ (Stag). miḻā - mēḻam = tiraṇṭa cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kaṭā. mēḻam - mēṣa (va.) mēḻam - mēḻakam - ēḻakam - ēṭakam - ēṭu - yāṭu - āṭu.
tullutal = poruntutal, ottal. tul - tuṉ, tuṉṉutal poruntutal. tul - tuli = oppu. iru puṟamum otta niṟikōl. tul - tulā = tuli pōṉṟa ēṟṟam. tulā - tulām - tulāṉ = oru niṟi. o. nō :  oppu = tuli. opparāvi = tuli ceyvōṉ. kummutal, kuvital. kum - kumpu - kumpam - kumpa (va.). miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam - mīṉa (va.).
paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil paṉṉī rōrip peyarkaḷē paṉṉiru mātap peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
mati - mātam - māsa (va.). ovvoru piṟi niliyum pakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. vaḷarpiṟi veṇpakkam eṉṟum, tēypiṟi karumpakkam eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
paku - pakkam. paku - bhaj (va.). pakkam - pakṣa (va.).
irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇi, uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu. iviyallāta potu uṭukkaḷ veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa. pukikkōḷ (comet) vālveḷḷi yeṉap paṭṭatu.
oru pakalum ōr iravum cērntatu oru nāḷ eṉṟum, ēḻu nāḷ koṇṭatu oru kiḻamiyeṉṟum, oru vaḷarpiṟiyum oru tēypiṟiyum cērntatu oru mātam eṉṟum, katiravaṉ paṉṉīrōrikkuḷḷum pukuvatu paṉṉiru mātam eṉṟum, ataṉ oru vaṭacelavum oru teṉ celavum cērntu ōr āṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭiruntatu. oru nāḷi āṟu ciṟupoḻutākap pirittatu pōṉṟu, ōr āṇṭi āṟu perum poḻutākap pirittiruntaṉar.
oru kōnakart tōṟṟam allatu oru pēraracaṉ piṟappup pōṉṟa nikaḻcciyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, toṭarāṇṭu kaṇittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ceññā yiṟṟuc celavum añ ñāyiṟṟup
parippum parippuc cūḻntamaṇ ṭilamum
vaḷitiritaru ticiyum
vaṟitu niliiya kāyamum eṉṟivi
ceṉṟaḷan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
iṉitteṉ pōrum uḷarē
eṉṉum (puṟam. 30) uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār kūṟṟum,  mākavicumpu (puṟam. 35, akam. 253, maturikkā. 454, pari. 1) eṉṉum vaḻakkum, mākamāvatu pūmikkum cuvarkkattukkum naṭuvu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar uriyum,
iṉṉici yeḻiliyi irappavum iyivatō
vaḷitarum celvaṉi vāḻttavum iyivatō?
eṉṉum pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkōviṉ pāṭṭaṭikaḷum (kalit. 15), kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
maḻikkum kāṟṟiṟkum katiravaṉ karaṇiyam eṉpati muṉṉit tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar. eḻili mukil. eḻiliyit tōṟṟuvikkum katiravaṉi eḻili eṉṟatu ilakkaṇiyeṉṉum ākupeyarp pōli.
(11) kaṇiyam (Astrology)
oruvar piṟanta nāḷiyum vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ kōḷ niliyiyum aṭippaṭiyāka vittu, avarukku varum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi muṟpaṭak kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu kaṇiyam. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvataṟkum variyappaṭum kaṇiyamē piṟappiyam (jātakam).
kōccēramāṉ yāṉikkaṭ cēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟi iṉṉanāḷ iṟappāṉ eṉṟu, kūṭalūrkiḻār oru viṇvīḻ koḷḷiyik kaṇṭu kaṇittaṟinta ceytiyi 229ām puṟappāṭṭuk kūṟum. nalla nāḷum vēḷiyum pārttu viṉikaḷit toṭaṅkuvatu, paṇṭināḷiṟ peruvaḻakkamā yiruntatu. nāḷēraṭittal, kuṭināṭkōḷ, vāḷnāṭkōḷ eṉpaṉa itit terivikkum.
toṉṟutoṭṭuk kaṇiyat toḻil ceytu varum tamiḻ vakuppāṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ. vaḷ kūrmi. vaḷḷuvaṉ kūrmatiyaṉ. āriyak kulap pirivāl, avaṉ tāḻttappaṭṭup pēriṭaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum piḻip piḻantāṉ.
kaṇṇutal = akakkaṇṇāṟ pārttal, karututal, matittal. kaṇṇiyam = matippu. kaṇ - kaṇi. kaṇittal = matittal, kaṇakkiṭutal, aḷaviṭutal. kaṇi - kaṇitam - kaṇicam = matippu (approximation). kaṇi - kaṇiki = tāḷam kaṇittāṭupavaḷ. kaṇi - kaṇikaṉ, kaṇiyaṉ = nāḷkōḷ nilikaṇṭu varuṅkāla naṉmi tīmi kaṇippavaṉ. kaṇ, kaṇi, kaṇitam, kaṇiki eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil mutaleḻut teṭuppōci yuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇitam eṉpatu kaṇita eṉṟum, kaṇiki eṉpatu kaṇikā eṉṟum, īṟu kuṉṟiyum tirintum vaḻaṅkum.
(12) kaṇakku
kaḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal, kūṭutal, kaḷḷa = okka (uvama urupu), kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭumiṭam. kaḷ - kaḷa - kaṇa. kaṇattal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal. kaṇakka = pōla. kuraṅku kaṇakka ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. kaṇa - kaṇam = kūṭṭam. kaṇavaṉ kūṭukiṉṟavaṉ. kaṇa - kaṇakku = kūṭiya toki, aḷavu.
kaṇakku eṉpatu mutalil kūṭṭal kaṇakki maṭṭum kuṟittu, piṉpu nālvakik kaṇakkiṟkum potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērntavar eṉpatu, avar kiyāṇṭa nuṇṇiya aḷavikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.
